+    val v = "SCHOLARCHAIN_LLM_REQUESTS_PER_MINUTE"
+    assert(load().runtime.llm.requestsPerMinute == 8) // reference default unchanged
+    assert(load(v -> "4").runtime.llm.requestsPerMinute == 4)
+    val f = file("scholarchain.runtime.llm.requests-per-minute = 2")
+    val overFile = load(ConfigLoader.ConfigFileEnv -> f.toString, v -> "4")
+    assert(overFile.runtime.llm.requestsPerMinute == 4) // the variable beats the file
+    assert(ConfigLoader.load(devEnv).map(_.runtime.llm.requestsPerMinute) == Right(4))
+    assert(load(v -> "1").runtime.llm.requestsPerMinute == 1)
+    for (x, msg) <- Seq(
+        "0" -> "rates, and timeouts must be positive",
+        "-1" -> "rates, and timeouts must be positive",
+        "9" -> "requests-per-minute must be at most 8",
+        "4.5" -> "whole number",
+        "" -> "runtime.llm.requests-per-minute",
+        "  " -> "runtime.llm.requests-per-minute"
+      )
+    do assert(errorsOf(devEnv + (v -> x)).contains(msg), s"'$x'")
+    assert(ConfigLoader.EnvOverrides(v) == "runtime.llm.requests-per-minute")
+  }
+
   private def errorsOf(env: Map[String, String]): String =
     ConfigLoader.load(env).fold(_.mkString("; "), c => fail(s"loaded: $c"))

 | Determinism | Tasks hold only per-task state built in `setup` (parser, run context, output writer). Reducers sort their values before choosing, so output does not depend on shuffle order. Chosen records are the smallest (works) or the most frequent, ties broken by the smallest (entities). A rerun differs only in `provenance.runId` and the J10 `decidedAt` (the run's `startedAt`), and `PipelineSpec` checks this. |
 | Provenance | Every record carries `Provenance`. Source records keep their dataset, source record ID, and dataset snapshot. Teams and pairs keep the DBLP work's source provenance. Match candidates are `DERIVED`, with `sourceRecordId = <key>|<sourceId>|<targetId>` and the DBLP snapshot; blocked candidates (J06) also carry `policyVersion = norm-v1.block-v1.feat-v1`. Graph edges carry `graph.policy-version`. Match decisions (J10) are `DERIVED`, keep the candidate's `sourceRecordId` and snapshot, and carry `policyVersion` = the configured match policy version (`er-v1` or `er-v2`), no prompt version, and no model version. |
 | Run ID | `StableIds.runId("hadoop-pipeline", --code-version, config-version, [dblp=<d>, openalex=<d>, crossref=<d>, orcid=<d>] ++ input fingerprints, attempt)`. A fingerprint is `input:<name>=<path as given>:<bytes>` for every `--dblp`, `--dblp-dtd`, `--openalex`, `--crossref`, and `--orcid` path (bytes: the file length, or the total below a directory, from `FileSystem.getContentSummary`), so changed input data gives a new run ID; a missing input path fails the run before any job. Size, not a checksum: an in-place edit that keeps the byte count keeps the run ID. The attempt is `--attempt <n>` when given (run scripts pass it; the run fails before any job if that attempt's run directory exists). Otherwise the driver uses the smallest attempt (>= 1) whose run directory does not exist, so every local rerun of the same inputs gets a new attempt. `_manifest.json` (`hadoop-run-manifest-v3`) is created with overwrite disabled, which claims the attempt; it records the attempt, inputs, input fingerprints, reducers, `graph.oversize-team-threshold`, `entity-resolution.max-block-size`, `entity-resolution.max-conflict-group-size`, the entity-resolution rule versions, the configured match policy version (`matchPolicyVersion`, `er-v1` or `er-v2`), the four thresholds, and `startedAt` (the ISO-8601 instant every J10 decision of the run carries as `decidedAt`). |

+## 5a. Starting the services on ECS (Phase 12 work order 6)
+
+Command line (`Launcher.Usage`): `--live [--profile <name>] [--region <r>] --snapshot <date> --graph-run <runId> --items-run <runId> --vectors-run <runId>`. The API key comes from the variable `services.api-key-env` names (`SCHOLARCHAIN_API_KEY`, injected by ECS from Secrets Manager).
+
+- **No `--profile` on ECS.** `--live` without `--profile` builds `LiveTarget(None, region)`, which selects the SDK default credentials chain, so the task role supplies the credentials. `--profile <name>` gives `LiveTarget(Some(name), region)` (a human-run laptop start). What the task definition must and must not set for credentials is in `modules/storage/STORAGE.md` "Credentials on ECS". The region is `--region`, else the core `application.region` (`SCHOLARCHAIN_AWS_REGION`). Tested: `LauncherSpec`, with the task-definition arguments and variable names, no client built and no AWS call.
+- **Entry point.** The image keeps the sbt-native-packager default ENTRYPOINT `/opt/docker/bin/services`, which needs `-main <class>` because the package has several main classes. ECS appends `command` to the entrypoint, so a command `bin/query-api` would stop with "You need to pass -main argument". Each task definition therefore sets `entryPoint` to `/opt/docker/bin/<service>` (`query-api`, `graph-evidence-api`, `worker`) and passes only the arguments above as `command`. This supersedes the `bin/query-api` command form of Phase 10 ruling 18 (Phase 12 ruling 14b; the staged image of Phase 12 work order 3 has the three start scripts).
+- **Window summaries.** query-api and the worker set `SCHOLARCHAIN_WINDOW_SUMMARY_RUN`; graph-evidence-api does not (ruling 14a, section 5).
+
 ## 6. Request read timeout and unread bodies (Phase 11 D5)
 
 The JDK HTTP server reads a request's line, headers, and body on an HTTP thread. When a route had not read the whole body, the server drained it (up to 64 KiB) as the exchange ended, after the response was written, and it waited for that body with no time limit. A client that declared a body and never sent it therefore held an HTTP thread and its connection indefinitely, with or without the API key (code review 1, D5). Two limits now apply:

 ### Credentials (ruling 35)
 
@@ -472,6 +472,20 @@ Since repair-01:
 - **HeadObject (N2).** S3 HEAD error responses have no body, so an expired token there carries no error code. `S3ObjectStore.sha256` therefore treats any 403 on HEAD as a possible expiry (`LiveAws.isHeadExpiredToken`): one refresh and one retry. A real denial fails again and propagates, at the cost of one extra request. Every other call keeps the strict code-or-message rule.
 - No change to the embedding output format, vector keys, metadata, or the EmbedCli run-ID inputs.
 
+### Credentials on ECS (Phase 10 ruling 18, Phase 12 work order 1)
+
+An ECS task sets no AWS profile. `LiveAws.provider(target)` selects the credentials source:
+- `profileName = Some(name)` (the human-run CLIs): `ProfileCredentialsProvider.create(name)`, unchanged.
+- `profileName = None` (ECS): `DefaultCredentialsProvider.builder().build()`, the SDK default chain. In SDK 2.41.32 (`javap` on `auth-2.41.32.jar`) its order is Java system properties, environment variables (`AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`, `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`, `AWS_SESSION_TOKEN`), web identity token, the `[default]` profile of the shared files, container credentials (ECS task role), then the EC2 instance profile. Source: https://docs.aws.amazon.com/sdk-for-java/latest/developer-guide/credentials-chain.html (accessed 2026-11-14).
+
+Either provider is wrapped in the same shared `RefreshingCredentials` (one per profile and one for the chain, keyed by `profileName`), so the expired-token refresh-and-retry above applies unchanged. Caching and refresh inside the provider stay the SDK default (container credentials are cached and refreshed by their `Expiration`). Region handling is unchanged: every client gets `Region.of(target.region)`; nothing reads `AWS_REGION`. Constructing either provider reads no file and makes no call (both are lazy, checked with `javap`); `LiveRequestSpec` "no profile (ECS) selects the SDK default chain ..." tests the selection with no network and no AWS.
+
+What the ECS task definition must and must not set for credentials:
+- Set `taskRoleArn` to the service's task role. The ECS agent then injects `AWS_CONTAINER_CREDENTIALS_RELATIVE_URI` into the container itself; the task definition does not set it. Task role credentials are valid for six hours and rotated by the agent. Source: https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonECS/latest/developerguide/security-iam-roles.html (accessed 2026-11-14).
+- Set no `AWS_PROFILE`, `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`, `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`, `AWS_SESSION_TOKEN`, `AWS_WEB_IDENTITY_TOKEN_FILE`, or `AWS_ROLE_ARN`, and no `aws.accessKeyId` or `aws.secretAccessKey` system property. Each comes before container credentials in the chain and would replace the task role.
+- The region comes from the service's `--region` or the core `application.region` (`SCHOLARCHAIN_AWS_REGION`, default us-east-2), not from `AWS_REGION`.
+- The service launcher starts live mode without `--profile` and passes `LiveTarget(None, region)`: `Launcher.liveTarget` in `modules/services` gives `LiveTarget(a.get("profile"), region)`, so `--profile` is optional (Phase 12 work order 6). The four live loader CLIs accept `--live` alone the same way (work order 5).
+
 | Adapter | Ports | Requests |
 |---|---|---|
 | `S3ObjectStore(client, bucket)` | ObjectReadPort, ObjectLoadPort | PutObject with `If-None-Match: *` and a SHA-256 checksum. On 412 the stored object is read and compared: same bytes `Unchanged`, else `Conflict`. A 409 is retried. GetObject; ListObjectsV2 (paginated). `sha256(key)`: HeadObject with `ChecksumMode = ENABLED`, returning the stored full-object SHA-256 as `sha256:<hex>` (None for a missing object or a composite multipart checksum). |

+  val StageFlag = "stage-from-bucket"
+
+  /** Bound on one staging run, below the 30-minute task wait of scripts/load-stores.sh; each GET is
+    * also bounded by the client's 120 s call timeout (LiveAws.overrides).
+    */
+  val StageTimeoutMs: Long = 20L * 60 * 1000
+
+  /** --stage-from-bucket (an ECS task has no local copy). Copies from the bucket into s3root,
+    * write-once: every object of the store-conversion run manifest under the load prefix and under
+    * curated/coauthor-evidence/ of the same run, then the manifest itself, last. Only manifest keys
+    * are read (no listing), so the object count and bytes are the manifest's, and they are printed
+    * first. Each object is written only if it has the manifest's size and SHA-256. Transient GET
+    * failures are retried (RetryPolicy.Default; a GET is idempotent). Empty means staged.
+    */
+  def stageFromBucket(
+      remote: ObjectReadPort,
+      s3root: Path,
+      snapshot: String,
+      sourceRun: String,
+      prefix: String,
+      out: String => Unit,
+      timeoutMs: Long = StageTimeoutMs,
+      now: () => Long = () => System.currentTimeMillis,
+      sleep: Long => Unit = Cli.jitteredSleep
+  ): Vector[String] =
+    val manifestKey = S3Layout.runManifest("store-conversion", snapshot, sourceRun)
+    val loadPrefix = S3Layout.neptuneRoot(snapshot, sourceRun) + "/"
+    val prefixes =
+      Vector(loadPrefix, S3Layout.base("curated", "coauthor-evidence", snapshot, sourceRun) + "/")
+    val local = FileObjectStore(s3root).loader
+    var retries = 0
+    def get(k: String): Option[Array[Byte]] =
+      val (b, attempts) = Retry.run(RetryPolicy.Default, sleep)(remote.get(k))
+      retries += attempts - 1
+      b
+    def write(k: String, b: Array[Byte]): Option[String] = local.putNew(k, b) match
+      case Left(ps)                            => Some(s"$k: ${ps.mkString("; ")}")
+      case Right(PutObjectOutcome.Conflict(h)) => Some(s"local $k has $h; not overwritten")
+      case Right(_)                            => None
+    def copy(entry: ManifestEntry): Option[String] =
+      val k = entry.key
+      get(k) match
+        case None => Some(s"$k is missing from the bucket")
+        case Some(b) if b.length != entry.bytes || Hashing.sha256(b) != entry.sha256 =>
+          Some(
+            s"$k is ${Hashing.sha256(b)} (${b.length} bytes), " +
+              s"manifest ${entry.sha256} (${entry.bytes} bytes)"
+          )
+        case Some(b) => write(k, b)
+    if prefix != loadPrefix then Vector(s"--dir must be <out>/s3/$loadPrefix")
+    else
+      get(manifestKey) match
+        case None     => Vector(s"no run manifest at $manifestKey in the bucket")
+        case Some(mb) =>
+          Try(RunManifest.fromJson(mb)).toEither match
+            case Left(err) => Vector(s"unreadable manifest $manifestKey: ${err.getMessage}")
+            case Right(m)
+                if m.stage != "store-conversion" || m.runId != sourceRun || m.snapshot != snapshot =>
+              Vector(s"manifest $manifestKey is ${m.stage} run ${m.runId} snapshot ${m.snapshot}")
+            case Right(m) =>
+              val entries = m.entries.filter(x => prefixes.exists(p => x.key.startsWith(p)))
+              val bytes = entries.map(_.bytes).sum
+              out(s"stage: ${entries.size} objects, $bytes bytes (from $manifestKey) into $s3root")
+              Cli.freeDiskProblem(s3root, bytes + mb.length) match
+                case Some(disk) => Vector(disk)
+                case None       =>
+                  val deadline = now() + timeoutMs
+                  val done =
+                    entries.iterator.takeWhile(_ => now() < deadline).map(copy).toVector
+                  val problems = done.flatten ++ Option.when(done.size < entries.size)(
+                    s"timeout after ${done.size} of ${entries.size} objects (${timeoutMs / 1000} s)"
+                  )
+                  val all = if problems.isEmpty then write(manifestKey, mb).toVector else problems
+                  out(
+                    s"stage: ${done.size} objects checked, retries $retries, problems ${all.size}"
+                  )
+                  all
+
   /** M3: the local copy and the live bucket must hold exactly the manifest's objects under prefix,
     * each with the manifest's SHA-256 (local: hashed here; live: the checksum S3 stored at upload,
     * read with HEAD). Empty means the bulk load may start.

+  private val stagePrefix = S3Layout.neptuneRoot(snapshot, runId) + "/"
+  private val stageManifestKey = S3Layout.runManifest("store-conversion", snapshot, runId)
+  private val stageObjects = Vector(
+    stagePrefix + "vertices/researcher/part-00000.csv" -> "id,label\n",
+    stagePrefix + "edges/authored/part-00000.csv" -> "from,to\n",
+    S3Layout.coauthorEvidence(snapshot, runId, "all-years", "cae-1") -> "{}",
+    S3Layout.dynamoItems(snapshot, runId, "ResearcherMetricCache") + "/part-00000.jsonl" -> "{}\n"
+  )
+
+  /** A fake bucket holding stageObjects (except the omitted keys, and with overrides of their
+    * bytes) and the store-conversion manifest of all of them.
+    */
+  private def stageBucket(
+      root: Path,
+      omit: Set[String] = Set.empty,
+      tamper: Map[String, String] = Map.empty
+  ): (ObjectReadPort, RunManifest) =
+    val bucket = FileObjectStore(root.resolve("bucket"))
+    val m = RunManifest(
+      RunManifest.SchemaVersion,
+      "store-conversion",
+      runId,
+      snapshot,
+      stageObjects.map((k, v) => ManifestEntry(k, Hashing.sha256(v), v.length.toLong, "src"))
+    )
+    stageObjects.foreach((k, v) =>
+      if !omit(k) then bucket.loader.putNew(k, tamper.getOrElse(k, v).getBytes(UTF_8))
+    )
+    bucket.loader.putNew(stageManifestKey, RunManifest.toJson(m))
+    (bucket.reader, m)
+
+  private def stage(
+      remote: ObjectReadPort,
+      s3root: Path,
+      prefix: String = stagePrefix,
+      now: () => Long = () => 0L
+  ): (Vector[String], Vector[String]) =
+    val lines = mutable.ArrayBuffer.empty[String]
+    val problems = LoadNeptuneCli.stageFromBucket(
+      remote,
+      s3root,
+      snapshot,
+      runId,
+      prefix,
+      lines += _,
+      timeoutMs = 1000,
+      now = now,
+      sleep = _ => ()
+    )
+    (problems, lines.toVector)
+
+  test("WO5 stage-from-bucket: the staged copy matches the manifest; a rerun is unchanged") {
+    withTemp { root =>
+      val (bucket, m) = stageBucket(root)
+      val s3 = root.resolve("out/s3")
+      val (problems, out) = stage(bucket, s3)
+      assert(problems.isEmpty, out)
+      assert(out.head == s"stage: 3 objects, 19 bytes (from $stageManifestKey) into $s3", out)
+      val staged = FileObjectStore(s3).reader
+      // The graph prefix, the run's coauthor evidence, and the manifest; nothing else (no dynamodb).
+      assert(staged.list("") == (stageObjects.take(3).map(_._1) :+ stageManifestKey).sorted)
+      m.entries
+        .take(3)
+        .foreach(e => assert(staged.get(e.key).map(Hashing.sha256).contains(e.sha256)))
+      assert(staged.get(stageManifestKey).map(RunManifest.fromJson).contains(m))
+      assert(stage(bucket, s3)._1.isEmpty) // write-once: identical objects are Unchanged
+    }
+  }
+
+  test("WO5 stage-from-bucket: a sha256 mismatch is refused and not written") {
+    withTemp { root =>
+      val bad = stageObjects(1)._1
+      val (bucket, _) = stageBucket(root, tamper = Map(bad -> "FROM,TO\n")) // same size
+      val s3 = root.resolve("out/s3")
+      val (problems, out) = stage(bucket, s3)
+      assert(problems.size == 1 && problems.head.startsWith(s"$bad is sha256:"), out)
+      assert(problems.head.contains(s"manifest ${Hashing.sha256("from,to\n")}"))
+      val staged = FileObjectStore(s3).reader
+      assert(staged.get(bad).isEmpty && staged.get(stageManifestKey).isEmpty)
+      // A local copy with other bytes is never overwritten either.
+      val (good, _) = stageBucket(root.resolve("2"))
+      FileObjectStore(s3).loader.putNew(bad, "FROM,TO\n".getBytes(UTF_8))
+      assert(stage(good, s3)._1.exists(_.startsWith(s"local $bad has")))
+    }
+  }
+

===== UNTRACKED FILES (git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- infra scripts modules datasets)

===== UNTRACKED: datasets/schemas/fractions.md
# Size-scaling fractions schema note (rule `fraction-v1`)

- Purpose: the 25 and 50 percent inputs of the D-13 size-scaling runs (`scripts/run-bounded-pipeline.sh --fraction 25|50`). The 100 percent input is the `--full` subset itself.
- Tool: `datasets/scripts/make_fraction.sh --fraction 25|50` builds a fraction, and `--verify` checks every existing fraction. Local only, with no network. The tool verifies each source against its `SHA256SUMS`, stops when the projected output would leave less than 5 GiB free, and skips a fraction whose `SHA256SUMS` already verifies for the same sources.
- Record formats are unchanged. Every output record is a byte-identical copy of a source record, so the `dblp.md`, `openalex.md`, `crossref.md`, and `orcid.md` notes apply. The only exception is the ORCID file: it is decompressed and re-compressed (gzip level 6, mtime 0, no file name), so its lines are identical but the `.gz` bytes are new.

## Layout (`scratch/datasets/fraction=<p>/`)

| File | Content |
|---|---|
| `dblp/works.xml` | Original prologue (`<!DOCTYPE dblp SYSTEM "dblp.dtd">`), then the kept records in source order, then `</dblp>`. The DTD is not copied: the pipeline reads `scratch/datasets/dblp/snapshot=2026-10-03/raw/dblp.dtd`. |
| `openalex/works.jsonl`, `crossref/works.jsonl` | Kept lines, in source order |
| `orcid/records.jsonl.gz` | Kept ORCID API v3.0 records, in source order |
| `run-info.json` | `fraction`, `rule_version`, `rule`, the DTD source, and per input `source_path`, `source_sha256`, `source_bytes`, `source_records`, `kept_records`, `kept_percent`, `output`, and `output_bytes`. DBLP adds `kept_distinct_dois`, and ORCID adds `linked_ids_on_kept_records`. Also `python`, `zlib`, `gzip`, and `generated_utc`. |
| `SHA256SUMS` | sha256 of every other file. It is written last and marks a complete run. |

DBLP person records are not produced. The pipeline uploads no persons file, and `modules/hadoop/JOBS.md` states that `persons.xml` is not read.

## Rule

1. DBLP: keep a record when `int(sha256("fraction-v1|" + key).hexdigest()[:8], 16) % 100 < p`.
2. OpenAlex and Crossref: keep a line when its lower-cased DOI is on a kept DBLP record. OpenAlex DOIs have `https://doi.org/` removed. DBLP DOIs are html-unescaped and lower-cased from the `<ee>` doi.org links.
3. ORCID: keep a record when `orcid-identifier.path` appears as an author iD on a kept DBLP record (`<author orcid>`), OpenAlex record (`authorships[].author.orcid`), or Crossref record (`author[].ORCID`). This is the exact `orcid:<iD>` linkage of Hadoop J03.

Every rule is monotone in `p`, so each fraction nests in the next: 25 is within 50, and 50 is within 100.

## Observed counts (2026-11-14)

| Input | 100 | 25 | 50 |
|---|---|---|---|
| DBLP records | 110,867 | 27,993 (25.249%) | 55,598 (50.148%) |
| OpenAlex lines | 107,506 | 27,129 (25.235%) | 53,949 (50.182%) |
| Crossref lines | 106,726 | 26,964 (25.265%) | 53,607 (50.229%) |
| ORCID records | 22,975 | 14,905 (64.875%) | 18,388 (80.035%) |

## Known limitations

- ORCID does not scale with `p`, because a researcher is kept when any one of their works is kept.
- 3,301 full ORCID records carry an iD that appears on no subset record, so no fraction can keep them.
- 94 full OpenAlex works have a DOI that is on no DBLP record, so no fraction keeps them.
- The source files contain duplicate identical lines (OpenAlex: 107,506 lines, 107,439 distinct; ORCID: 22,975 lines, 22,914 distinct). The tool keeps them as they are.

===== UNTRACKED: datasets/scripts/make_fraction.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Deterministic, nested size-scaling fractions of the ScholarChain inputs (rule fraction-v1).

Python 3 standard library only. Called by make_fraction.sh; run with python3 -I.

make:   keep a DBLP record when int(sha256("fraction-v1|" + key)[:8], 16) % 100 < p. Records are copied
        byte-for-byte (same reader and writer as dblp_extract.py). OpenAlex and Crossref lines are kept when
        their DOI is on a kept DBLP record. ORCID records are kept when their iD (orcid-identifier.path)
        appears as an author iD on a kept DBLP, OpenAlex, or Crossref record, the exact-key linkage the
        Hadoop pipeline uses (J03). Every rule is monotone in p, so fraction 25 is inside 50 inside 100.
verify: parse every output (DBLP with expat and the DTD, JSONL line by line, ORCID through gzip), check the
        DBLP rule exactly against the full input, check linkage, and check record-level nesting
        (every output record is byte-identical to a record of the next larger fraction, up to the full input).
"""
import argparse
import gzip
import hashlib
import html
import importlib.util
import json
import os
import platform
import re
import sys
import zlib
from datetime import datetime, timezone

RULE_VERSION = "fraction-v1"
RULE = ('keep a DBLP record when int(first 8 hex digits of sha256("fraction-v1|" + key), 16) mod 100 < p; '
        "OpenAlex and Crossref lines whose lower-cased DOI is on a kept DBLP record; ORCID records whose "
        "orcid-identifier.path appears as an author iD on a kept DBLP, OpenAlex, or Crossref record")
ID = re.compile(r"[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{3}[0-9X]")
NAMES = {"dblp": "dblp/works.xml", "openalex": "openalex/works.jsonl", "crossref": "crossref/works.jsonl",
         "orcid": "orcid/records.jsonl.gz"}

sys.dont_write_bytecode = True  # python3 -I does not imply -B: no __pycache__ under datasets/scripts (code review 1 F2)
_spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("dblp_extract", os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "dblp_extract.py"))
dx = importlib.util.module_from_spec(_spec)
_spec.loader.exec_module(dx)


def bucket(key):
    return int(hashlib.sha256(f"{RULE_VERSION}|{key}".encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:8], 16) % 100


def dblp_records(path):
    """Yield (prologue_or_None, key, text) for every record of a works.xml."""
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        for prologue, _, text in dx.records(f):
            km = dx.KEY.search(text)
            if not km:
                raise ValueError(f"{path}: record without key: {text[:80]!r}")
            yield prologue, km.group(1), text


def dblp_dois(text):
    return {html.unescape(x).strip().lower() for x in dx.DOI_EE.findall(text)}


def oa_doi(w):
    return (w.get("doi") or "").lower().removeprefix("https://doi.org/")


def oa_ids(w):
    return {m.group(0) for a in w.get("authorships") or [] for m in [ID.search(((a or {}).get("author") or {}).get("orcid") or "")] if m}


def cr_ids(w):
    return {m.group(0) for a in w.get("author") or [] for m in [ID.search((a or {}).get("ORCID") or "")] if m}


def orcid_path(r):
    return (r.get("orcid-identifier") or {}).get("path")


def jsonl(path, opener=open):
    with opener(path, "rb") as f:
        for line in f:
            if line.strip():
                yield line, json.loads(line)


def make(p, src, out, source_meta):
    os.makedirs(os.path.join(out, "dblp"), exist_ok=True)
    counts = {k: {"source_records": 0, "kept_records": 0} for k in NAMES}
    xml = dx.XmlOut(os.path.join(out, NAMES["dblp"]))
    dois, ids = set(), set()
    for prologue, key, text in dblp_records(src["dblp"]):
        if prologue is not None:
            xml.header(prologue)
        counts["dblp"]["source_records"] += 1
        if bucket(key) < p:
            xml.f.write(text + "\n")
            counts["dblp"]["kept_records"] += 1
            dois |= dblp_dois(text)
            ids |= {m.group(1) for attrs, _ in dx.AUTHOR.findall(text) for m in [dx.ORCID_ATTR.search(attrs or "")] if m}
    xml.close()
    counts["dblp"]["kept_distinct_dois"] = len(dois)

    for name, doi_of, ids_of in (("openalex", oa_doi, oa_ids), ("crossref", lambda w: (w.get("DOI") or "").lower(), cr_ids)):
        dest = os.path.join(out, NAMES[name])
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dest), exist_ok=True)
        with open(dest + ".part", "wb") as o:
            for line, w in jsonl(src[name]):
                counts[name]["source_records"] += 1
                if doi_of(w) in dois:
                    o.write(line if line.endswith(b"\n") else line + b"\n")
                    counts[name]["kept_records"] += 1
                    ids |= ids_of(w)
        os.replace(dest + ".part", dest)
    counts["orcid"]["linked_ids_on_kept_records"] = len(ids)

    dest = os.path.join(out, NAMES["orcid"])
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dest), exist_ok=True)
    with open(dest + ".part", "wb") as raw, gzip.GzipFile(filename="", mode="wb", fileobj=raw, mtime=0, compresslevel=6) as o:
        for line, r in jsonl(src["orcid"], gzip.open):
            counts["orcid"]["source_records"] += 1
            if orcid_path(r) in ids:
                o.write(line if line.endswith(b"\n") else line + b"\n")
                counts["orcid"]["kept_records"] += 1
    os.replace(dest + ".part", dest)

    for k, c in counts.items():
        c["kept_percent"] = round(100.0 * c["kept_records"] / c["source_records"], 3) if c["source_records"] else None
        c.update(source_meta.get(k, {}))
        c["output"] = NAMES[k]
        c["output_bytes"] = os.path.getsize(os.path.join(out, NAMES[k]))
    info = {"dataset": "scholarchain-size-fraction", "fraction": p, "rule_version": RULE_VERSION, "rule": RULE,
            "dblp_persons": "not produced: scripts/run-bounded-pipeline.sh uploads no persons file and modules/hadoop/JOBS.md says persons.xml is not read",
            "dtd": source_meta.get("dtd", {}), "inputs": counts, "tool": "datasets/scripts/make_fraction.sh",
            "python": platform.python_version(), "zlib": zlib.ZLIB_VERSION, "gzip": {"mtime": 0, "compresslevel": 6},
            "generated_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")}
    with open(os.path.join(out, "run-info.json.part"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(info, f, indent=2, sort_keys=True)
        f.write("\n")
    os.replace(os.path.join(out, "run-info.json.part"), os.path.join(out, "run-info.json"))
    print(json.dumps({k: {"source": c["source_records"], "kept": c["kept_records"], "percent": c["kept_percent"]} for k, c in counts.items()}, sort_keys=True))
    return counts


def parse_xml(path, dtd):
    """expat with the external DTD loaded only from the given path (as validate.sh checks the sample)."""
    import xml.parsers.expat as expat
    parser = expat.ParserCreate()
    parser.SetParamEntityParsing(expat.XML_PARAM_ENTITY_PARSING_ALWAYS)
    n = {"n": 0}

    def ext(context, base, system_id, public_id):
        sub = parser.ExternalEntityParserCreate(context)
        with open(dtd, "rb") as d:
            sub.ParseFile(d)
        return 1

    def start(name, attrs):
        if name in ("article", "inproceedings"):
            n["n"] += 1
    parser.ExternalEntityRefHandler = ext
    parser.StartElementHandler = start
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        parser.ParseFile(f)
    return n["n"]


def digest(b):
    return hashlib.sha256(b).digest()


def load_level(paths):
    """Record digests, keys, and linkage sets of one level (a fraction directory or the full inputs)."""
    lv = {"dblp": {}, "dois": set(), "ids": set(), "rows": {}, "unlinked": {}}
    for _, key, text in dblp_records(paths["dblp"]):
        lv["dblp"][key] = digest(text.encode("latin-1"))
        lv["dois"] |= dblp_dois(text)
        lv["ids"] |= {m.group(1) for attrs, _ in dx.AUTHOR.findall(text) for m in [dx.ORCID_ATTR.search(attrs or "")] if m}
    checks = (("openalex", open, lambda w: oa_doi(w) in lv["dois"], oa_ids),
              ("crossref", open, lambda w: (w.get("DOI") or "").lower() in lv["dois"], cr_ids),
              ("orcid", gzip.open, lambda r: orcid_path(r) in lv["ids"], lambda r: set()))
    for name, opener, linked, ids_of in checks:  # orcid last: lv["ids"] then holds every linked iD
        lv[name], lv["rows"][name], lv["unlinked"][name] = set(), 0, 0
        for line, w in jsonl(paths[name], opener):
            lv[name].add(digest(line.rstrip(b"\n")))
            lv["rows"][name] += 1
            lv["unlinked"][name] += not linked(w)
            if name != "orcid":
                lv["ids"] |= ids_of(w)
    return lv


def verify(full, dirs, dtd):
    fails = 0

    def report(ok, msg):
        nonlocal fails
        print(("PASS " if ok else "FAIL ") + msg)
        fails += 0 if ok else 1
    big = load_level(full)
    print(f"INFO full: dblp {len(big['dblp'])} records; rows {big['rows']}; not linked by the fraction rule at 100 percent {big['unlinked']}")
    levels = []
    for d in dirs:
        p = int(re.search(r"fraction=(\d+)", d).group(1))
        paths = {k: os.path.join(d, v) for k, v in NAMES.items()}
        n_xml = parse_xml(paths["dblp"], dtd)
        lv = load_level(paths)
        report(n_xml == len(lv["dblp"]) > 0, f"fraction={p} dblp/works.xml parses with expat and the DTD: {n_xml} records")
        for k in ("openalex", "crossref", "orcid"):
            report(lv["rows"][k] > 0, f"fraction={p} {NAMES[k]} parses as JSON per line: {lv['rows'][k]} records")
        expect = {k for k in big["dblp"] if bucket(k) < p}
        report(set(lv["dblp"]) == expect, f"fraction={p} dblp keys equal the rule applied to the full input: {len(lv['dblp'])} kept, {len(expect)} expected")
        u = lv["unlinked"]
        report(u["openalex"] == 0 and u["crossref"] == 0, f"fraction={p} every OpenAlex and Crossref DOI is on a kept DBLP record (unlinked: openalex {u['openalex']}, crossref {u['crossref']})")
        report(u["orcid"] == 0, f"fraction={p} every ORCID iD appears on a kept record (unlinked: {u['orcid']})")
        levels.append((f"fraction={p}", lv))
    levels.append(("full", big))
    for (a, la), (b, lb) in zip(levels, levels[1:]):
        report(set(la["dblp"]) <= set(lb["dblp"]) and all(lb["dblp"][k] == v for k, v in la["dblp"].items()),
               f"nesting dblp {a} within {b}: {len(la['dblp'])} of {len(lb['dblp'])} keys, byte-identical records")
        for k in ("openalex", "crossref", "orcid"):
            report(la[k] <= lb[k], f"nesting {k} {a} within {b}: {len(la[k])} of {len(lb[k])} distinct records, byte-identical lines")
    return fails


def self_test():
    import tempfile
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        keys = [f"conf/x/K{i}" for i in range(400)]
        recs = "".join(f'<inproceedings mdate="2020-01-01" key="{k}"><author orcid="0000-0000-0001-{i:04d}">A&uuml;</author>'
                       f"<year>2020</year><ee>https://doi.org/10.1/X{i}</ee></inproceedings>\n" for i, k in enumerate(keys))
        src = {k: os.path.join(tmp, "src-" + k) for k in NAMES}
        with open(src["dblp"], "w", encoding="latin-1") as f:
            f.write('<?xml version="1.0" encoding="ISO-8859-1"?>\n<!DOCTYPE dblp SYSTEM "dblp.dtd">\n<dblp>\n' + recs + "</dblp>\n")
        with open(os.path.join(tmp, "dblp.dtd"), "w") as f:
            f.write('<!ELEMENT dblp ANY><!ENTITY uuml "&#252;">\n')
        with open(src["openalex"], "w") as f:
            f.writelines(json.dumps({"id": f"W{i}", "doi": f"https://doi.org/10.1/x{i}",
                                     "authorships": [{"author": {"orcid": f"https://orcid.org/0000-0000-0002-{i:04d}"}}]}) + "\n" for i in range(400))
            f.write(json.dumps({"id": "Wnone", "doi": "https://doi.org/10.9/unlinked"}) + "\n")
        with open(src["crossref"], "w") as f:
            f.writelines(json.dumps({"DOI": f"10.1/X{i}", "author": [{"ORCID": f"http://orcid.org/0000-0000-0003-{i:04d}"}]}) + "\n" for i in range(400))
        with gzip.open(src["orcid"], "wt") as f:
            for i in range(400):
                for s in (1, 2, 3):
                    f.write(json.dumps({"orcid-identifier": {"path": f"0000-0000-000{s}-{i:04d}"}}) + "\n")
            f.write(json.dumps({"orcid-identifier": {"path": "0000-0000-0009-0000"}}) + "\n")
        outs = []
        for p in (25, 50):
            out = os.path.join(tmp, f"fraction={p}")
            c = make(p, src, out, {})
            kept = c["dblp"]["kept_records"]
            assert kept == sum(1 for k in keys if bucket(k) < p) and 0 < kept < 400, c
            assert c["openalex"]["kept_records"] == kept and c["crossref"]["kept_records"] == kept, c
            assert c["orcid"]["kept_records"] == 3 * kept, c
            outs.append(out)
        assert verify(src, outs, os.path.join(tmp, "dblp.dtd")) == 0
        a = set(re.findall(r'key="([^"]+)"', open(os.path.join(outs[0], NAMES["dblp"]), encoding="latin-1").read()))
        b = set(re.findall(r'key="([^"]+)"', open(os.path.join(outs[1], NAMES["dblp"]), encoding="latin-1").read()))
        assert a < b, "25 must be a strict subset of 50 on 400 keys"
        o1 = open(os.path.join(outs[0], NAMES["orcid"]), "rb").read()
        make(25, src, outs[0], {})
        assert open(os.path.join(outs[0], NAMES["orcid"]), "rb").read() == o1, "gzip output must be deterministic"
    assert bucket("conf/ics/A20") == int(hashlib.sha256(b"fraction-v1|conf/ics/A20").hexdigest()[:8], 16) % 100
    print("make_fraction self-test: OK")


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    ap.add_argument("--self-test", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--verify", action="store_true", help="verify --dirs against the full inputs")
    ap.add_argument("--fraction", type=int)
    for k in NAMES:
        ap.add_argument(f"--{k}", help=f"full {k} input")
    ap.add_argument("--dtd")
    ap.add_argument("--out")
    ap.add_argument("--meta", help="JSON object of source metadata (sha256, bytes, path) per input")
    ap.add_argument("--dirs", nargs="*", default=[], help="fraction directories, smallest first")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    if a.self_test:
        self_test()
        return 0
    src = {k: getattr(a, k) for k in NAMES}
    if not all(src.values()):
        ap.error("--dblp, --openalex, --crossref and --orcid are required")
    if a.verify:
        return 1 if verify(src, a.dirs, a.dtd) else 0
    if a.fraction not in range(1, 100) or not a.out:
        ap.error("--fraction 1..99 and --out are required")
    make(a.fraction, src, a.out, json.loads(a.meta or "{}"))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

===== UNTRACKED: datasets/scripts/make_fraction.sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Build a deterministic, nested size-scaling fraction (rule fraction-v1, D-13) of the four full local inputs,
# in exactly the layout scripts/run-bounded-pipeline.sh --fraction 25|50 reads:
#   scratch/datasets/fraction=<p>/{dblp/works.xml, openalex/works.jsonl, crossref/works.jsonl,
#                                  orcid/records.jsonl.gz, run-info.json, SHA256SUMS}
# The DTD is not copied: the pipeline reads scratch/datasets/dblp/snapshot=<d>/raw/dblp.dtd for every fraction.
# DBLP persons are not produced: the pipeline uploads no persons file (modules/hadoop/JOBS.md: not read).
#
# Usage: datasets/scripts/make_fraction.sh --fraction 25|50
#        datasets/scripts/make_fraction.sh --verify   (parse, rule, linkage, and nesting checks of every
#                                                     existing fraction directory against the full inputs)
# Local only: no network. Reads the full inputs, verifies them against their SHA256SUMS, refuses to run when
# the projected output would leave less than 5 GiB free, and skips a fraction whose SHA256SUMS already verifies
# for the same source checksums. SHA256SUMS is written last and marks a complete run.
source "$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)/lib.sh"

usage() { sed -n '2,14p' "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}" | sed 's/^# \{0,1\}//'; }

FRACTION="" VERIFY=0
while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do
  case "$1" in
    --fraction) FRACTION="${2-}"; shift 2 || { usage; die "--fraction needs a value" 2; } ;;
    --verify) VERIFY=1; shift ;;
    -h|--help) usage; exit 0 ;;
    *) usage; die "unknown argument: $1" 2 ;;
  esac
done
require_tools python3 jq df awk

SNAP="$(cfg .dblp.snapshot_date)"
DBLP_DIR="$(dblp_out_dir full)"
DTD="$SCRATCH/dblp/snapshot=$SNAP/raw/dblp.dtd"
SRC_DBLP="$DBLP_DIR/works.xml"
SRC_OA="$SCRATCH/openalex/full/works.jsonl"
SRC_CR="$SCRATCH/crossref/full/works.jsonl"
SRC_OR="$SCRATCH/orcid/full/records.jsonl.gz"
for f in "$SRC_DBLP" "$SRC_OA" "$SRC_CR" "$SRC_OR" "$DTD"; do
  [ -f "$f" ] || die "missing full input ${f#"$REPO_ROOT"/}: run the fetch scripts with --full first" 6
done
PY=(python3 -I "$SCRIPTS_DIR/make_fraction.py" --dblp "$SRC_DBLP" --openalex "$SRC_OA" --crossref "$SRC_CR" --orcid "$SRC_OR")

if [ "$VERIFY" = 1 ]; then
  dirs=()
  for p in 25 50; do [ -f "$SCRATCH/fraction=$p/SHA256SUMS" ] && dirs+=("$SCRATCH/fraction=$p"); done
  [ "${#dirs[@]}" -gt 0 ] || die "no complete fraction directory to verify" 6
  for d in "${dirs[@]}"; do
    (cd "$d" && shasum -a 256 -c --quiet SHA256SUMS) || die "${d#"$REPO_ROOT"/}: SHA256SUMS does not verify" 4
    log PASS "${d#"$REPO_ROOT"/}/SHA256SUMS verifies"
  done
  log STEP "verify ${#dirs[@]} fraction(s) against the full inputs"
  "${PY[@]}" --verify --dtd "$DTD" --dirs "${dirs[@]}" || die "fraction verification failed" 4
  log DONE "verify: all checks passed"
  exit 0
fi

case "$FRACTION" in 25|50) ;; *) usage; die "--fraction 25|50 is required (100 is the full input itself)" 2 ;; esac
OUT="$SCRATCH/fraction=$FRACTION"

# Source integrity: each full input must match the SHA256SUMS its fetch run recorded.
META="{}"
for pair in "dblp:$SRC_DBLP" "openalex:$SRC_OA" "crossref:$SRC_CR" "orcid:$SRC_OR" "dtd:$DTD"; do
  name="${pair%%:*}" f="${pair#*:}"
  sum="$(sha256_of "$f")"
  if [ "$name" != dtd ]; then
    want="$(awk -v n="$(basename "$f")" '$2 == n {print $1}' "$(dirname "$f")/SHA256SUMS")"
    [ "$sum" = "$want" ] || die "${f#"$REPO_ROOT"/}: sha256 $sum does not match its SHA256SUMS ($want)" 4
  fi
  log INFO "source $name ${f#"$REPO_ROOT"/} sha256=$sum bytes=$(bytes_of "$f")"
  META="$(jq -c --arg n "$name" --arg p "${f#"$REPO_ROOT"/}" --arg s "$sum" --argjson b "$(bytes_of "$f")" \
    '. + {($n): {source_path: $p, source_sha256: $s, source_bytes: $b}}' <<<"$META")"
done

if [ -f "$OUT/SHA256SUMS" ] && [ -f "$OUT/run-info.json" ] && (cd "$OUT" && shasum -a 256 -c --quiet SHA256SUMS) &&
  [ "$(jq -c '[.inputs[] | .source_sha256] | sort' "$OUT/run-info.json")" = "$(jq -c '[del(.dtd)[] | .source_sha256] | sort' <<<"$META")" ]; then
  log SKIP "${OUT#"$REPO_ROOT"/} is complete for these sources (SHA256SUMS verifies)"
  exit 0
fi

# Disk bound: the output is about p percent of the inputs; keep at least 5 GiB free after writing.
src_kb=0
for f in "$SRC_DBLP" "$SRC_OA" "$SRC_CR" "$SRC_OR"; do src_kb=$((src_kb + $(bytes_of "$f") / 1024)); done
proj_kb=$((src_kb * (FRACTION + 10) / 100))
free_kb="$(df -Pk "$SCRATCH" | awk 'NR == 2 {print $4}')"
log PLAN "fraction $FRACTION -> ${OUT#"$REPO_ROOT"/}: projected $((proj_kb / 1024)) MiB, free $((free_kb / 1024)) MiB"
[ $((free_kb - proj_kb)) -ge $((5 * 1024 * 1024)) ] || die "projected output would leave less than 5 GiB free" 3

log STEP "select fraction $FRACTION (rule fraction-v1)"
mkdir -p "$OUT"
rm -f "$OUT/SHA256SUMS"
"${PY[@]}" --fraction "$FRACTION" --out "$OUT" --meta "$META"
write_sha256sums "$OUT"
while read -r h f; do log INFO "wrote ${OUT#"$REPO_ROOT"/}/$f bytes=$(bytes_of "$OUT/$f") sha256=$h"; done <"$OUT/SHA256SUMS"
log DONE "fraction $FRACTION complete"

===== UNTRACKED: infra/terraform/README.md
# ScholarChain Terraform

Owner: infra-engineer. Spec Phase 12, D-10, D-13, and the Phase 12 human rulings 1 to 21
(`docs/agent-runs/phase-12/prompt.md`). Agents never run `terraform plan`, `apply`, `destroy`,
`import`, or any state command; the human runs the plan through `scripts/plan.sh` and applies only
through the approved path in Phase 14.

## Layout

```
infra/terraform/
  envs/course/              the one root module (ruling 2)
    versions.tf             required_version = "1.16.6"; hashicorp/aws = 6.28.0; default tags
    main.tf                 module composition and the ECS environment contract
    variables.tf            every variable, bound, and validation
    outputs.tf              every value the scripts and ECS need (no secret values)
    course.tfvars           course values (no secrets, no personal data)
    .terraform.lock.hcl     provider lock (commit it)
  modules/
    foundation/             account, Region, partition, the two pinned AZs, name prefix
    network/                VPC, subnets, routes, S3 and DynamoDB gateway endpoints, runtime security groups
    data/                   the environment data bucket
    emr/                    EMR service and instance roles, EMR-managed security groups, log location
    build/                  ECR repository and the CodeBuild image project
    observability/          log groups (always); alarms, dashboard, SNS (runtime only)
    iam/                    ECS task execution roles and task roles (runtime only)
    dynamodb/               the seven runtime tables (runtime only)
    neptune/                Neptune cluster, instance, groups, bulk-load role (runtime only)
    vectors/                S3 Vectors bucket and five indexes (runtime only)
    messaging/              SQS job queue and DLQ (runtime only)
    apikey/                 the API key secret container (runtime only; the work order's "secrets"
                            module, renamed because the permission policy denies any secrets/ path)
    ecs/                    cluster, task definitions, services, ALB (runtime only)
```

No provider other than `hashicorp/aws` is used.

## The two stages (D-13, ruling 2)

`runtime_enabled` (default `false`) selects the cost stage.

| Stage | `runtime_enabled` | Creates |
|---|---|---|
| Cloud window 1 | `false` | network (VPC, 2 public and 2 private subnets, IGW, routes, S3 and DynamoDB gateway endpoints, closed default security group), the data bucket, EMR roles and security groups, the ECR repository, the CodeBuild project and its role, log groups |
| Cloud window 2 | `true` | window 1 plus Neptune, the DynamoDB tables, the S3 Vectors bucket and indexes, SQS, the API key secret, the ECS roles, cluster, task definitions, services and ALB, the runtime security groups, the alarms, the dashboard, and the alarm SNS topic (when `alarm_email` is set) |

EMR clusters are never Terraform resources. `scripts/run-bounded-pipeline.sh` (work order 3) reads
the `emr` output and launches each run as an ephemeral cluster that terminates after its steps.

Ending window 2 means a plan with `runtime_enabled = false`, which destroys every runtime resource,
including the stateful ones (Neptune, the DynamoDB tables, the vector bucket and indexes, SQS, and the
secret, which enters its recovery window). That destroy is expected and is the D-13 cost plan; the
data bucket, ECR, and logs stay.

### Expected resources per stage (the plan is human-run; these are counts from the code)

Window 1 (`runtime_enabled = false`, empty state): **43 to add, 0 to change, 0 to destroy**.

| Module | Resources |
|---|---|
| network | 16: `aws_vpc` 1, `aws_default_security_group` 1, `aws_internet_gateway` 1, `aws_subnet` 4, `aws_route_table` 2, `aws_route` 1, `aws_route_table_association` 4, `aws_vpc_endpoint` 2 |
| data | 7: `aws_s3_bucket` 1, `_ownership_controls` 1, `_public_access_block` 1, `_versioning` 1, `_server_side_encryption_configuration` 1, `_policy` 1, `_lifecycle_configuration` 1 |
| emr | 10: `aws_iam_role` 2, `aws_iam_role_policy_attachment` 1, `aws_iam_role_policy` 2, `aws_iam_instance_profile` 1, `aws_security_group` 2, `aws_vpc_security_group_egress_rule` 2 |
| build | 6: `aws_ecr_repository` 1, `aws_ecr_lifecycle_policy` 1, `aws_cloudwatch_log_group` 1, `aws_iam_role` 1, `aws_iam_role_policy` 1, `aws_codebuild_project` 1 |
| observability | 4: `aws_cloudwatch_log_group` 4 |

Window 2 (from the window 1 state, `runtime_enabled = true`, one ALB CIDR, `alarm_email` set):
**91 to add, 0 to change, 0 to destroy** (89 without an alarm email). From an empty state: 134.

| Module | Resources |
|---|---|
| network | 24: `aws_security_group` 6, `aws_vpc_security_group_ingress_rule` 7 (ALB 1 per CIDR, 2 from the ALB, 4 into Neptune), `aws_vpc_security_group_egress_rule` 11 (ALB 2, HTTPS 4, to Neptune 4, Neptune to the S3 prefix list 1) |

| observability | 12: `aws_sns_topic` 1 and `aws_sns_topic_subscription` 1 (only with an email), `aws_cloudwatch_metric_alarm` 9 (ALB target and ELB 5xx, 2 unhealthy-target, DLQ, 3 ECS CPU, Neptune CPU), `aws_cloudwatch_dashboard` 1 |

## Variables

All variables, their bounds, and their validations are in `envs/course/variables.tf`; the course
values are in `envs/course/course.tfvars`. The ones a human sets or changes:

| Variable | Default | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `runtime_enabled` | `false` | the cost stage; `true` requires `image_tag` and the three run IDs (validated) |
| `alarm_email` | none (required) | pass `TF_VAR_alarm_email`; `""` creates no subscription. Never in `course.tfvars` |
| `image_tag` | `null` | the 40-hex Git SHA that `scripts/package.sh` built and pushed |
| `runtime_graph_run`, `runtime_items_run`, `runtime_vectors_run` | `null` | `--graph-run`, `--items-run`, `--vectors-run` of the three services |
| `runtime_snapshot` | `2026-10-03` | `--snapshot` |
| `window_summary_run` | `run-6fbad498beb0d8f570df67665fb97544` | `SCHOLARCHAIN_WINDOW_SUMMARY_RUN`, query-api and worker only |
| `availability_zones` | `["us-east-2a", "us-east-2b"]` | exactly two distinct AZs in the Region (validated); pinned, not a data source, so an AZ state change never replans the subnets. A change replaces the subnets |
| `alb_ingress_cidr_blocks` | `["0.0.0.0/0"]` | narrow to the demo client when possible |
| `data_bucket_force_destroy` | `false` | course switch, see Cleanup |
| `emr_instance_type`, `emr_core_instance_count` | `m6g.xlarge`, `2` | Graviton only; core count 1 to 2; primary always 1 |
| `neptune_instance_class` | `db.t4g.medium` | `db.t4g.medium` or `db.t3.medium` only |
| `service_max_counts` | query-api 1, graph-evidence-api 2, worker 1 | each 1 or 2 (ruling 2) |
| `haiku_calls_per_minute_cap` | `8` | validated 1 to 8 |
| `llm_requests_per_minute` | `4` | validated: `llm_requests_per_minute x (query-api max + worker max) <= haiku_calls_per_minute_cap` |
| `haiku_destination_regions` | `["us-east-1", "us-east-2", "us-west-2"]` | the US Haiku 4.5 profile's Regions (ruling 18) |

## Design notes

### Data bucket
- `scholarchain-course-data-<account>-us-east-2`, output `data_bucket_name` (SCHOLARCHAIN_BUCKET).
- Prefixes are the core `storage.prefixes` (`raw/`, `curated/`, `graph/`, `semantic-context/`,
  `vectors/`, `reports/`, `logs/`, `runs/`) plus `artifacts/` (job jars under `artifacts/jobs/`, image
  build contexts under `artifacts/codebuild/source/`). The work order's "rag" prefix is
  `semantic-context/` plus `vectors/`.
- Versioning, SSE-S3, all four public access blocks, `BucketOwnerEnforced`, and a TLS-only policy.
- Lifecycle: incomplete multipart uploads abort after 1 day; `logs/` current objects expire after 30
  days (the Phase 7 ruling 22 audit retention); `artifacts/` after 90 days; noncurrent versions of
  both after 7 days. No rule touches the data prefixes.
- `force_destroy` comes only from `data_bucket_force_destroy` (default `false`). With `false`, a
  destroy of the bucket fails while any object version remains, which protects grading evidence.

### Network and security groups
- `10.40.0.0/16`, two AZs (`availability_zones`, default us-east-2a and us-east-2b). Public subnets (`/20`) for the ALB, the ECS tasks (public IP) and EMR
  nodes; private subnets for Neptune only. No NAT gateway, so the public subnets assign public IPs.
- Security groups by role: the ALB accepts port 80 from `alb_ingress_cidr_blocks` (the only CIDR
  ingress) and sends only to the query-api (8080) and graph-evidence-api (8081) groups; those accept
  only from the ALB; the worker and loader accept nothing; Neptune accepts 8182 only from the four
  task groups and sends only to the S3 prefix list (bulk loader). Task groups send HTTPS to
  `0.0.0.0/0`, because without NAT or interface endpoints the AWS APIs (ECR, Logs, Secrets Manager,
  SQS, Bedrock, S3 Vectors) are public endpoints. The default security group has no rules.
- EMR uses two EMR-managed groups; EMR writes their intra-cluster rules, so Terraform ignores rule
  drift there and revokes the rules on delete. Terraform therefore cannot show or enforce the ingress
  EMR adds: that no rule admits `0.0.0.0/0` or `::/0` is unverified until the first cluster runs.
  `scripts/run-bounded-pipeline.sh` describes both groups after every cluster, records them, and
  warns about any such rule.

### Neptune
- Engine `1.4.8.1` (released 2026-10-06 per the Neptune engine release table, at least 14 days old,
  later than 1.4.6.0), parameter family `neptune1.4`, `db.t4g.medium`, one instance, private subnets,
  storage encrypted with the AWS-managed key, IAM authentication on, `neptune_query_timeout` 30 s.
- Course settings: `deletion_protection = false`, `skip_final_snapshot = true`, backup retention 1
  day. The window lasts about 48 hours and the graph is rebuilt by the bulk loader from `graph/`.
- Bulk-load role `scholarchain-course-neptune-bulk-loader` (trusted by `rds.amazonaws.com`, attached
  to the cluster): `s3:ListBucket` with `s3:prefix` `graph/*` and `s3:GetObject` on `graph/*`.
- Runtime grant: `neptune-db:ReadDataViaQuery` only.

### DynamoDB
- Seven tables named `<dynamodb_table_prefix><Table>`, on demand, string keys as in
  `modules/storage` `DynamoContract`; TTL on `expiresAt` for QuerySession, EvidenceBundle, and
  ServiceState (`recordKey`, `recordType`); GSI `evidenceById` (hash `itemKey`, range `queryId`,
  projection ALL) on EvidenceBundle. Default encryption (AWS owned key).
- PITR off and deletion protection off: the loaded tables are rebuilt from the write-once S3 item
  files by `LoadDynamoCli`, the runtime tables are 30-day TTL records, and the window is ephemeral.

### S3 Vectors
- Vector bucket `scholarchain-course-vectors-<account>` (output `vector_bucket_name`, SSE-S3) and the
  five indexes of `s3-vectors.indexes`: float32, 1024 dimensions, cosine, non-filterable metadata keys
  `vectorKey`, `textHash`, `textRef` (fixed at creation).

### SQS and the API key
- `scholarchain-course-jobs`: visibility 300 s, retention 4 days, long poll 20 s, SQS-managed SSE,
  redrive to `scholarchain-course-jobs-dlq` after 3 receives. The DLQ keeps 14 days and accepts
  redrives only from the job queue.
- `scholarchain-course/api-key`: a Secrets Manager secret with no version. ECS injects it as
  `SCHOLARCHAIN_API_KEY` by ARN into the three services (the loader gets none).

### ECS and the ALB
- Cluster `scholarchain-course-runtime`, Fargate ARM64, one image tag (`image_tag`) for every task.
- Entrypoint: the image keeps the sbt-native-packager default ENTRYPOINT `/opt/docker/bin/services`,
  which needs `-main <class>` because the package has several mains. An ECS `command` is appended to
  the entrypoint, so a command `bin/query-api` would fail. Each task definition therefore sets
  `entryPoint` to `/opt/docker/bin/query-api`, `/opt/docker/bin/graph-evidence-api`, or
  `/opt/docker/bin/worker`, and the `command` holds only the arguments. The image is unchanged.
- Arguments (all three services): `--live --region us-east-2 --snapshot <d> --graph-run <r>
  --items-run <r> --vectors-run <r>`. No `--profile`: credentials come from the task role
  (STORAGE.md "Credentials on ECS"). No `AWS_PROFILE`, key, token, web identity, or `AWS_ROLE_ARN`
  variable is set.
- query-api and the worker share one argument list and one environment (SERVICES.md section 5).
  Environment: `SCHOLARCHAIN_ENVIRONMENT=course`, Region, account, bucket, Neptune endpoint, port,
  and bulk-load role, table prefix, vector bucket, the four model IDs (planner and synthesis Haiku
  4.5; verification and adjudication are not called at runtime but must be non-placeholder outside
  `local`, so they are set to the same Haiku profile), the embedding model, `SCHOLARCHAIN_PROMPTS_DIR
  =/opt/docker/prompts`, `SCHOLARCHAIN_STUB_LLM=false`, the queue and DLQ URLs, the max receive count,
  and `SCHOLARCHAIN_LLM_REQUESTS_PER_MINUTE`; query-api and worker also get
  `SCHOLARCHAIN_WINDOW_SUMMARY_RUN`. A precondition refuses any empty value (Phase 11 D12).
- `stopTimeout` 30 s; awslogs to `/scholarchain/scholarchain-course/ecs/<task>`.
- Sizes: 1 vCPU and 2 GiB per service task; 2 vCPU and 8 GiB for the loader.
- Services: desired 1, minimum healthy 100%, deployment circuit breaker with rollback, public IP in
  the public subnets. Application Auto Scaling holds each service between 1 and its maximum; services
  with a maximum above 1 (graph-evidence-api by default) track 60% CPU.
- Haiku bound: each process throttles its own Haiku calls at `runtime.llm.requests-per-minute`, so
  the combined rate is that value times the query-api plus worker task count. Defaults: query-api
  maximum 1, worker maximum 1, 4 per minute each, 8 per minute in total. For the scale-out
  experiment, `service_max_counts.query_api = 2` with `llm_requests_per_minute = 2` (validation
  enforces the product), at the cost of slower synchronous answers.
- ALB: public, HTTP on port 80, idle timeout 60 s (above the 40 s synchronous deadline), invalid
  headers dropped. Rules: `/v1/query`, `/v1/jobs/*`, `/v1/explain/*` and `/healthz`, `/readyz` to
  query-api; `/v1/evidence/*`, `/v1/graph/*` to graph-evidence-api; everything else a fixed 404.
  `/metrics` is not routed. Target health uses `/healthz` (liveness), so a downstream outage that
  `/readyz` reports does not make ECS replace healthy tasks.
- **HTTP risk.** HTTPS needs a domain and a certificate, which the course window does not have. The
  API key therefore travels in clear text in the `X-Api-Key` header. Mitigations: a narrow
  `alb_ingress_cidr_blocks`, a key used only for the window and rotated or deleted after it.

### Neptune loader path (cloud window 2)
Neptune is private, so `LoadNeptuneCli` (StartLoaderJob and GetLoaderJobStatus on the Neptune
endpoint) must run inside the VPC. Chosen path: a one-off ECS task definition
`scholarchain-course-neptune-loader` from the services image, which already holds the storage
classes, run by `scripts/load-stores.sh` with a command override:

```
entryPoint /opt/docker/bin/query-api   (the -main option selects another class)
command    -main scholarchain.storage.cli.LoadNeptuneCli --live
           --dir /tmp/stores/s3/graph/neptune/snapshot=<d>/run=<r> --out /tmp/stores
           [--loader-role-arn <neptune_loader_role_arn>]        (default: SCHOLARCHAIN_NEPTUNE_LOADER_ROLE_ARN)
command    -main scholarchain.storage.cli.LoadNeptuneCli --live --status <loadId>
```

Why not an EMR step: the storage classes are not in the Hadoop or Spark job jars, and a cluster
costs more than a minutes-long Fargate task. The loader task role reads `graph/neptune/*`,
`curated/coauthor-evidence/*`, and `runs/store-conversion/*`, and may only start and read bulk
loads; the load itself reads S3 with the bulk-load role. Network: public subnet, public IP, loader
security group. The live loader CLI runs without `--profile` and stages its local copy from the
bucket with `--stage-from-bucket` (Phase 12 work order 5). The other loads (`LoadS3Cli`, `LoadDynamoCli`, `LoadVectorsCli`) call public AWS
APIs and run from the laptop with a profile.

### IAM summary
| Role | Grants |
|---|---|
| `scholarchain-course-emr-service` | AWS `AmazonEMRServicePolicy_v2` (EC2 actions limited to resources tagged `for-use-with-amazon-emr-managed-policies=true`); `iam:PassRole` on the EMR instance role to `ec2.amazonaws.com` |
| `scholarchain-course-emr-instance` (+ profile) | data bucket: GetBucketLocation, ListBucketMultipartUploads; ListBucket only with `s3:prefix` `raw/* curated/* artifacts/* logs/emr/*`; GetObject on `raw/ curated/ artifacts/`; Put, Delete, AbortMultipartUpload, ListMultipartUploadParts on `curated/ logs/emr/` (no `graph/`, `semantic-context/`, `runs/`, or `reports/` grant; code review 1 F1) |
| `scholarchain-course-codebuild-services-image` | Logs on its log group; GetObject, GetObjectVersion on `artifacts/codebuild/source/*`; GetBucketLocation, GetBucketAcl; `ecr:GetAuthorizationToken` (no resource scope exists); push and pull actions on the services repository only |
| `scholarchain-course-neptune-bulk-loader` | ListBucket (prefix `graph/*`), GetObject `graph/*` |
| `scholarchain-course-ecs-exec-services` | ECR pull on the services repository, `ecr:GetAuthorizationToken`, logs on the three service log groups, `secretsmanager:GetSecretValue` on the API key secret |
| `scholarchain-course-ecs-exec-loader` | ECR pull, logs on the loader log group; no secret |
| `scholarchain-course-task-query-api` | `s3:ListBucket` (unconditioned, so missing keys answer 404, SERVICES.md section 4); GetObject `logs/rag-runtime/*`, `reports/rag-runtime/*`, `curated/window-summary/*`, `curated/coauthor-evidence/*`, `semantic-context/text/*`; PutObject `logs/rag-runtime/*`, `reports/rag-runtime/*`; DynamoDB GetItem and Query on EntityLinkCache, ResearcherMetricCache, ArtifactRegistry, QuerySession, EvidenceBundle; PutItem on QuerySession, EvidenceBundle; GetItem and PutItem on ServiceState; `neptune-db:ReadDataViaQuery`; `s3vectors:QueryVectors`, `GetVectors` on the five indexes; `bedrock:InvokeModel` on Titan V2, on the Haiku 4.5 US profile, and on the Haiku foundation model in the profile's destination Regions only through that profile; `sqs:SendMessage` on the job queue |
| `scholarchain-course-task-worker` | the query-api grants without SendMessage, plus `sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `DeleteMessage`, `ChangeMessageVisibility` on the job queue |
| `scholarchain-course-task-graph-evidence-api` | `dynamodb:Query` on `EvidenceBundle/index/evidenceById`; GetObject `logs/rag-runtime/*`; `neptune-db:ReadDataViaQuery`. No window-summary, vector, model, queue, or ServiceState grant |
| `scholarchain-course-task-neptune-loader` | ListBucket (prefixes `graph/neptune/*`, `curated/coauthor-evidence/*`, `runs/store-conversion/*`); GetObject on those prefixes; `neptune-db:StartLoaderJob`, `GetLoaderJobStatus` |

Every role trusts one service principal. Every role except the EMR instance role also carries
`aws:SourceAccount` (and `aws:SourceArn` for ECS and the EMR service role). The EMR instance role
trusts `ec2.amazonaws.com` with no condition: an unverified condition there could stop clusters
from launching (ruling 19). No statement uses a wildcard action. Deployment (the human's `scholarchain-sdk` profile) and
runtime roles are separate.

## Stateful resources and their protections

| Resource | Stage | Protection |
|---|---|---|
| Data bucket | always | versioning, `force_destroy = false`, public access blocked, TLS only; noncurrent versions kept except under `logs/` and `artifacts/` (7 days) |
| ECR repository | always | immutable tags, `force_delete = false` (destroy fails while images exist), lifecycle removes untagged images only |
| Log groups | always | 14-day retention |
| Neptune cluster | runtime | encrypted; deletion protection off and skip-final-snapshot on (course, documented); rebuilt from `graph/` |
| DynamoDB tables | runtime | deletion protection off, PITR off (course, documented); rebuilt from `curated/dynamodb/` |
| S3 Vectors bucket and indexes | runtime | rebuilt from `vectors/backup/` by `LoadVectorsCli`; index settings are force-new, so any change to dimension, metric, or keys replaces the index |
| API key secret | runtime | no value in Terraform; 7-day recovery window on destroy |
| SQS queues | runtime | transient job messages only |

Any plan line that destroys or replaces one of these outside the planned window 2 teardown needs
explicit human sign-off.

## Cost estimate per stage

Prices are us-east-2 on-demand list prices remembered from the AWS pricing pages, which are not on
docs.aws.amazon.com and were not read here: **UNVERIFIED**. The human checks them at the plan gate.

| Stage | Hourly | Per window |
|---|---|---|
| Window 1, idle (nothing running) | about $0.00 (S3 and ECR storage, a few cents per month) | under $0.10 |
| Window 1, one EMR run (1 primary + 2 core `m6g.xlarge`, about $0.154 EC2 + $0.039 EMR each, 3 public IPv4 at $0.005, EBS) | about $0.60 | about $0.60 per cluster-hour; four runs (baseline plus 25/50/100 percent) of about 1 hour each: about $2.50 |
| Image build (CodeBuild ARM small) | about $0.20 per build-hour | about $0.02 per 5-minute build |
| Window 2 (Neptune `db.t4g.medium` about $0.09; 3 Fargate ARM tasks of 1 vCPU and 2 GiB about $0.04 each; ALB about $0.03 with LCUs; 5 public IPv4 about $0.025; Secrets Manager, alarms, dashboard about $0.01) | about $0.27 | 48 hours: about $13, plus one-off loads (DynamoDB about 1.2 M WRU, about $0.75; Neptune I/O and storage under $1; S3 Vectors under $0.10) and Bedrock use (Haiku 4.5, bounded at 8 calls per minute) |

Largest drivers: the window 2 duration (Neptune and Fargate by the hour) and EMR cluster hours.
Leaving `runtime_enabled = true` costs about $6.50 per day.

## Remote-state design (documented only; ruling 1 keeps local state)

Local state: `envs/course/terraform.tfstate` (gitignored by `*.tfstate`), backed up by the human
outside the repository after every apply. A later remote backend would be:

- an S3 bucket `scholarchain-tfstate-<account>-us-east-2`, created once outside this root (a
  bootstrap root or by hand), with versioning, SSE-S3, all public access blocked, a TLS-only and
  deployer-role-only bucket policy, and no lifecycle expiry of state versions;
- `backend "s3" { bucket = ..., key = "scholarchain/course/terraform.tfstate", region = "us-east-2",
  use_lockfile = true, encrypt = true }` (S3-native locking, so no DynamoDB lock table);
- migration with `terraform init -migrate-state` by the human, and reads of state denied to every
  agent as today.

## Human actions

1. **Init** (once, and after any provider or module change):
   `terraform -chdir=infra/terraform/envs/course init -backend=false -input=false` for validation, or
   without `-backend=false` for the local backend used by plan and apply. Commit
   `envs/course/.terraform.lock.hcl`.
2. **Plan** at the Phase 12 gate, through `scripts/plan.sh` (work order 3) with
   `AWS_PROFILE=scholarchain-sdk`, `TF_VAR_alarm_email` set (or empty), and `course.tfvars`. Review
   the add, change, and destroy counts against the tables above, every IAM statement, and any
   stateful destroy or replacement; sign the plan checksum.
3. **Apply** only in Phase 14, only through the approved path (`scripts/deploy.sh` with the approved
   plan checksum), window 1 first.
4. **Before window 2**: verify with read-only calls that `db.t4g.medium` is orderable for engine
   1.4.8.1 (`aws neptune describe-orderable-db-instance-options`), that `m6g.xlarge` is supported by
   `emr-spark-8.0.0` (`aws emr list-supported-instance-types`), and the Haiku 4.5 US profile's
   destination Regions (`aws bedrock get-inference-profile`). Done by the human on 2026-11-14
   (ruling 18): Neptune is orderable in us-east-2a, 2b, and 2c; m6g.xlarge is supported; the profile
   routes to us-east-1, us-east-2, and us-west-2. Repeat them if a value changes.
5. **Secret value** right after the window 2 apply, without the value entering shell history or any
   file in the repository, for example
   `read -rs K && printf '%s' "$K" | aws secretsmanager put-secret-value --secret-id scholarchain-course/api-key --secret-string file:///dev/stdin; unset K`.
   Until the value exists, ECS cannot start the service tasks; `scripts/deploy.sh` then forces a new
   deployment.
6. **Cleanup** is `scripts/cleanup.sh`, human only. `data_bucket_force_destroy` stays `false` until
   the instructor approves the evidence archive.

===== UNTRACKED: infra/terraform/envs/course/variables.tf
# Root variables of the course environment. Values for this environment are in course.tfvars (no
# secrets). Every bound below is a default the human approved through rulings 1 and 2; changing one
# beyond its validation range is a human-gated scale change (docs/agents.md, escalation 6).

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Foundation and tags
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

variable "region" {
  description = "AWS Region (D-04)."
  type        = string
  default     = "us-east-2"

  validation {
    condition     = var.region == "us-east-2"
    error_message = "D-04 ratified us-east-2; another Region is an architecture change."
  }
}

  description = "Environment name; part of every resource name and the Environment tag."
  type        = string
  default     = "course"

    condition     = can(regex("^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{1,15}$", var.environment))
    error_message = "environment must be 2 to 16 lower-case letters, digits, or hyphens."
  }
}

  description = "Owner tag: a role name, never a personal name or email."
  type        = string

  description = "Expiration tag (YYYY-MM-DD): the date after which every resource may be cleaned up."

  validation {
    condition     = can(regex("^20[0-9]{2}-[01][0-9]-[0-3][0-9]$", var.expiration_date))
    error_message = "expiration_date must be YYYY-MM-DD."
  }
}

variable "runtime_enabled" {
  description = "Cost stage (D-13, ruling 2). false: cloud window 1 (network, data bucket, EMR roles and security groups, ECR, CodeBuild, log groups). true: also Neptune, DynamoDB, S3 Vectors, SQS, the API key secret, ECS, the ALB, and the runtime alarms (cloud window 2)."
  type        = bool
  default     = false

  validation {
    condition     = !var.runtime_enabled || (var.image_tag != null && var.runtime_graph_run != null && var.runtime_items_run != null && var.runtime_vectors_run != null)
    error_message = "runtime_enabled = true needs image_tag (from scripts/package.sh) and runtime_graph_run, runtime_items_run, and runtime_vectors_run."
  }
}

variable "availability_zones" {
  description = "The two AZs of the VPC (ruling 1), pinned rather than read from a data source (code review 1 F6). Neptune 1.4.8.1 on db.t4g.medium is orderable in us-east-2a and us-east-2b (ruling 18). Changing a value replaces the subnets and everything in them."
  type        = list(string)
  default     = ["us-east-2a", "us-east-2b"]

  validation {
    condition     = length(var.availability_zones) == 2 && length(distinct(var.availability_zones)) == 2 && alltrue([for z in var.availability_zones : can(regex("^${var.region}[a-z]$", z))])
    error_message = "availability_zones must be exactly two distinct AZ names in var.region (for example us-east-2a and us-east-2b)."
  }
}

variable "vpc_cidr" {
  description = "VPC CIDR; split into two public and two private /20 subnets."
  type        = string
  default     = "10.40.0.0/16"

  validation {
    condition     = can(cidrhost(var.vpc_cidr, 0)) && tonumber(split("/", var.vpc_cidr)[1]) <= 16
    error_message = "vpc_cidr must be a valid CIDR of /16 or larger."
  }
}

variable "alb_ingress_cidr_blocks" {
  description = "CIDR blocks allowed to reach the ALB listener (the only broad ingress, ruling 1). Narrow it to the demo client when possible."
  type        = list(string)
  default     = ["0.0.0.0/0"]

  validation {
    condition     = length(var.alb_ingress_cidr_blocks) >= 1 && alltrue([for c in var.alb_ingress_cidr_blocks : can(cidrhost(c, 0))])
    error_message = "alb_ingress_cidr_blocks must hold at least one valid IPv4 CIDR."
  }
}
