variable "haiku_inference_profile_id" {
  description = "Bedrock US inference profile of Claude Haiku 4.5 (D-12), used for the planner and synthesis."
  type        = string
  default     = "us.anthropic.claude-haiku-4-5-20251001-v1:0"

  validation {
    condition     = startswith(var.haiku_inference_profile_id, "us.")
    error_message = "haiku_inference_profile_id must be a US geo inference profile (us.)."
  }
}

variable "haiku_destination_regions" {
  description = "Regions the US Haiku 4.5 profile routes to (verified read-only by the human, ruling 18). The IAM grant on the foundation model covers exactly these."
  type        = list(string)
  default     = ["us-east-1", "us-east-2", "us-west-2"]
}

variable "service_max_counts" {
  description = "ECS maximum task count per service (ruling 2: desired 1, maximum at most 2). query-api and worker are 1 so that their Haiku calls stay within haiku_calls_per_minute_cap (see llm_requests_per_minute)."
  type = object({
    query_api          = number
    graph_evidence_api = number
    worker             = number
  })
  default = {
    query_api          = 1
    graph_evidence_api = 2
    worker             = 1
  }

  validation {
    condition     = alltrue([for n in values(var.service_max_counts) : n >= 1 && n <= 2])
    error_message = "Ruling 2: every ECS service maximum is 1 or 2."
  }
}

variable "haiku_calls_per_minute_cap" {
  description = "Upper bound on live Haiku calls per minute from query-api and the worker together (work order 2: 8)."
  type        = number
  default     = 8

  validation {
    condition     = var.haiku_calls_per_minute_cap >= 1 && var.haiku_calls_per_minute_cap <= 8
    error_message = "haiku_calls_per_minute_cap must be 1 to 8 (code review 1 F5); more is a human-gated scale change."
  }
}

variable "llm_requests_per_minute" {
  description = "Per-process Haiku throttle (runtime.llm.requests-per-minute), passed as SCHOLARCHAIN_LLM_REQUESTS_PER_MINUTE. The product with the query-api plus worker maximums must not exceed haiku_calls_per_minute_cap. The core loader honours the variable (Phase 12 work order 4) and caps each process at 8."
  type        = number
  default     = 4

  validation {
    condition     = var.llm_requests_per_minute >= 1 && var.llm_requests_per_minute * (var.service_max_counts.query_api + var.service_max_counts.worker) <= var.haiku_calls_per_minute_cap
    error_message = "llm_requests_per_minute x (query_api + worker maximum tasks) must not exceed haiku_calls_per_minute_cap."
  }
}

variable "task_sizes" {
  description = "Fargate ARM64 task CPU units and memory MiB per task definition."
  type = map(object({
    cpu    = number
    memory = number
  }))
  default = {
    query-api          = { cpu = 1024, memory = 2048 }
    graph-evidence-api = { cpu = 1024, memory = 2048 }
    worker             = { cpu = 1024, memory = 2048 }
    neptune-loader     = { cpu = 2048, memory = 8192 }
  }

  validation {
    condition     = alltrue([for k in ["query-api", "graph-evidence-api", "worker", "neptune-loader"] : contains(keys(var.task_sizes), k)]) && alltrue([for s in values(var.task_sizes) : s.cpu <= 2048 && s.memory <= 8192])
    error_message = "task_sizes needs all four task definitions, each at most 2 vCPU and 8 GiB."
  }
}

===== UNTRACKED: infra/terraform/envs/course/versions.tf
# Terraform and provider pins (ruling 4, D-09). Local state only (ruling 1): no backend block, so
# Terraform uses the local backend; terraform.tfstate is gitignored and backed up by the human outside
# the repository. The remote-state design is documented in infra/terraform/README.md.
terraform {
  required_version = "1.16.6"

  required_providers {
    aws = {
      source  = "hashicorp/aws"
      version = "= 6.28.0"
    }
  }
}

provider "aws" {
  region = var.region

  default_tags {
    tags = {
      Project     = "ScholarChain"
      Environment = var.environment
      Course      = "CS441"
      Owner       = var.owner
      Expiration  = var.expiration_date
      ManagedBy   = "terraform"
    }
  }
}

===== UNTRACKED: infra/terraform/modules/apikey/main.tf
# API key secret (runtime_enabled; D-10 item 9; the work order's "secrets" module, named apikey because
# the permission policy denies any path segment named secrets/). Terraform creates the secret container
# only; it never holds a value, so no secret enters source, variables, plans, or state. The human sets
# the value after apply (infra/terraform/README.md). ECS injects it as SCHOLARCHAIN_API_KEY by ARN.
# Encryption: the AWS-managed key aws/secretsmanager.
terraform {
  required_providers {
    aws = {
      source = "hashicorp/aws"
    }
  }
}

variable "name" {
  type = string
}

variable "recovery_window_days" {
  type = number
}

resource "aws_secretsmanager_secret" "api_key" {
  name                    = "${var.name}/api-key"
  description             = "ScholarChain REST API key (X-Api-Key). Value set by the human, never by Terraform."
  recovery_window_in_days = var.recovery_window_days
}

output "api_key_secret_arn" {
  value = aws_secretsmanager_secret.api_key.arn
}

output "api_key_secret_name" {
  value = aws_secretsmanager_secret.api_key.name
}

===== UNTRACKED: infra/terraform/modules/build/main.tf
# Images without Docker on the laptop (ruling 3): one ECR repository (immutable tags, scan on push)
# and one CodeBuild project (ARM container, privileged for docker) that builds the services image
# from the Docker context scripts/package.sh stages with `sbt services/Docker/stage`, zips, and
# uploads under artifacts/codebuild/source/. The project tags the image with the Git SHA passed as
# IMAGE_TAG and pushes it.
terraform {
  required_providers {
    aws = {
      source = "hashicorp/aws"
    }
  }
}

variable "name" {
  type = string
}

variable "account_id" {
  type = string
}

variable "region" {
  type = string
}

variable "data_bucket_arn" {
  type = string
}

variable "data_bucket_name" {
  type = string
}

variable "log_retention_days" {
  type = number
}

locals {
  source_prefix = "artifacts/codebuild/source/"
  log_group     = "/scholarchain/${var.name}/codebuild/services-image"
  project       = "${var.name}-services-image"
}

resource "aws_ecr_repository" "services" {
  name                 = "${var.name}-services"
  image_tag_mutability = "IMMUTABLE"
  # Never delete a repository that still holds images (law 3).
  force_delete = false

  image_scanning_configuration {
    scan_on_push = true
  }

  encryption_configuration {
    encryption_type = "AES256"
  }
}

# Removes only untagged images (failed or superseded pushes); every Git SHA tag is kept.
resource "aws_ecr_lifecycle_policy" "services" {
  repository = aws_ecr_repository.services.name
  policy = jsonencode({
    rules = [{
      rulePriority = 1
      description  = "Expire untagged images after 7 days"
      selection = {
        tagStatus   = "untagged"
        countType   = "sinceImagePushed"
        countUnit   = "days"
        countNumber = 7
      }
      action = { type = "expire" }
    }]
  })
}

resource "aws_cloudwatch_log_group" "codebuild" {
  name              = local.log_group
  retention_in_days = var.log_retention_days
}

data "aws_iam_policy_document" "trust" {
  statement {
    actions = ["sts:AssumeRole"]

    principals {
      type        = "Service"
      identifiers = ["codebuild.amazonaws.com"]
    }

    condition {
      test     = "StringEquals"
      variable = "aws:SourceAccount"
      values   = [var.account_id]
    }
  }
}

resource "aws_iam_role" "codebuild" {
  name               = "${var.name}-codebuild-services-image"
  description        = "CodeBuild: read the build source prefix, push to the services repository, write its log group"
  assume_role_policy = data.aws_iam_policy_document.trust.json
}

data "aws_iam_policy_document" "codebuild" {
  statement {
    sid       = "Logs"
    actions   = ["logs:CreateLogStream", "logs:PutLogEvents"]
    resources = ["${aws_cloudwatch_log_group.codebuild.arn}:*"]
  }

  statement {
    sid       = "SourceBucket"
    actions   = ["s3:GetBucketLocation", "s3:GetBucketAcl"]
    resources = [var.data_bucket_arn]
  }

  statement {
    sid       = "SourceObjects"
    actions   = ["s3:GetObject", "s3:GetObjectVersion"]
    resources = ["${var.data_bucket_arn}/${local.source_prefix}*"]
  }

  # GetAuthorizationToken has no resource-level scope.
  statement {
    sid       = "EcrLogin"
    actions   = ["ecr:GetAuthorizationToken"]
    resources = ["*"]
  }

  statement {
    sid = "EcrPush"
    actions = [
      "ecr:BatchCheckLayerAvailability",
      "ecr:BatchGetImage",
      "ecr:GetDownloadUrlForLayer",
      "ecr:InitiateLayerUpload",
      "ecr:UploadLayerPart",
      "ecr:CompleteLayerUpload",
      "ecr:PutImage",
    ]
    resources = [aws_ecr_repository.services.arn]
  }
}

resource "aws_iam_role_policy" "codebuild" {
  name   = "services-image"
  role   = aws_iam_role.codebuild.id
  policy = data.aws_iam_policy_document.codebuild.json
}

resource "aws_codebuild_project" "services_image" {
  name                   = local.project
  description            = "Builds and pushes the ScholarChain services image (linux/arm64) tagged with the Git SHA"
  service_role           = aws_iam_role.codebuild.arn
  build_timeout          = 30
  queued_timeout         = 30
  concurrent_build_limit = 1

  # scripts/package.sh overrides the location per build with artifacts/codebuild/source/<sha>.zip.
  source {
    type      = "S3"
    location  = "${var.data_bucket_name}/${local.source_prefix}services-docker-context.zip"
    buildspec = <<-YAML
      version: 0.2
      env:
        shell: bash
      phases:
        pre_build:
          commands:
            - '[[ "$IMAGE_TAG" =~ ^[0-9a-f]{40}$ ]] || { echo "IMAGE_TAG must be a 40-hex Git SHA"; exit 1; }'
            - test -f Dockerfile
            - aws ecr get-login-password --region "$AWS_REGION" | docker login --username AWS --password-stdin "$${REPOSITORY_URI%%/*}"
        build:
          commands:
            - docker build --platform linux/arm64 -t "$REPOSITORY_URI:$IMAGE_TAG" .
        post_build:
          commands:
            - '[ "$CODEBUILD_BUILD_SUCCEEDING" = "1" ] || exit 1'
            - docker push "$REPOSITORY_URI:$IMAGE_TAG"
            - echo "==> IMAGE $(docker inspect --format '{{index .RepoDigests 0}}' "$REPOSITORY_URI:$IMAGE_TAG")"
    YAML
  }

  artifacts {
    type = "NO_ARTIFACTS"
  }

  environment {
    type            = "ARM_CONTAINER"
    compute_type    = "BUILD_GENERAL1_SMALL"
    image           = "aws/codebuild/amazonlinux-aarch64-standard:3.0"
    privileged_mode = true

    environment_variable {
      name  = "REPOSITORY_URI"
      value = aws_ecr_repository.services.repository_url
    }

    # Placeholder; scripts/package.sh overrides it, and the buildspec refuses anything but a SHA.
    environment_variable {
      name  = "IMAGE_TAG"
      value = "unset"
    }
  }

  logs_config {
    cloudwatch_logs {
      group_name = aws_cloudwatch_log_group.codebuild.name
      status     = "ENABLED"
    }

    s3_logs {
      status = "DISABLED"
    }
  }
}

output "repository_url" {
  value = aws_ecr_repository.services.repository_url

    status = "Enabled"

    filter {}

    abort_incomplete_multipart_upload {
      days_after_initiation = 1
    }

  # logs/: runtime audits (30-day retention, Phase 7 ruling 22) and EMR logs.

    id     = "logs"
    status = "Enabled"

    }

      noncurrent_days = var.noncurrent_version_expiration_days

===== UNTRACKED: infra/terraform/modules/dynamodb/main.tf
# DynamoDB runtime tables (runtime_enabled): the seven tables of modules/storage (DynamoContract.Tables,
# STORAGE.md "DynamoDB" and "Service state"), on demand, named <dynamodb.table-prefix><Table>.
# TTL on expiresAt for QuerySession, EvidenceBundle, and ServiceState; GSI evidenceById on
# EvidenceBundle. Default encryption (AWS owned key). Point-in-time recovery and deletion
# protection are course variables (off: the window is ephemeral and every loaded table is rebuilt
# from the write-once S3 item files).
terraform {
  required_providers {
    aws = {
      source = "hashicorp/aws"
    }
  }
}

variable "table_prefix" {
  type = string
}

variable "deletion_protection" {
  type = bool
}

variable "point_in_time_recovery" {
  type = bool
}

locals {
  # Logical name -> keys (modules/storage/src/main/scala/scholarchain/storage/Dynamo.scala).
  tables = {
    RunLedger             = { hash = "runId", range = "entryKey", ttl = false }
    EntityLinkCache       = { hash = "nameKey", range = "researcherId", ttl = false }
    QuerySession          = { hash = "sessionId", range = "turnKey", ttl = true }
    EvidenceBundle        = { hash = "queryId", range = "itemKey", ttl = true }
    ArtifactRegistry      = { hash = "artifactKey", range = "versionKey", ttl = false }
    ResearcherMetricCache = { hash = "researcherId", range = "metricKey", ttl = false }
    ServiceState          = { hash = "recordKey", range = "recordType", ttl = true }
  }
  evidence_index = "evidenceById" # DynamoContract.EvidenceByIdIndex
}

resource "aws_dynamodb_table" "this" {
  for_each = local.tables

  name                        = "${var.table_prefix}${each.key}"
  billing_mode                = "PAY_PER_REQUEST"
  hash_key                    = each.value.hash
  range_key                   = each.value.range
  deletion_protection_enabled = var.deletion_protection

  attribute {
    name = each.value.hash
    type = "S"
  }

  attribute {
    name = each.value.range
    type = "S"
  }

  dynamic "ttl" {
    for_each = each.value.ttl ? [1] : []
    content {
      attribute_name = "expiresAt"
      enabled        = true
    }
  }

  # evidenceById: hash itemKey, range queryId, projection ALL (STORAGE.md "Evidence by ID").
  dynamic "global_secondary_index" {
    for_each = each.key == "EvidenceBundle" ? [1] : []
    content {
      name            = local.evidence_index
      hash_key        = "itemKey"
      range_key       = "queryId"
      projection_type = "ALL"
    }
  }

  point_in_time_recovery {
    enabled = var.point_in_time_recovery
  }
}

output "table_names" {
  value = { for k, t in aws_dynamodb_table.this : k => t.name }
}

output "table_arns" {
  value = { for k, t in aws_dynamodb_table.this : k => t.arn }
}

output "evidence_index_arn" {
  value = "${aws_dynamodb_table.this["EvidenceBundle"].arn}/index/${local.evidence_index}"
}

===== UNTRACKED: infra/terraform/modules/ecs/main.tf
# ECS Fargate ARM64 runtime (runtime_enabled; D-10 item 7, ruling 1): one cluster, task definitions
# for query-api, graph-evidence-api, and worker from one image tag, one one-off Neptune loader task
# definition, three services (desired 1, bounded maximums), and the public ALB.
#
# Image entrypoint. The image keeps the sbt-native-packager default ENTRYPOINT
# (/opt/docker/bin/services, a launcher that needs -main <class> because the package has several
# main classes; Phase 10 ruling 18). An ECS `command` is appended to that entrypoint, so a command
# "bin/query-api" would run "bin/services bin/query-api" and stop with "You need to pass -main
# argument". Each task definition therefore sets entryPoint to its own start script
# (/opt/docker/bin/query-api, ...) and passes only arguments as the command. The image is unchanged.
#
# Credentials (STORAGE.md "Credentials on ECS"): every task definition sets taskRoleArn and sets no
# AWS_PROFILE, static key, session token, web identity, or AWS_ROLE_ARN variable; the Region comes
# from --region and SCHOLARCHAIN_AWS_REGION. Live mode starts without --profile (the post-fix
# launcher form; see infra/terraform/README.md, service-engineer dependency).
terraform {
  required_providers {
    aws = {
      source = "hashicorp/aws"
    }
  }
}

variable "name" {
  type = string
}

variable "region" {
  type = string
}

variable "vpc_id" {
  type = string
}

variable "public_subnet_ids" {
  type = list(string)
}

variable "security_group_ids" {
  description = "Runtime security groups by role (network module)."
  type        = map(string)
}

variable "repository_url" {
  type = string
}

variable "image_tag" {
  type = string
}

variable "task_sizes" {
  type = map(object({
    cpu    = number
    memory = number
  }))
}

variable "service_max_counts" {
  type = object({
    query_api          = number
    graph_evidence_api = number
    worker             = number
  })
}

variable "service_ports" {
  type = object({
    query_api          = number
    graph_evidence_api = number
  })
}

variable "execution_role_arns" {
  type = map(string)
}

variable "task_role_arns" {
  type = map(string)
}

variable "log_group_names" {
  type = map(string)
}

variable "api_key_secret_arn" {
  type = string
}

variable "runtime_args" {
  description = "Live-mode arguments shared by the three services (SERVICES.md section 5)."
  type = object({
    snapshot    = string
    graph_run   = string
    items_run   = string
    vectors_run = string
  })
}

variable "environment" {
  description = "SCHOLARCHAIN_* variables common to every task. Values must be non-empty (Phase 11 D12)."
  type        = map(string)
}

variable "window_summary_run" {
  type = string
}

locals {
  image = "${var.repository_url}:${var.image_tag}"

  args = [
    "--live",
    "--region", var.region,
    "--snapshot", var.runtime_args.snapshot,
    "--graph-run", var.runtime_args.graph_run,
    "--items-run", var.runtime_args.items_run,
    "--vectors-run", var.runtime_args.vectors_run,
  ]

  # query-api and the worker must compute the same runtime run ID (SERVICES.md section 5), so they
  # share exactly one argument list and one environment. Only they open the window-summary object.
  answering_env = merge(var.environment, { SCHOLARCHAIN_WINDOW_SUMMARY_RUN = var.window_summary_run })

  services = {
    query-api = {
      script = "query-api"
      env    = local.answering_env
      port   = var.service_ports.query_api
      max    = var.service_max_counts.query_api
      sg     = var.security_group_ids.query_api
    }
    graph-evidence-api = {
      script = "graph-evidence-api"
      env    = var.environment
      port   = var.service_ports.graph_evidence_api
      max    = var.service_max_counts.graph_evidence_api
      sg     = var.security_group_ids.graph_evidence_api
    }
    worker = {
      script = "worker"
      env    = local.answering_env
      port   = null
      max    = var.service_max_counts.worker
      sg     = var.security_group_ids.worker
    }
  }

  routed = {
    query-api = {
      port     = var.service_ports.query_api
      priority = 10
      paths    = ["/v1/query", "/v1/jobs/*", "/v1/explain/*"]
    }
    graph-evidence-api = {
      port     = var.service_ports.graph_evidence_api
      priority = 20
      paths    = ["/v1/evidence/*", "/v1/graph/*"]
    }
  }

  log_config = { for k, g in var.log_group_names : k => {
    logDriver = "awslogs"
    options = {
      awslogs-group         = g
      awslogs-region        = var.region
      awslogs-stream-prefix = k
    }
  } }
}

resource "aws_ecs_cluster" "this" {
  name = "${var.name}-runtime"

  # Container Insights adds per-metric charges; the AWS/ECS service metrics the alarms use are free.
  setting {
    name  = "containerInsights"
    value = "disabled"
  }
}

resource "aws_ecs_task_definition" "service" {
  for_each = local.services

  family                   = "${var.name}-${each.key}"
  requires_compatibilities = ["FARGATE"]
  network_mode             = "awsvpc"
  cpu                      = tostring(var.task_sizes[each.key].cpu)
  memory                   = tostring(var.task_sizes[each.key].memory)
  execution_role_arn       = var.execution_role_arns["services"]
  task_role_arn            = var.task_role_arns[each.key]

  runtime_platform {
    operating_system_family = "LINUX"
    cpu_architecture        = "ARM64"
  }

  container_definitions = jsonencode([{
    name         = each.key
    image        = local.image
    essential    = true
    entryPoint   = ["/opt/docker/bin/${each.value.script}"]
    command      = local.args
    stopTimeout  = 30
    portMappings = each.value.port == null ? [] : [{ containerPort = each.value.port, protocol = "tcp" }]
    environment  = [for k in sort(keys(each.value.env)) : { name = k, value = each.value.env[k] }]
    secrets = [{
      name      = "SCHOLARCHAIN_API_KEY"
      valueFrom = var.api_key_secret_arn
    }]
    logConfiguration = local.log_config[each.key]
  }])

  lifecycle {
    precondition {
      condition     = alltrue([for v in values(each.value.env) : trimspace(v) != ""])
      error_message = "Every SCHOLARCHAIN_* value must be non-empty (Phase 11 D12)."
    }
  }
}

# One-off Neptune loader (the in-VPC loader path). scripts/load-stores.sh runs it with
# `aws ecs run-task` and a command override. It reuses the services image and the query-api start
# script with -main, which the native-packager script honours, so LoadNeptuneCli runs with the same
# classpath and JVM options (MaxRAMPercentage 75: a 6 GiB heap in 8 GiB). No API key.
resource "aws_ecs_task_definition" "neptune_loader" {
  family                   = "${var.name}-neptune-loader"
  requires_compatibilities = ["FARGATE"]
  network_mode             = "awsvpc"
  cpu                      = tostring(var.task_sizes["neptune-loader"].cpu)
  memory                   = tostring(var.task_sizes["neptune-loader"].memory)
  execution_role_arn       = var.execution_role_arns["loader"]
  task_role_arn            = var.task_role_arns["neptune-loader"]

  runtime_platform {
    operating_system_family = "LINUX"
    cpu_architecture        = "ARM64"
  }

  container_definitions = jsonencode([{
    name             = "neptune-loader"
    image            = local.image
    essential        = true
    entryPoint       = ["/opt/docker/bin/query-api"]
    command          = ["-main", "scholarchain.storage.cli.LoadNeptuneCli"]
    stopTimeout      = 30
    environment      = [for k in sort(keys(var.environment)) : { name = k, value = var.environment[k] }]
    logConfiguration = local.log_config["neptune-loader"]
  }])
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# ALB (public, HTTP). HTTPS needs a domain and certificate, which the course window does not have;
# the API key therefore crosses the internet in the X-Api-Key header in clear text (README, risks).
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

resource "aws_lb" "this" {
  name                       = "${var.name}-api"
  load_balancer_type         = "application"
  internal                   = false
  subnets                    = var.public_subnet_ids
  security_groups            = [var.security_group_ids.alb]
  idle_timeout               = 60 # above the 40 s synchronous deadline
  drop_invalid_header_fields = true
  enable_deletion_protection = false
}

resource "aws_lb_target_group" "service" {
  for_each = local.routed

  name                 = substr("${var.name}-${each.key}", 0, 32)
  port                 = each.value.port
  protocol             = "HTTP"
  target_type          = "ip"
  vpc_id               = var.vpc_id
  deregistration_delay = 30

  # Liveness decides target health, so a downstream outage (which /readyz reports) does not make ECS
  # replace healthy tasks. /readyz is routed for the smoke test.
  health_check {
    path                = "/healthz"
    matcher             = "200"
    interval            = 15
    timeout             = 5
    healthy_threshold   = 2
    unhealthy_threshold = 3
  }
}

resource "aws_lb_listener" "http" {
  load_balancer_arn = aws_lb.this.arn
  port              = 80
  protocol          = "HTTP"

  default_action {
    type = "fixed-response"

    fixed_response {
      content_type = "application/json"
      message_body = "{\"code\":\"NOT_FOUND\",\"message\":\"no such route\"}"
      status_code  = "404"
    }
  }
}

resource "aws_lb_listener_rule" "service" {
  for_each = local.routed

  listener_arn = aws_lb_listener.http.arn
  priority     = each.value.priority

  action {
    type             = "forward"
    target_group_arn = aws_lb_target_group.service[each.key].arn
  }

  condition {
    path_pattern {
      values = each.value.paths
    }
  }
}

# Health and readiness of query-api through the ALB (smoke test). /metrics is not routed.
resource "aws_lb_listener_rule" "health" {
  listener_arn = aws_lb_listener.http.arn
  priority     = 30

  action {
    type             = "forward"
    target_group_arn = aws_lb_target_group.service["query-api"].arn
  }

  condition {
    path_pattern {
      values = ["/healthz", "/readyz"]
    }
  }
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Services: desired 1 each, in public subnets with a public IP (no NAT, ruling 1).
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

resource "aws_ecs_service" "this" {
  for_each = local.services

  name                               = each.key
  cluster                            = aws_ecs_cluster.this.id
  task_definition                    = aws_ecs_task_definition.service[each.key].arn
  desired_count                      = 1
  launch_type                        = "FARGATE"
  deployment_minimum_healthy_percent = 100
  deployment_maximum_percent         = 200
  health_check_grace_period_seconds  = contains(keys(local.routed), each.key) ? 120 : null
  enable_execute_command             = false
  propagate_tags                     = "SERVICE"

  network_configuration {
    subnets          = var.public_subnet_ids
    security_groups  = [each.value.sg]
    assign_public_ip = true
  }

  deployment_circuit_breaker {
    enable   = true
    rollback = true
  }

  dynamic "load_balancer" {
    for_each = contains(keys(local.routed), each.key) ? [1] : []
    content {
      target_group_arn = aws_lb_target_group.service[each.key].arn
      container_name   = each.key
      container_port   = each.value.port
    }
  }

  # Application Auto Scaling owns the running count between 1 and the service maximum.
  lifecycle {
    ignore_changes = [desired_count]
  }

  depends_on = [aws_lb_listener_rule.service]
}

resource "aws_appautoscaling_target" "this" {
  for_each = local.services

  service_namespace  = "ecs"
  resource_id        = "service/${aws_ecs_cluster.this.name}/${aws_ecs_service.this[each.key].name}"
  scalable_dimension = "ecs:service:DesiredCount"
  min_capacity       = 1
  max_capacity       = each.value.max
}

resource "aws_appautoscaling_policy" "cpu" {
  for_each = { for k, s in local.services : k => s if s.max > 1 }

  name               = "${var.name}-${each.key}-cpu"
  policy_type        = "TargetTrackingScaling"
  service_namespace  = aws_appautoscaling_target.this[each.key].service_namespace
  resource_id        = aws_appautoscaling_target.this[each.key].resource_id
  scalable_dimension = aws_appautoscaling_target.this[each.key].scalable_dimension

  target_tracking_scaling_policy_configuration {
    target_value       = 60
    scale_in_cooldown  = 120
    scale_out_cooldown = 60

    predefined_metric_specification {
      predefined_metric_type = "ECSServiceAverageCPUUtilization"
    }
  }
}

output "cluster_name" {
  value = aws_ecs_cluster.this.name
}

output "service_names" {
  value = { for k, s in aws_ecs_service.this : k => s.name }
}

output "task_definition_arns" {
  value = merge(
    { for k, t in aws_ecs_task_definition.service : k => t.arn },
    { neptune-loader = aws_ecs_task_definition.neptune_loader.arn },

  )
}

===== UNTRACKED: infra/terraform/modules/emr/main.tf
# EMR support (ruling 2): the service role, the EC2 instance role and profile, the EMR-managed
# security groups, and the log location. No cluster: scripts/run-bounded-pipeline.sh launches each
# run as an ephemeral cluster (release, instance type, and counts from this module's outputs) that
# terminates after its steps.
terraform {
  required_providers {
    aws = {
      source = "hashicorp/aws"
    }
  }
}

variable "name" {
  type = string
}

variable "account_id" {
  type = string
}

variable "region" {
  type = string
}

variable "partition" {
  type = string
}

variable "vpc_id" {
  type = string
}

variable "data_bucket_arn" {
  type = string
}

variable "data_bucket_name" {
  type = string
}

locals {
  emr_tag = { "for-use-with-amazon-emr-managed-policies" = "true" }
  b       = var.data_bucket_arn
  # Only what the steps touch (code review 1 F1): run-bounded-pipeline.sh and emr/step.sh read raw
  # inputs, job jars and step.sh under artifacts/jobs/, and the Hadoop run under curated/hadoop; the
  # jobs write curated/hadoop and curated/spark; EMR pushes logs to logs/emr/. No graph/,
  # semantic-context/, runs/, or reports/ grant: StoreConversion and the loaders never run on EMR.
  read_prefixes = ["raw/", "curated/", "artifacts/"]
  # DeleteObject is needed by the s3a committers (temporary and rename files); versioning keeps
  # every overwritten or deleted version.
  write_prefixes = ["curated/", "logs/emr/"]
  # ListBucket only under the prefixes the role reads or writes (s3:prefix condition).
  list_prefixes = distinct(concat(local.read_prefixes, local.write_prefixes))
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Service role
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

data "aws_iam_policy_document" "service_trust" {
  statement {
    actions = ["sts:AssumeRole"]

    principals {
      type        = "Service"
      identifiers = ["elasticmapreduce.amazonaws.com"]
    }

    condition {
      test     = "StringEquals"
      variable = "aws:SourceAccount"
      values   = [var.account_id]
    }

    condition {
      test     = "ArnLike"
      variable = "aws:SourceArn"
      values   = ["arn:${var.partition}:elasticmapreduce:${var.region}:${var.account_id}:*"]
    }
  }
}

resource "aws_iam_role" "service" {
  name               = "${var.name}-emr-service"
  description        = "EMR service role for ephemeral ScholarChain clusters"
  assume_role_policy = data.aws_iam_policy_document.service_trust.json
}

# AWS-managed v2 service policy: its EC2 actions are limited to resources tagged
# for-use-with-amazon-emr-managed-policies=true (the VPC, public subnets, and EMR groups here).
resource "aws_iam_role_policy_attachment" "service_v2" {
  role       = aws_iam_role.service.name
  policy_arn = "arn:${var.partition}:iam::aws:policy/service-role/AmazonEMRServicePolicy_v2"
}

# The v2 policy passes only the default instance role name; a custom one needs this grant.
data "aws_iam_policy_document" "service_pass_role" {
  statement {
    actions   = ["iam:PassRole"]
    resources = [aws_iam_role.instance.arn]

    condition {
      test     = "StringEquals"
      variable = "iam:PassedToService"
      values   = ["ec2.amazonaws.com"]
    }
  }
}

data "aws_iam_policy_document" "instance_s3" {
  statement {
    sid       = "BucketLevel"
    actions   = ["s3:GetBucketLocation", "s3:ListBucketMultipartUploads"]
    resources = [local.b]
  }

  statement {
    sid       = "ListOwnPrefixes"
    actions   = ["s3:ListBucket"]
    resources = [local.b]

    condition {
      test     = "StringLike"
      variable = "s3:prefix"
      values   = [for p in local.list_prefixes : "${p}*"]
    }
  }

  statement {
    sid       = "ReadInputs"
    actions   = ["s3:GetObject"]
    resources = [for p in local.read_prefixes : "${local.b}/${p}*"]
  }

  statement {
    sid = "WriteOutputs"
    actions = [
      "s3:PutObject",
      "s3:DeleteObject",
      "s3:AbortMultipartUpload",
      "s3:ListMultipartUploadParts",
    ]
    resources = [for p in local.write_prefixes : "${local.b}/${p}*"]
  }
}

  name   = "data-bucket"
  role   = aws_iam_role.instance.id
  policy = data.aws_iam_policy_document.instance_s3.json
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# EMR-managed security groups (public subnets). EMR adds and maintains the ingress rules between the
# primary and core nodes itself, so Terraform ignores rule drift and revokes them on delete. Terraform
# therefore can neither show nor enforce the ingress EMR adds (code review 1 F7c). Terraform itself
# creates no ingress rule, and EMR block public access (account default, on) should stop any
# internet ingress, but that is unverified until the first cluster runs: run-bounded-pipeline.sh
# describes both groups after each cluster and records and warns about any 0.0.0.0/0 or ::/0 ingress.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

resource "aws_security_group" "primary" {
  name                   = "${var.name}-emr-primary"
  description            = "EMR-managed group for the primary node"
  vpc_id                 = var.vpc_id
  revoke_rules_on_delete = true
  tags                   = merge(local.emr_tag, { Name = "${var.name}-emr-primary" })

  lifecycle {
    ignore_changes = [ingress, egress]
  }
}

resource "aws_security_group" "core" {
  name                   = "${var.name}-emr-core"
  description            = "EMR-managed group for core nodes"
  vpc_id                 = var.vpc_id
  revoke_rules_on_delete = true
  tags                   = merge(local.emr_tag, { Name = "${var.name}-emr-core" })

  lifecycle {
    ignore_changes = [ingress, egress]
  }
}

# Without NAT, nodes reach S3 (gateway endpoint), the EMR service, and package repositories over
# their public IPs. Outbound only.
resource "aws_vpc_security_group_egress_rule" "emr_all" {
  for_each          = { primary = aws_security_group.primary.id, core = aws_security_group.core.id }
  security_group_id = each.value
  description       = "Outbound to AWS endpoints"
  cidr_ipv4         = "0.0.0.0/0"
  ip_protocol       = "-1"
}

output "service_role_name" {
  value = aws_iam_role.service.name
}

output "service_role_arn" {
  value = aws_iam_role.service.arn
}

output "instance_role_arn" {
  value = aws_iam_role.instance.arn
}

output "instance_profile_name" {
  value = aws_iam_instance_profile.instance.name
}

output "primary_security_group_id" {
  value = aws_security_group.primary.id
}

output "core_security_group_id" {
  value = aws_security_group.core.id
}

output "log_uri" {
  description = "EMR LogUri (expires with the logs/ lifecycle rule)."
  value       = "s3://${var.data_bucket_name}/logs/emr/"
}

===== UNTRACKED: infra/terraform/modules/iam/main.tf
# ECS runtime IAM (runtime_enabled; modules/services/SERVICES.md section 4, STORAGE.md). Task
# execution roles (image pull, logs, the API key secret) are separate from task roles (what the
# code calls). Every statement names its actions and resources; none uses a wildcard action. The
# runtime graph grant is neptune-db:ReadDataViaQuery only. EMR roles live in the emr module, the
# CodeBuild role in the build module, and the Neptune bulk-load role in the neptune module.
terraform {
  required_providers {
    aws = {
      source = "hashicorp/aws"
    }
  }
}

variable "name" {
  type = string
}

variable "account_id" {
  type = string
}

variable "region" {
  type = string
}

variable "partition" {
  type = string
}

variable "data_bucket_arn" {
  type = string
}

variable "table_arns" {
  description = "Logical table name -> ARN."
  type        = map(string)
}

variable "evidence_index_arn" {
  type = string
}

variable "queue_arn" {
  type = string
}

variable "api_key_secret_arn" {
  type = string
}

variable "neptune_cluster_resource_id" {
  type = string
}

variable "vector_index_arns" {
  type = map(string)
}

variable "ecr_repository_arn" {
  type = string
}

variable "log_group_arns" {
  description = "Task definition name -> log group ARN."
  type        = map(string)
}

variable "embedding_model_id" {
  type = string
}

variable "haiku_inference_profile_id" {
  type = string
}

variable "haiku_destination_regions" {
  type = list(string)
}

locals {
  b       = var.data_bucket_arn
  p       = var.partition
  neptune = "arn:${local.p}:neptune-db:${var.region}:${var.account_id}:${var.neptune_cluster_resource_id}/*"

  haiku_profile_arn = "arn:${local.p}:bedrock:${var.region}:${var.account_id}:inference-profile/${var.haiku_inference_profile_id}"
  haiku_model_arns = [
    for r in var.haiku_destination_regions :
    "arn:${local.p}:bedrock:${r}::foundation-model/${trimprefix(var.haiku_inference_profile_id, "us.")}"
  ]
  titan_arn = "arn:${local.p}:bedrock:${var.region}::foundation-model/${var.embedding_model_id}"

  # Tables the rag runtime reads (RUNTIME.md section 8) and writes create-only (QuerySession,
  # EvidenceBundle), plus ServiceState (jobs and idempotency keys).
  runtime_read_tables  = ["EntityLinkCache", "ResearcherMetricCache", "ArtifactRegistry", "QuerySession", "EvidenceBundle"]
  runtime_write_tables = ["QuerySession", "EvidenceBundle"]

  task_roles = toset(["query-api", "graph-evidence-api", "worker", "neptune-loader"])
}

data "aws_iam_policy_document" "ecs_trust" {
  statement {
    actions = ["sts:AssumeRole"]

    principals {
      type        = "Service"
      identifiers = ["ecs-tasks.amazonaws.com"]
    }

    condition {
      test     = "StringEquals"
      variable = "aws:SourceAccount"
      values   = [var.account_id]
    }

    condition {
      test     = "ArnLike"
      variable = "aws:SourceArn"
      values   = ["arn:${local.p}:ecs:${var.region}:${var.account_id}:*"]
    }
  }
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Task execution roles: the services role reads the API key secret; the loader role does not.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

data "aws_iam_policy_document" "execution" {
  for_each = toset(["services", "loader"])

  statement {
    sid       = "EcrLogin"
    actions   = ["ecr:GetAuthorizationToken"]
    resources = ["*"]
  }

  statement {
    sid       = "EcrPull"
    actions   = ["ecr:BatchCheckLayerAvailability", "ecr:BatchGetImage", "ecr:GetDownloadUrlForLayer"]
    resources = [var.ecr_repository_arn]
  }

  statement {
    sid     = "Logs"
    actions = ["logs:CreateLogStream", "logs:PutLogEvents"]
    resources = [
      for k, arn in var.log_group_arns : "${arn}:*"
      if(each.key == "loader") == (k == "neptune-loader")
    ]
  }

  dynamic "statement" {
    for_each = each.key == "services" ? [1] : []
    content {
      sid       = "ApiKeySecret"
      actions   = ["secretsmanager:GetSecretValue"]
      resources = [var.api_key_secret_arn]
    }
  }
}

resource "aws_iam_role" "execution" {
  for_each           = toset(["services", "loader"])
  name               = "${var.name}-ecs-exec-${each.key}"
  description        = "ECS task execution role (${each.key}): image pull, logs${each.key == "services" ? ", API key secret" : ""}"
  assume_role_policy = data.aws_iam_policy_document.ecs_trust.json
}

resource "aws_iam_role_policy" "execution" {
  for_each = toset(["services", "loader"])
  name     = "execution"
  role     = aws_iam_role.execution[each.key].id
  policy   = data.aws_iam_policy_document.execution[each.key].json
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Task roles
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

# query-api and the worker run the rag workflow: identical store, vector, and model grants.
data "aws_iam_policy_document" "answering" {
  # Unconditioned on purpose: S3 answers a GET of a missing key with 404 only to a caller that holds
  # s3:ListBucket on the bucket; otherwise 403, which the services map to 502 and count against the s3
  # breaker (SERVICES.md section 4: the readiness probe and unknown query IDs read missing keys).
  statement {
    sid       = "ListDataBucket"
    actions   = ["s3:ListBucket"]
    resources = [local.b]
  }

  statement {
    sid     = "ReadRuntimeObjects"
    actions = ["s3:GetObject"]
    resources = [
      "${local.b}/logs/rag-runtime/*",
      "${local.b}/reports/rag-runtime/*",
      "${local.b}/curated/window-summary/*",
      "${local.b}/curated/coauthor-evidence/*",
      "${local.b}/semantic-context/text/*",
    ]
  }

  statement {
    sid     = "WriteRuntimeRecords"
    actions = ["s3:PutObject"]
    resources = [
      "${local.b}/logs/rag-runtime/*",
      "${local.b}/reports/rag-runtime/*",
    ]
  }

  statement {
    sid       = "ReadStoreTables"
    actions   = ["dynamodb:GetItem", "dynamodb:Query"]
    resources = [for t in local.runtime_read_tables : var.table_arns[t]]
  }

  statement {
    sid       = "CreateRuntimeRecords"
    actions   = ["dynamodb:PutItem"]
    resources = [for t in local.runtime_write_tables : var.table_arns[t]]
  }

  statement {
    sid       = "ServiceState"
    actions   = ["dynamodb:GetItem", "dynamodb:PutItem"]
    resources = [var.table_arns["ServiceState"]]
  }

  statement {
    sid       = "GraphRead"
    actions   = ["neptune-db:ReadDataViaQuery"]
    resources = [local.neptune]
  }

  statement {
    sid       = "VectorQuery"
    actions   = ["s3vectors:QueryVectors", "s3vectors:GetVectors"]
    resources = values(var.vector_index_arns)
  }

  statement {
    sid       = "EmbedQuestion"
    actions   = ["bedrock:InvokeModel"]
    resources = [local.titan_arn]
  }

  statement {
    sid       = "HaikuProfile"
    actions   = ["bedrock:InvokeModel"]
    resources = [local.haiku_profile_arn]
  }

  # The US profile routes to the foundation model in its destination Regions; allowed only through
  # that profile.
  statement {
    sid       = "HaikuThroughProfile"
    actions   = ["bedrock:InvokeModel"]
    resources = local.haiku_model_arns

    condition {
      test     = "StringEquals"
      variable = "bedrock:InferenceProfileArn"
      values   = [local.haiku_profile_arn]
    }
  }
}

data "aws_iam_policy_document" "query_api_queue" {
  statement {
    sid       = "SendJobs"
    actions   = ["sqs:SendMessage"]
    resources = [var.queue_arn]
  }
}

data "aws_iam_policy_document" "worker_queue" {
  statement {
    sid       = "ConsumeJobs"
    actions   = ["sqs:ReceiveMessage", "sqs:DeleteMessage", "sqs:ChangeMessageVisibility"]
    resources = [var.queue_arn]
  }
}

# graph-evidence-api: evidence by ID (GSI), the payload objects it names, and graph reads. No
# window-summary, vector, model, queue, or ServiceState grant (SERVICES.md section 4).
data "aws_iam_policy_document" "graph_evidence_api" {
  statement {
    sid       = "EvidenceById"
    actions   = ["dynamodb:Query"]
    resources = [var.evidence_index_arn]
  }

  statement {
    sid       = "EvidencePayloads"
    actions   = ["s3:GetObject"]
    resources = ["${local.b}/logs/rag-runtime/*"]
  }

  statement {
    sid       = "GraphRead"
    actions   = ["neptune-db:ReadDataViaQuery"]
    resources = [local.neptune]
  }
}

# One-off Neptune loader task (LoadNeptuneCli in the VPC): read the store-conversion manifest, the
# graph CSV, and the coauthor evidence objects; start a bulk load and read its status. The bulk load
# itself reads S3 with the neptune module's bulk-load role.
data "aws_iam_policy_document" "neptune_loader" {
  statement {
    sid       = "ListLoadInputs"
    actions   = ["s3:ListBucket"]
    resources = [local.b]

    condition {
      test     = "StringLike"
      variable = "s3:prefix"
      values   = ["graph/neptune/*", "curated/coauthor-evidence/*", "runs/store-conversion/*"]
    }
  }

  statement {
    sid     = "ReadLoadInputs"
    actions = ["s3:GetObject"]
    resources = [
      "${local.b}/graph/neptune/*",
      "${local.b}/curated/coauthor-evidence/*",
      "${local.b}/runs/store-conversion/*",
    ]
  }

  statement {
    sid       = "BulkLoad"
    actions   = ["neptune-db:StartLoaderJob", "neptune-db:GetLoaderJobStatus"]
    resources = [local.neptune]
  }
}

data "aws_iam_policy_document" "task" {
  for_each = local.task_roles

  source_policy_documents = compact([
    contains(["query-api", "worker"], each.key) ? data.aws_iam_policy_document.answering.json : "",
    each.key == "query-api" ? data.aws_iam_policy_document.query_api_queue.json : "",
    each.key == "worker" ? data.aws_iam_policy_document.worker_queue.json : "",
    each.key == "graph-evidence-api" ? data.aws_iam_policy_document.graph_evidence_api.json : "",
    each.key == "neptune-loader" ? data.aws_iam_policy_document.neptune_loader.json : "",
  ])
}

resource "aws_iam_role" "task" {
  for_each           = local.task_roles
  name               = "${var.name}-task-${each.key}"
  description        = "ECS task role: ${each.key}"
  assume_role_policy = data.aws_iam_policy_document.ecs_trust.json
}

resource "aws_iam_role_policy" "task" {
  for_each = local.task_roles
  name     = "runtime"
  role     = aws_iam_role.task[each.key].id
  policy   = data.aws_iam_policy_document.task[each.key].json
}

output "execution_role_arns" {
  description = "services (query-api, graph-evidence-api, worker) and loader."
  value       = { for k, r in aws_iam_role.execution : k => r.arn }
}

output "task_role_arns" {
  value = { for k, r in aws_iam_role.task : k => r.arn }
}

===== UNTRACKED: infra/terraform/modules/messaging/main.tf
# Job queue (runtime_enabled; STORAGE.md "Job queue", core services.queue): a standard queue with a
# 300 s visibility timeout (services.worker.visibility-timeout-seconds) and SQS-managed SSE, redriving
# to a dead-letter queue after 3 receives (services.queue.max-receive-count). The DLQ keeps messages
# longer (14 days) than the source queue (4 days).
terraform {
  required_providers {
    aws = {
      source = "hashicorp/aws"
    }
  }
}

variable "name" {
  type = string
}

variable "max_receive_count" {
  type = number
}

resource "aws_sqs_queue" "dlq" {
  name                      = "${var.name}-jobs-dlq"
  message_retention_seconds = 1209600
  sqs_managed_sse_enabled   = true
}

resource "aws_sqs_queue" "jobs" {
  name                       = "${var.name}-jobs"
  visibility_timeout_seconds = 300
  message_retention_seconds  = 345600
  receive_wait_time_seconds  = 20
  sqs_managed_sse_enabled    = true

  redrive_policy = jsonencode({
    deadLetterTargetArn = aws_sqs_queue.dlq.arn
    maxReceiveCount     = var.max_receive_count
  })
}

# Only the job queue may redrive into the DLQ.
resource "aws_sqs_queue_redrive_allow_policy" "dlq" {
  queue_url = aws_sqs_queue.dlq.id
  redrive_allow_policy = jsonencode({
    redrivePermission = "byQueue"
    sourceQueueArns   = [aws_sqs_queue.jobs.arn]
  })
}

output "queue_url" {
  value = aws_sqs_queue.jobs.url
}

output "queue_arn" {
  value = aws_sqs_queue.jobs.arn
}

output "dlq_url" {
  value = aws_sqs_queue.dlq.url
}

output "dlq_arn" {
  value = aws_sqs_queue.dlq.arn
}

output "dlq_name" {
  value = aws_sqs_queue.dlq.name
}

resource "aws_neptune_cluster_parameter_group" "this" {

  family      = var.parameter_group_family
  description = "ScholarChain Neptune cluster parameters"

  # The storage templates add USING QUERY:TIMEOUTMILLISECONDS (neptune.query-timeout-ms, 5 s); the
  # effective timeout is the smaller of that hint and this cluster bound (STORAGE.md).

    name  = "neptune_query_timeout"

  port                                 = var.port
  neptune_subnet_group_name            = aws_neptune_subnet_group.this.name
  neptune_cluster_parameter_group_name = aws_neptune_cluster_parameter_group.this.name
  vpc_security_group_ids               = [var.security_group_id]
  iam_database_authentication_enabled  = true
  storage_encrypted                    = true
  iam_roles                            = [aws_iam_role.bulk_loader.arn]
  backup_retention_period              = 1
  preferred_backup_window              = "07:00-08:00"
  deletion_protection                  = var.deletion_protection
  skip_final_snapshot                  = var.skip_final_snapshot
  final_snapshot_identifier            = var.skip_final_snapshot ? null : "${var.name}-graph-final"
  copy_tags_to_snapshot                = true
  apply_immediately                    = true
}

resource "aws_neptune_cluster_instance" "this" {
  identifier                   = "${var.name}-graph-1"
  cluster_identifier           = aws_neptune_cluster.this.id
  engine                       = "neptune"
  instance_class               = var.instance_class
  neptune_parameter_group_name = aws_neptune_parameter_group.this.name
  # Engine version is pinned by the cluster; no minor upgrade during the window.
  auto_minor_version_upgrade = false
  apply_immediately          = true
}

output "cluster_id" {
  value = aws_neptune_cluster.this.id
}

output "endpoint" {
  description = "Cluster (writer) endpoint: SCHOLARCHAIN_NEPTUNE_ENDPOINT."
  value       = aws_neptune_cluster.this.endpoint
}

output "port" {
  value = aws_neptune_cluster.this.port
}

output "cluster_resource_id" {
  value = aws_neptune_cluster.this.cluster_resource_id
}

output "bulk_loader_role_arn" {

  value       = aws_iam_role.bulk_loader.arn
}

===== UNTRACKED: infra/terraform/modules/network/main.tf
# Network (ruling 1, D-10 item 8): a two-AZ VPC. Public subnets hold the ALB, the ECS tasks (public
# IP, no NAT gateway) and the EMR nodes; private subnets hold Neptune only. S3 and DynamoDB gateway
# endpoints serve both route tables. Runtime security groups (runtime_enabled) are chained by role:
# only the ALB listener accepts a CIDR. EMR security groups live in the emr module.
terraform {
  required_providers {
    aws = {
      source = "hashicorp/aws"
    }
  }
}

variable "name" {
  type = string
}

variable "region" {
  type = string
}

variable "vpc_cidr" {
  type = string
}

variable "azs" {
  type = list(string)
}

variable "runtime_enabled" {
  type = bool
}

variable "alb_ingress_cidr_blocks" {
  type = list(string)
}

variable "service_ports" {
  description = "Container ports: query-api and graph-evidence-api (core services.*.port)."
  type = object({
    query_api          = number
    graph_evidence_api = number
  })
}

variable "neptune_port" {
  type = number
}

locals {
  # Tag the AWS-managed EMR service policy (AmazonEMRServicePolicy_v2) requires on the VPC
  # resources EMR may use.
  emr_tag = { "for-use-with-amazon-emr-managed-policies" = "true" }
  rt      = var.runtime_enabled ? 1 : 0
}

resource "aws_vpc" "this" {
  cidr_block           = var.vpc_cidr
  enable_dns_support   = true
  enable_dns_hostnames = true

  tags = merge(local.emr_tag, { Name = "${var.name}-vpc" })
}

# The default security group is left with no rules, so nothing can use it by accident.
resource "aws_default_security_group" "this" {
  vpc_id = aws_vpc.this.id
  tags   = { Name = "${var.name}-default-closed" }
}

resource "aws_internet_gateway" "this" {
  vpc_id = aws_vpc.this.id
  tags   = { Name = "${var.name}-igw" }
}

resource "aws_subnet" "public" {
  count = 2

  vpc_id            = aws_vpc.this.id
  availability_zone = var.azs[count.index]
  cidr_block        = cidrsubnet(var.vpc_cidr, 4, count.index)
  # No NAT gateway (ruling 1): EMR nodes need public IPs to reach AWS endpoints. Security groups
  # admit no inbound traffic from the internet except to the ALB.
  map_public_ip_on_launch = true

  tags = merge(local.emr_tag, { Name = "${var.name}-public-${var.azs[count.index]}", Tier = "public" })
}

resource "aws_subnet" "private" {
  count = 2

  vpc_id            = aws_vpc.this.id
  availability_zone = var.azs[count.index]
  cidr_block        = cidrsubnet(var.vpc_cidr, 4, count.index + 8)

  tags = { Name = "${var.name}-private-${var.azs[count.index]}", Tier = "private" }
}

resource "aws_route_table" "public" {
  vpc_id = aws_vpc.this.id
  tags   = { Name = "${var.name}-public" }
}

resource "aws_route" "public_internet" {
  route_table_id         = aws_route_table.public.id
  destination_cidr_block = "0.0.0.0/0"
  gateway_id             = aws_internet_gateway.this.id
}

# Private route table: no route to the internet; only the VPC and the gateway endpoints.
resource "aws_route_table" "private" {
  vpc_id = aws_vpc.this.id
  tags   = { Name = "${var.name}-private" }
}

resource "aws_route_table_association" "public" {
  count          = 2
  subnet_id      = aws_subnet.public[count.index].id
  route_table_id = aws_route_table.public.id
}

resource "aws_route_table_association" "private" {
  count          = 2
  subnet_id      = aws_subnet.private[count.index].id
  route_table_id = aws_route_table.private.id
}

# Gateway endpoints (no hourly charge). The Neptune bulk loader reaches S3 only through this
# endpoint. The default (full access) endpoint policy is kept: ECR image layers and EMR packages
# come from AWS-owned buckets, and IAM scopes every role to its own resources.
resource "aws_vpc_endpoint" "s3" {
  vpc_id            = aws_vpc.this.id
  service_name      = "com.amazonaws.${var.region}.s3"
  vpc_endpoint_type = "Gateway"
  route_table_ids   = [aws_route_table.public.id, aws_route_table.private.id]
  tags              = { Name = "${var.name}-s3" }
}

resource "aws_vpc_endpoint" "dynamodb" {
  vpc_id            = aws_vpc.this.id
  service_name      = "com.amazonaws.${var.region}.dynamodb"
  vpc_endpoint_type = "Gateway"
  route_table_ids   = [aws_route_table.public.id, aws_route_table.private.id]
  tags              = { Name = "${var.name}-dynamodb" }
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Runtime security groups (runtime_enabled). Each role has its own group.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

resource "aws_security_group" "alb" {
  count       = local.rt
  name        = "${var.name}-alb"
  description = "Public ALB: HTTP listener only"
  vpc_id      = aws_vpc.this.id
  tags        = { Name = "${var.name}-alb" }
}

resource "aws_security_group" "query_api" {
  count       = local.rt
  name        = "${var.name}-query-api"
  description = "query-api tasks: from the ALB only"
  vpc_id      = aws_vpc.this.id
  tags        = { Name = "${var.name}-query-api" }
}

  count       = local.rt
  name        = "${var.name}-graph-evidence-api"
  description = "graph-evidence-api tasks: from the ALB only"
  vpc_id      = aws_vpc.this.id
  tags        = { Name = "${var.name}-graph-evidence-api" }
}

resource "aws_security_group" "worker" {
  count       = local.rt
  name        = "${var.name}-worker"
  description = "worker tasks: no inbound traffic"
  vpc_id      = aws_vpc.this.id
  tags        = { Name = "${var.name}-worker" }
}

resource "aws_security_group" "loader" {
  count       = local.rt
  name        = "${var.name}-neptune-loader"
  description = "One-off Neptune loader tasks: no inbound traffic"
  vpc_id      = aws_vpc.this.id
  tags        = { Name = "${var.name}-neptune-loader" }
}

resource "aws_security_group" "neptune" {
  count       = local.rt
  name        = "${var.name}-neptune"
  description = "Neptune: from the service and loader groups only"
  vpc_id      = aws_vpc.this.id
  tags        = { Name = "${var.name}-neptune" }
}

resource "aws_vpc_security_group_ingress_rule" "alb_http" {
  for_each          = var.runtime_enabled ? toset(var.alb_ingress_cidr_blocks) : toset([])
  security_group_id = aws_security_group.alb[0].id
  description       = "HTTP listener"
  cidr_ipv4         = each.value
  ip_protocol       = "tcp"
  from_port         = 80
  to_port           = 80
}

resource "aws_vpc_security_group_egress_rule" "alb_to_query_api" {
  count                        = local.rt
  security_group_id            = aws_security_group.alb[0].id
  description                  = "To query-api targets"
  referenced_security_group_id = aws_security_group.query_api[0].id
  ip_protocol                  = "tcp"
  from_port                    = var.service_ports.query_api
  to_port                      = var.service_ports.query_api
}

resource "aws_vpc_security_group_egress_rule" "alb_to_graph_evidence_api" {
  count                        = local.rt
  security_group_id            = aws_security_group.alb[0].id
  description                  = "To graph-evidence-api targets"
  referenced_security_group_id = aws_security_group.graph_evidence_api[0].id
  ip_protocol                  = "tcp"
  from_port                    = var.service_ports.graph_evidence_api
  to_port                      = var.service_ports.graph_evidence_api
}

resource "aws_vpc_security_group_ingress_rule" "query_api_from_alb" {
  count                        = local.rt
  security_group_id            = aws_security_group.query_api[0].id
  description                  = "From the ALB"
  referenced_security_group_id = aws_security_group.alb[0].id
  ip_protocol                  = "tcp"
  from_port                    = var.service_ports.query_api
  to_port                      = var.service_ports.query_api
}

resource "aws_vpc_security_group_ingress_rule" "graph_evidence_api_from_alb" {
  count                        = local.rt
  security_group_id            = aws_security_group.graph_evidence_api[0].id
  description                  = "From the ALB"
  referenced_security_group_id = aws_security_group.alb[0].id
  ip_protocol                  = "tcp"
  from_port                    = var.service_ports.graph_evidence_api
  to_port                      = var.service_ports.graph_evidence_api
}

locals {
  # Neptune clients: every task group that reads the graph or starts a bulk load.
  neptune_clients = var.runtime_enabled ? {
    query_api          = aws_security_group.query_api[0].id
    graph_evidence_api = aws_security_group.graph_evidence_api[0].id
    worker             = aws_security_group.worker[0].id
    loader             = aws_security_group.loader[0].id
  } : {}
}

# Tasks run in public subnets without NAT or interface endpoints, so AWS APIs (ECR, CloudWatch Logs,
# Secrets Manager, SQS, Bedrock, S3 Vectors) are reached over HTTPS on public endpoints. S3 and
# DynamoDB traffic stays on the gateway endpoints.
resource "aws_vpc_security_group_egress_rule" "clients_https" {
  for_each          = local.neptune_clients
  security_group_id = each.value
  description       = "HTTPS to AWS service endpoints"
  cidr_ipv4         = "0.0.0.0/0"
  ip_protocol       = "tcp"
  from_port         = 443
  to_port           = 443
}

resource "aws_vpc_security_group_egress_rule" "clients_to_neptune" {
  for_each                     = local.neptune_clients
  security_group_id            = each.value
  description                  = "To Neptune"
  referenced_security_group_id = aws_security_group.neptune[0].id
  ip_protocol                  = "tcp"
  from_port                    = var.neptune_port
  to_port                      = var.neptune_port
}

resource "aws_vpc_security_group_ingress_rule" "neptune_from_clients" {
  for_each                     = local.neptune_clients
  security_group_id            = aws_security_group.neptune[0].id
  description                  = "From ${each.key}"
  referenced_security_group_id = each.value
  ip_protocol                  = "tcp"
  from_port                    = var.neptune_port
  to_port                      = var.neptune_port
}

# The bulk loader reads graph/ from S3 through the gateway endpoint.
resource "aws_vpc_security_group_egress_rule" "neptune_to_s3" {
  count             = local.rt
  security_group_id = aws_security_group.neptune[0].id
  description       = "Bulk loader to S3 (gateway endpoint)"
  prefix_list_id    = aws_vpc_endpoint.s3.prefix_list_id
  ip_protocol       = "tcp"
  from_port         = 443
  to_port           = 443
}

output "vpc_id" {
  value = aws_vpc.this.id
}

output "public_subnet_ids" {
  value = aws_subnet.public[*].id
}

output "private_subnet_ids" {
  value = aws_subnet.private[*].id
}

output "s3_prefix_list_id" {
  value = aws_vpc_endpoint.s3.prefix_list_id
}

output "security_group_ids" {
  description = "Runtime security groups by role (null when runtime_enabled is false)."
  value = var.runtime_enabled ? {
    alb                = aws_security_group.alb[0].id
    query_api          = aws_security_group.query_api[0].id
    graph_evidence_api = aws_security_group.graph_evidence_api[0].id
    worker             = aws_security_group.worker[0].id
    loader             = aws_security_group.loader[0].id
    neptune            = aws_security_group.neptune[0].id
  } : null
}

===== UNTRACKED: infra/terraform/modules/observability/main.tf
# Observability. Always: one CloudWatch log group per ECS task definition (14-day retention), so the
# window 1 plan has them (ruling 2). runtime_enabled only: the alarms (ALB 5xx, unhealthy targets,
# DLQ depth above 0, ECS CPU, Neptune CPU), one small dashboard, and an SNS topic when an alarm email
# is given (the email comes from a variable with no default).
terraform {
  required_providers {
    aws = {
      source = "hashicorp/aws"
    }
  }
}

variable "name" {
  type = string
}

variable "region" {
  type = string
}

variable "runtime_enabled" {
  type = bool
}

variable "log_retention_days" {
  type = number
}

variable "alarm_email" {
  type = string
}

variable "alb_5xx_threshold" {
  type = number
}

variable "cpu_alarm_threshold" {
  type = number
}

variable "alb_arn_suffix" {
  type    = string
  default = null
}

variable "target_group_arn_suffixes" {
  description = "Service name -> target group ARN suffix."
  type        = map(string)
  default     = null
}

variable "cluster_name" {
  type    = string
  default = null
}

variable "service_names" {
  description = "Service key -> ECS service name."
  type        = map(string)
  default     = null
}

variable "dlq_name" {
  type    = string
  default = null
}

variable "neptune_cluster_id" {
  type    = string
  default = null
}

locals {
  task_definitions = toset(["query-api", "graph-evidence-api", "worker", "neptune-loader"])
  rt               = var.runtime_enabled ? 1 : 0
  notify           = var.runtime_enabled && var.alarm_email != ""
  actions          = local.notify ? [aws_sns_topic.alarms[0].arn] : []
  services         = var.runtime_enabled ? var.service_names : {}
  target_groups    = var.runtime_enabled ? var.target_group_arn_suffixes : {}
}

resource "aws_cloudwatch_log_group" "ecs" {
  for_each          = local.task_definitions
  name              = "/scholarchain/${var.name}/ecs/${each.key}"
  retention_in_days = var.log_retention_days
}

# Alarm notifications carry no data. The topic keeps the default (unencrypted) setting because
# CloudWatch cannot publish to a topic encrypted with the AWS-managed aws/sns key, and ruling 1
# allows no customer-managed key.
resource "aws_sns_topic" "alarms" {
  count = local.notify ? 1 : 0
  name  = "${var.name}-alarms"
}

resource "aws_sns_topic_subscription" "email" {
  count     = local.notify ? 1 : 0
  topic_arn = aws_sns_topic.alarms[0].arn
  protocol  = "email"
  endpoint  = var.alarm_email
}

resource "aws_cloudwatch_metric_alarm" "alb_5xx" {
  for_each = var.runtime_enabled ? {
    target = "HTTPCode_Target_5XX_Count"
    elb    = "HTTPCode_ELB_5XX_Count"
  } : {}

  alarm_name          = "${var.name}-alb-${each.key}-5xx"
  alarm_description   = "ALB ${each.value} at or above ${var.alb_5xx_threshold} in 5 minutes"
  namespace           = "AWS/ApplicationELB"
  metric_name         = each.value
  dimensions          = { LoadBalancer = var.alb_arn_suffix }
  statistic           = "Sum"
  period              = 300
  evaluation_periods  = 1
  threshold           = var.alb_5xx_threshold
  comparison_operator = "GreaterThanOrEqualToThreshold"
  treat_missing_data  = "notBreaching"
  alarm_actions       = local.actions
  ok_actions          = local.actions
}

resource "aws_cloudwatch_metric_alarm" "unhealthy_targets" {
  for_each = local.target_groups

  alarm_name          = "${var.name}-${each.key}-unhealthy-targets"
  alarm_description   = "${each.key}: at least one unhealthy target for 2 minutes"
  namespace           = "AWS/ApplicationELB"
  metric_name         = "UnHealthyHostCount"
  dimensions          = { LoadBalancer = var.alb_arn_suffix, TargetGroup = each.value }
  statistic           = "Maximum"
  period              = 60
  evaluation_periods  = 2
  threshold           = 0
  comparison_operator = "GreaterThanThreshold"
  treat_missing_data  = "notBreaching"
  alarm_actions       = local.actions
  ok_actions          = local.actions
}

resource "aws_cloudwatch_metric_alarm" "dlq_depth" {
  count = local.rt

  alarm_name          = "${var.name}-jobs-dlq-not-empty"
  alarm_description   = "Job dead-letter queue holds a message"
  namespace           = "AWS/SQS"
  metric_name         = "ApproximateNumberOfMessagesVisible"
  dimensions          = { QueueName = var.dlq_name }
  statistic           = "Maximum"
  period              = 300
  evaluation_periods  = 1
  threshold           = 0
  comparison_operator = "GreaterThanThreshold"
  treat_missing_data  = "notBreaching"
  alarm_actions       = local.actions
  ok_actions          = local.actions
}

resource "aws_cloudwatch_metric_alarm" "ecs_cpu" {
  for_each = local.services

  alarm_name          = "${var.name}-${each.key}-cpu"
  alarm_description   = "${each.key}: average CPU at or above ${var.cpu_alarm_threshold}% for 10 minutes"
  namespace           = "AWS/ECS"
  metric_name         = "CPUUtilization"
  dimensions          = { ClusterName = var.cluster_name, ServiceName = each.value }
  statistic           = "Average"
  period              = 300
  evaluation_periods  = 2
  threshold           = var.cpu_alarm_threshold
  comparison_operator = "GreaterThanOrEqualToThreshold"
  treat_missing_data  = "notBreaching"
  alarm_actions       = local.actions
  ok_actions          = local.actions
}

resource "aws_cloudwatch_metric_alarm" "neptune_cpu" {
  count = local.rt

  alarm_name          = "${var.name}-neptune-cpu"
  alarm_description   = "Neptune: average CPU at or above ${var.cpu_alarm_threshold}% for 10 minutes"
  namespace           = "AWS/Neptune"
  metric_name         = "CPUUtilization"
  dimensions          = { DBClusterIdentifier = var.neptune_cluster_id }
  statistic           = "Average"
  period              = 300
  evaluation_periods  = 2
  threshold           = var.cpu_alarm_threshold
  comparison_operator = "GreaterThanOrEqualToThreshold"
  treat_missing_data  = "notBreaching"
  alarm_actions       = local.actions
  ok_actions          = local.actions
}

resource "aws_cloudwatch_dashboard" "runtime" {
  count          = local.rt
  dashboard_name = "${var.name}-runtime"

  dashboard_body = jsonencode({
    widgets = [
      {
        type = "metric", x = 0, y = 0, width = 12, height = 6
        properties = {
          title  = "ALB requests and 5xx"
          region = var.region
          stat   = "Sum"
          period = 60
          metrics = [
            ["AWS/ApplicationELB", "RequestCount", "LoadBalancer", var.alb_arn_suffix],
            ["AWS/ApplicationELB", "HTTPCode_Target_5XX_Count", "LoadBalancer", var.alb_arn_suffix],
            ["AWS/ApplicationELB", "HTTPCode_ELB_5XX_Count", "LoadBalancer", var.alb_arn_suffix],
          ]
        }
      },
      {
        type = "metric", x = 12, y = 0, width = 12, height = 6
        properties = {
          title  = "ALB target response time (p50, p95, p99)"
          region = var.region
          period = 60
          metrics = [
            ["AWS/ApplicationELB", "TargetResponseTime", "LoadBalancer", var.alb_arn_suffix, { stat = "p50" }],
            ["AWS/ApplicationELB", "TargetResponseTime", "LoadBalancer", var.alb_arn_suffix, { stat = "p95" }],
            ["AWS/ApplicationELB", "TargetResponseTime", "LoadBalancer", var.alb_arn_suffix, { stat = "p99" }],
          ]
        }
      },
      {
        type = "metric", x = 0, y = 6, width = 12, height = 6
        properties = {
          title   = "ECS CPU by service"
          region  = var.region
          stat    = "Average"
          period  = 60
          metrics = [for k, s in local.services : ["AWS/ECS", "CPUUtilization", "ClusterName", var.cluster_name, "ServiceName", s]]
        }
      },
      {
        type = "metric", x = 12, y = 6, width = 12, height = 6
        properties = {
          title  = "Healthy targets, job DLQ depth, Neptune CPU"
          region = var.region
          stat   = "Maximum"
          period = 60
          metrics = concat(
            [for k, tg in local.target_groups : ["AWS/ApplicationELB", "HealthyHostCount", "TargetGroup", tg, "LoadBalancer", var.alb_arn_suffix]],
            [
              ["AWS/SQS", "ApproximateNumberOfMessagesVisible", "QueueName", var.dlq_name],
              ["AWS/Neptune", "CPUUtilization", "DBClusterIdentifier", var.neptune_cluster_id],
            ]
          )
        }
      },
    ]
  })
}

output "log_group_names" {
  value = { for k, g in aws_cloudwatch_log_group.ecs : k => g.name }
}

output "log_group_arns" {
  value = { for k, g in aws_cloudwatch_log_group.ecs : k => g.arn }
}

output "dashboard_name" {
  value = var.runtime_enabled ? aws_cloudwatch_dashboard.runtime[0].dashboard_name : null
}

===== UNTRACKED: infra/terraform/modules/vectors/main.tf
# S3 Vectors (runtime_enabled): one vector bucket (SCHOLARCHAIN_VECTOR_BUCKET) and the five indexes of
# the storage contract (core s3-vectors.indexes; STORAGE.md "S3 Vectors"): float32, 1024 dimensions,
# cosine. The non-filterable keys vectorKey, textHash, and textRef are fixed at creation and cannot
# change later. Encryption: SSE-S3 (AES256), the service default.
terraform {
  required_providers {
    aws = {
      source = "hashicorp/aws"
    }
  }
}

variable "name" {
  type = string
}

variable "account_id" {
  type = string
}

locals {
  indexes = toset([
    "researcher-profile-v1",
    "collaboration-community-v1",
    "paper-abstract-v1",
    "collaboration-path-v1",
    "topic-institution-summary-v1",
  ])
}

resource "aws_s3vectors_vector_bucket" "this" {
  vector_bucket_name = "${var.name}-vectors-${var.account_id}"
  # Indexes are Terraform resources, so destroy removes them first; no force_destroy needed.
  force_destroy = false

  encryption_configuration {
    sse_type = "AES256"
  }
}

resource "aws_s3vectors_index" "this" {
  for_each = local.indexes

  vector_bucket_name = aws_s3vectors_vector_bucket.this.vector_bucket_name
  index_name         = each.value
  data_type          = "float32"
  dimension          = 1024
  distance_metric    = "cosine"

  metadata_configuration {
    non_filterable_metadata_keys = ["vectorKey", "textHash", "textRef"]
  }
}

output "vector_bucket_name" {
  value = aws_s3vectors_vector_bucket.this.vector_bucket_name
}

output "vector_bucket_arn" {
  value = aws_s3vectors_vector_bucket.this.vector_bucket_arn
}

output "index_arns" {
  value = { for k, i in aws_s3vectors_index.this : k => i.index_arn }
}

===== UNTRACKED: modules/services/src/test/scala/scholarchain/services/LauncherSpec.scala
package scholarchain.services

import org.scalatest.funsuite.AnyFunSuite
import scholarchain.core.config.ConfigLoader
import scholarchain.storage.live.LiveTarget

/** Phase 12 work order 6: live mode without --profile (an ECS task) selects the SDK default
  * credentials chain. The arguments and variable names are those of the ECS task definitions
  * (infra/terraform/modules/ecs/main.tf locals.args, envs/course/main.tf module "ecs"); the values
  * are non-placeholder stand-ins. Nothing here builds a client or calls AWS.
  */
class LauncherSpec extends AnyFunSuite:
  private val ecsArgs = Seq(
    "--live",
    "--region",
    "us-east-2",
    "--snapshot",
    "2026-10-03",
    "--graph-run",
    "run-" + "a" * 32,
    "--items-run",
    "run-" + "b" * 32,
    "--vectors-run",
    "run-" + "c" * 32
  )
  private val haiku = "us.anthropic.claude-haiku-4-5-20251001-v1:0"
  private val ecsEnv = Map(
    "SCHOLARCHAIN_ENVIRONMENT" -> "course",
    "SCHOLARCHAIN_AWS_REGION" -> "us-east-2",
    "SCHOLARCHAIN_AWS_ACCOUNT_ID" -> "123456789012",
    "SCHOLARCHAIN_BUCKET" -> "scholarchain-course-data-123456789012",
    "SCHOLARCHAIN_NEPTUNE_ENDPOINT" -> "sc.cluster-x.us-east-2.neptune.amazonaws.com",
    "SCHOLARCHAIN_NEPTUNE_PORT" -> "8182",
    "SCHOLARCHAIN_NEPTUNE_LOADER_ROLE_ARN" -> "arn:aws:iam::123456789012:role/sc-neptune-loader",
    "SCHOLARCHAIN_DYNAMODB_TABLE_PREFIX" -> "scholarchain-course-",
    "SCHOLARCHAIN_VECTOR_BUCKET" -> "scholarchain-course-vectors",
    "SCHOLARCHAIN_EMBEDDING_MODEL_ID" -> "amazon.titan-embed-text-v2:0",
    "SCHOLARCHAIN_PLANNER_MODEL_ID" -> haiku,
    "SCHOLARCHAIN_SYNTHESIS_MODEL_ID" -> haiku,
    "SCHOLARCHAIN_VERIFICATION_MODEL_ID" -> haiku,
    "SCHOLARCHAIN_ADJUDICATION_MODEL_ID" -> haiku,
    "SCHOLARCHAIN_PROMPTS_DIR" -> "/opt/docker/prompts",
    "SCHOLARCHAIN_STUB_LLM" -> "false",
    "SCHOLARCHAIN_JOB_QUEUE_URL" -> "https://sqs.us-east-2.amazonaws.com/123456789012/sc-jobs",
    "SCHOLARCHAIN_JOB_DLQ_URL" -> "https://sqs.us-east-2.amazonaws.com/123456789012/sc-jobs-dlq",
    "SCHOLARCHAIN_JOB_MAX_RECEIVE_COUNT" -> "3",
    "SCHOLARCHAIN_LLM_REQUESTS_PER_MINUTE" -> "4"
  )

  test("ECS form: --live without --profile gives profileName None; --profile x gives Some(x)") {
    // query-api and worker add the window-summary run; graph-evidence-api does not (ruling 14a).
    Seq(ecsEnv, ecsEnv + ("SCHOLARCHAIN_WINDOW_SUMMARY_RUN" -> ("run-" + "d" * 32))).foreach {
      env =>
        val cfg = ConfigLoader.load(env).fold(e => fail(e.mkString("; ")), identity)
        assert(cfg.runtime.llm.requestsPerMinute == 4 && !cfg.services.stubLlm)
        val a = Launcher.parse(ecsArgs).fold(fail(_), identity)
        val t = Launcher.liveTarget(a, cfg)
        assert(t == LiveTarget(None, "us-east-2"))
        assert(t.profile == LiveTarget.DefaultChainLabel)
        val named = Launcher.parse(ecsArgs ++ Seq("--profile", "x")).fold(fail(_), identity)
        assert(Launcher.liveTarget(named, cfg).profileName == Some("x"))
    }
    val cfg = ConfigLoader.load(ecsEnv + ("SCHOLARCHAIN_AWS_REGION" -> "eu-west-1")).toOption.get
    // No --region: the core application.region.
    val noRegion = Launcher.parse(Seq("--live")).fold(fail(_), identity)
    assert(Launcher.liveTarget(noRegion, cfg) == LiveTarget(None, "eu-west-1"))
  }

  test("--live alone is no longer refused for --profile; the next missing argument is named") {
    val cfg = ConfigLoader.load(ecsEnv).fold(e => fail(e.mkString("; ")), identity)
    val a = Launcher.parse(Seq("--live")).fold(fail(_), identity)
    val out = scala.collection.mutable.ArrayBuffer.empty[String]
    assert(Launcher.backend(a, cfg, out += _) == Left("missing --snapshot"))
    assert(out.isEmpty)
    assert(Launcher.Usage.contains("--live [--profile <name>]"))
  }

===== UNTRACKED: scripts/README.md
# ScholarChain operational scripts

Owner: infra-engineer (authoring only). Spec Phases 12 and 13, D-10, D-13, and the Phase 12 human
rulings 1 to 21 (`docs/agent-runs/phase-12/prompt.md`). The infra-engineer never runs a script against
AWS. Who runs each script is set by `docs/agents.md` section 8 and is repeated below.

## Conventions (every script)

- `set -euo pipefail`, bash 3.2 compatible (macOS), no interactive prompts: every choice is a flag.
- `--help` prints the full contract. `--dry-run` checks flags and local inputs, prints the steps, and
  makes no AWS, Terraform, git, or sbt call.
- Step markers: `==> STEP <name>`, `==> OK ...`, `==> FAIL ... (exit n)`, `==> WARN ...`, plus one
  result line per script (`==> PLAN`, `==> RELEASE`, `==> PACKAGE`, `==> CLUSTER`, `==> PIPELINE`,
  `==> SMOKE`, `==> ROLLBACK`, `==> STORES`, `==> EXPERIMENT`, `==> ARCHIVE`, `==> CLEANUP-PLAN`).
- Exit codes: 0 success; 1 a gate or verification failed; 2 usage error or missing tool;
  3 precondition (dirty tree, wrong account or Region, missing input or record); 4 approval mismatch
  (plan checksum); 5 waiting for a documented human action; 6 cloud-operation timeout.
- AWS: `--profile` (default `$AWS_PROFILE`) and `--region` (default and only allowed value
  `us-east-2`, D-04). Every AWS-calling script prints `==> TARGET account=... region=... profile=...`
  before acting, and checks that the Terraform state belongs to the same account.
- Every AWS CLI call has `--cli-connect-timeout 10 --cli-read-timeout 120`, including those in
  `emr/step.sh` and the step bootstrap that `run-bounded-pipeline.sh` submits. Reads and idempotent
  writes (same key and bytes, same desired state) use SDK standard retries (5 attempts). Calls that are
  not idempotent (create a cluster, start a build, run a task, register a task definition revision,
  force a new ECS deployment) make exactly one attempt. Long waits poll with a deadline (exit 6).
- No secret value is in source, flags, or output. The API key is read only by `smoke-test.sh`, into a
  variable, and reaches curl on standard input.
- Dirty tree: any change outside `operations/` and `experiments/results/` (the record trees the
  scripts themselves write), untracked files included, stops `plan.sh`, `deploy.sh`, `package.sh`,
  `run-bounded-pipeline.sh`, `load-stores.sh`, `run-experiment.sh`, and `cleanup.sh` (exit 3).
- sbt always runs as `SPARK_LOCAL_IP=127.0.0.1 sbt -J-Xmx6g -batch ...`, and a script refuses to start
  sbt while another sbt process is running.
- Helpers live in `scripts/lib/common.sh`. `scripts/emr/step.sh` runs on the EMR primary node only.

## Where records go

| Record | Path | Committed? |
|---|---|---|
| Plan file, `plan.json`, `plan.txt` | `scratch/plans/<UTC stamp>-<commit 12>/` | no (`scratch/` is gitignored; `*.tfplan` too) |
| Plan summary (secret-free) | `operations/plans/<UTC stamp>-<commit 12>.md` | evidence |
| Package record | `operations/releases/package-<commit>.json` | evidence |
| Release, smoke, rollback records | `operations/releases/{release,smoke,rollback}-*.json` | evidence |
| Pipeline run records | `operations/pipeline-runs/<stamp>-fraction<p>/` | evidence |
| Store-load records | `operations/pipeline-runs/store-load-<stamp>/` | evidence |
| Experiment raw results | `experiments/results/<experimentId>/<stamp>/` (read-only after the run) | evidence |
| Cleanup plans and records | `scratch/plans/cleanup-*/`, `operations/cleanup/cleanup-*/` | plans no, records evidence |

`operations/**` belongs to the deploy-engineer and `experiments/**` to the experiment-engineer
(`docs/agents.md`); the scripts write there when those agents or the human run them. No new
`.gitignore` line is needed: plan files land under the ignored `scratch/`. Logs the scripts write
under `operations/` end in `.txt`, never `.log`, because `.gitignore` ignores `*.log`.

## The flow

```text
Cloud window 1 (runtime_enabled = false)
  human:            TF_VAR_alarm_email=... scripts/plan.sh --runtime-enabled false      -> sign the sha256
  deploy-engineer:  scripts/deploy.sh --plan-file <plan> --approved-plan-sha256 <sha>
  deploy-engineer:  scripts/package.sh                                                   -> package record
  deploy-engineer:  scripts/run-bounded-pipeline.sh --fraction 100 --package-record <rec> (then 50, 25)
Cloud window 2 (runtime_enabled = true; image_tag and the three runs committed in course.tfvars)
  human:            scripts/plan.sh --runtime-enabled true                               -> sign the sha256
  deploy-engineer:  scripts/deploy.sh --plan-file <plan> --approved-plan-sha256 <sha> --package-record <rec>
                    -> exit 5 until the human sets the API key secret value; then the same command + --resume
  human:            scripts/load-stores.sh --store-run <run> --vectors-run <run>
  deploy-engineer:  scripts/smoke-test.sh
  experiment-engineer: scripts/run-experiment.sh --manifest experiments/<m>.json
  human or deploy-engineer: scripts/archive-evidence.sh --out-dir <dir> [--metrics-from/--metrics-to]
```
