```text
  human:            scripts/cleanup.sh --stage runtime   (then --plan-file ... --confirm <sha>)
After the evidence is archived: cleanup.sh --stage infra; after the instructor approves the archive: --stage final
```

## check.sh

- **Purpose:** every non-cloud gate of a checkout (spec Phase 13 item 1).
- **Command:** `scripts/check.sh` (only `--help` besides).
- **Gates:** jq parses `.claude/settings.json` and `.mcp.json`; `.claude/hooks/validate-agent-files.sh`
  (agent files and hook self-tests); `bash -n` on `.claude/hooks/*.sh`, `scripts/**/*.sh`, and
  `datasets/scripts/*.sh`; `shellcheck -S error` when installed (otherwise reported SKIPPED);
  `bash datasets/scripts/validate.sh` (dataset manifest, samples, checksums, and the dataset script
  self-tests; run through `bash` because those files are not executable); `terraform fmt -check -recursive
  infra/terraform`; `terraform init -backend=false -lockfile=readonly` and `terraform validate`;
  `sbt check` (scalafmt check, Test/compile, every test, markTestRun). All gates run; exit 1 if any failed.
- **Environment:** none. Network: the first terraform init downloads the pinned provider; sbt resolves
  pinned artifacts. No AWS credentials.
- **Runs it:** anyone, including agents (ruling 5). **Approval points:** none.
- **Evidence:** its output (step markers per gate). **Rollback:** not applicable.

## package.sh

- **Purpose:** job jars and the services image for one commit (spec Phase 13 item 2, ruling 3).
- **Command:** `scripts/package.sh [--profile p] [--region us-east-2] [--record-dir operations/releases] [--build-timeout-minutes 30] [--dry-run]`
- **Steps:** clean tree; `sbt packageJobs`, jar sha256 values, Hadoop `Main-Class` must be
  `scholarchain.hadoop.Pipeline`; `SCHOLARCHAIN_IMAGE_TAG=<commit> sbt services/Docker/stage` (no
  Docker), zip of the staged context with the Dockerfile at its root; write-once uploads to
  `artifacts/jobs/<commit>/` (jars, `emr/step.sh`) and `artifacts/codebuild/source/<commit>.zip`; one
  CodeBuild build with `IMAGE_TAG=<commit>` unless ECR already holds that tag (tags are immutable);
  digest and scan-on-push status from ECR.
- **Environment:** `AWS_PROFILE`. **Runs it:** the human or deploy-engineer after cloud window 1 is
  applied. **Approval points:** none beyond the applied window 1 plan (ECR and CodeBuild come from it).
- **Evidence:** `operations/releases/package-<commit>.json` (commit, jar keys and sha256 values, Docker
  context sha256, repository, tag, digest, build ID, scan status).
- **Rollback:** none needed: artifacts are additive and keyed by commit.

## plan.sh

- **Purpose:** the Terraform plan of the course environment; never applies (spec Phase 13 item 3).
- **Command:** `TF_VAR_alarm_email=<address or empty> scripts/plan.sh --runtime-enabled true|false [--var-file infra/terraform/envs/course/course.tfvars] [--profile p] [--region us-east-2] [--out-dir scratch/plans] [--evidence-dir operations/plans] [--dry-run]`
- **Steps:** terraform 1.16.6; clean tree; account and Region; `terraform init -input=false
  -lockfile=readonly` (local backend); `terraform plan -input=false -var-file=... -var
  runtime_enabled=... -var region=... -out=plan.tfplan`, printed in full and saved as `plan.txt`;
  `terraform show -json` (mode 600); sha256; the plan's account must equal the profile's.
- **Environment:** `AWS_PROFILE` (the human's `scholarchain-sdk`, which Terraform reads through its
  `credential_process`), `TF_VAR_alarm_email` (required by the configuration; never printed by the
  script, though the plan output shows it).
- **Runs it:** the human architect at the plan gate. **Approval points:** the human reviews the plan
  and signs its sha256; that checksum is the only approval `deploy.sh` accepts.
- **Evidence:** in `scratch/plans/<stamp>-<sha12>/`: `plan.tfplan`, `plan.sha256`, `plan.txt`,
  `plan.json`, `plan-meta.json` (commit, account, Region, runtime_enabled, var file and lock file
  sha256, counts), `summary.md` (counts; stateful destroys or replacements first; IAM changes;
  high-cost resources); `summary.md` is copied to `operations/plans/`.
- **Rollback:** not applicable (nothing changes).

## deploy.sh

- **Purpose:** apply exactly one approved saved plan and verify the deployed revision (ruling 6).
- **Command:** `scripts/deploy.sh --plan-file scratch/plans/<dir>/plan.tfplan --approved-plan-sha256 <sha> [--package-record operations/releases/package-<sha>.json] [--profile p] [--region us-east-2] [--release-dir operations/releases] [--stable-timeout-minutes 20] [--resume] [--dry-run]`
- **Gates:** sha256 of the plan file must equal `--approved-plan-sha256` (else exit 4, nothing
  applied); clean tree; HEAD, account, and Region equal the plan's (`plan-meta.json`); `terraform apply
  <plan file>` (a saved plan needs no prompt; `-auto-approve` is never used; no other input is
  accepted). With runtime_enabled: the API key secret must have a version (DescribeSecret; the value
  is never read), else exit 5; `--resume` (no apply) forces a new deployment of all three services;
  bounded wait until every service has one COMPLETED deployment and running = desired; every running
  task's image digest equals the package record's, and its task definition names
  `<repository>:<package tag>`; query-api and the worker log the same runtime run ID.
- **Environment:** `AWS_PROFILE`. **Runs it:** deploy-engineer (`/deploy`). **Approval points:** the
  human's signed plan checksum and approved environment (escalations 4 and 5); the human sets the API
  key secret value between the window 2 apply and `--resume`.
- **Evidence:** `operations/releases/release-<stamp>-<sha12>.json` (commit, plan sha256, account,
  Region, image tag and digest, per-service task definition ARN and running digest, runtime run IDs),
  `.apply.txt` (full apply output), `.outputs.json` (terraform outputs; no secret values).
- **Rollback:** a failing ECS deployment is rolled back by the deployment circuit breaker; otherwise
  `rollback.sh` to the previous release record. Terraform changes are not reverted automatically;
  a revert is a new plan through `plan.sh`.

## rollback.sh

- **Purpose:** move the three services back to the image of a known-good release record, with no
  Terraform apply.
- **Command:** `scripts/rollback.sh --to-release operations/releases/release-<stamp>-<sha12>.json [--reason "<text>"] [--profile p] [--region us-east-2] [--stable-timeout-minutes 20] [--record-dir operations/releases] [--dry-run]`; `scripts/rollback.sh --list` (local records only).
- **Steps:** the record must be a runtime release of this account and Region; ECR must hold its tag
  with its digest; for query-api, graph-evidence-api, and the worker together: copy the current task
  definition, change only the image, register a new revision (one attempt), update the service;
  bounded stable wait; digest and runtime run ID checks.
- **Environment:** `AWS_PROFILE`. **Runs it:** deploy-engineer for its own failing in-flight
  deployment; the incident-responder for a live revision (`/rollback`). **Approval points:** the human
  approves each AWS mutation at the permission prompt.
- **Evidence:** `operations/releases/rollback-<stamp>.json` (previous and new task definitions, image,
  reason, verified digests, run IDs).
- **Rollback:** roll forward with `rollback.sh` to a newer release record, or plan and deploy.
  Terraform state still names the newer image until `image_tag` in `course.tfvars` is changed and
  applied; the next plan shows that drift.

## smoke-test.sh

- **Purpose:** prove the deployed runtime answers a real bounded collaboration query with evidence
  (spec Phase 13 item 5).
- **Command:** `scripts/smoke-test.sh [--profile p] [--region us-east-2] [--base-url http://<alb>] [--question "<text>"] [--record-dir operations/releases] [--dry-run]`
- **Checks:** `/healthz` 200 ok; `/readyz` 200 ready; sync `POST /v1/query` (default: benchmark B07,
  "Have Jack J. Dongarra and Yves Robert coauthored a paper?") with a fresh Idempotency-Key returns 200,
  a `qry-` ID, and a claim with an evidence ID; `GET /v1/evidence/<id>` returns that evidence; the same
  POST and key replay the same queryId and answerHash; a POST without `X-Api-Key` is 401 UNAUTHORIZED.
  GETs are retried; POSTs are not.
- **Environment:** `AWS_PROFILE`. **Runs it:** deploy-engineer after `deploy.sh`; the
  incident-responder after a rollback. **Approval points:** none (read-only apart from one query,
  bounded by the 8 Haiku calls per minute cap).
- **Evidence:** `operations/releases/smoke-<stamp>.json` and the response bodies in
  `operations/releases/smoke-<stamp>/` (no key; answers and evidence only).
- **Rollback:** none; a failure sends the deploy-engineer to the rollback gate.

## run-bounded-pipeline.sh

- **Purpose:** one bounded Hadoop and Spark run on an ephemeral EMR cluster (spec Phase 13 item 6, D-13
  size scaling).
- **Command:** `scripts/run-bounded-pipeline.sh --fraction 25|50|100 --package-record operations/releases/package-<sha>.json [--reducers 2] [--shuffle-partitions 16] [--hadoop-timeout-minutes 90] [--spark-timeout-minutes 150] [--record-dir operations/pipeline-runs] [--profile p] [--region us-east-2] [--dry-run]`
- **Steps:** clean tree, HEAD = the package commit, local jars and `emr/step.sh` hash to the record;
  ruling 2 bounds re-checked from the `emr` output (emr-spark-8.0.0, 1 primary, 1 to 2 core, Graviton,
  on-demand); write-once uploads of the five inputs to
  `raw/<dataset>/snapshot=<d>/run=<input run>/` (input run = hash of the fraction and the file hashes)
  and of the jars; one `create-cluster` (no retry, `--auto-terminate`, tag
  `for-use-with-amazon-emr-managed-policies=true` that the v2 EMR service policy requires) with three
  steps through `command-runner.jar` and `emr/step.sh`:
  1. `classpath-probe` (CONTINUE): Java, Hadoop, Spark versions and the Typesafe Config, AWS SDK v2,
     and Scala entries on the cluster classpath (the Phase 11 check);
  2. `hadoop-pipeline` (TERMINATE_CLUSTER): `hadoop jar` with Main-Class `scholarchain.hadoop.Pipeline`,
     which runs J01, J02, J03+J04, J05 p0..p2, J06, J10, J07+J08, J09 in the documented order with
     `--out s3a://<bucket>/curated/hadoop --code-version <commit> --dblp ... --dblp-dtd ...
     --dblp-snapshot 2026-10-03 --openalex ... --crossref ... --orcid ... (snapshot 2026-11-03)
     --reducers <n>`, killed at its timeout;
  3. `spark-pipeline` (TERMINATE_CLUSTER): finds the one new Hadoop run directory, then
     `spark-submit --master yarn --class scholarchain.spark.PipelineJob` (jobs 1 to 11) with
     `--input <that run> --out s3a://<bucket>/curated/spark --checkpoint-dir
     hdfs:///scholarchain/spark/checkpoints --code-version <commit> --shuffle-partitions <n>`, killed at
     its timeout.
  Then a bounded wait for TERMINATED; collection of step timings, normalized instance hours, the new
  run directories and their `_manifest.json`, and the step logs (bounded wait for EMR to push them);
  then a read-only `ec2 describe-security-groups` of both EMR-managed groups. Terraform ignores their
  rule drift, so this is the only check that EMR added no `0.0.0.0/0` or `::/0` ingress: any such rule
  is printed as `==> WARN EMR GROUP OPEN TO THE INTERNET ...` and recorded; the script changes no rule.
- **Inputs:** fraction 100 uses the full local subset (`scratch/datasets/dblp/snapshot=2026-10-03/full/works.xml`,
  `raw/dblp.dtd`, `scratch/datasets/{openalex,crossref}/full/works.jsonl`,
  `scratch/datasets/orcid/full/records.jsonl.gz`). Fractions 25 and 50 read
  `scratch/datasets/fraction=<p>/{dblp/works.xml,openalex/works.jsonl,crossref/works.jsonl,orcid/records.jsonl.gz}`,
  written by `datasets/scripts/make_fraction.sh` (rule fraction-v1, nested 25 within 50 within 100;
  `datasets/schemas/fractions.md`); a missing file stops the script with exit 3. Every resolved input
  path is printed with its size and sha256 (step `inputs`, also in `--dry-run`).
- **Environment:** `AWS_PROFILE`. EMR jobs use the core configuration defaults (environment `local`
  semantics for thresholds and windows); paths are arguments.
- **Runs it:** deploy-engineer (`/run-pipeline`) or the human. **Approval points:** the applied window 1
  plan and the bounds in it; anything beyond them is a human-gated scale change.
- **Evidence:** `operations/pipeline-runs/<stamp>-fraction<p>/`: `run.json` (commit and code version,
  fraction, snapshots, input keys with sha256 and bytes, input run, configuration, cluster ID, final
  state and reason, step names, states and timestamps, normalized instance hours, output run
  directories, `emrSecurityGroups` with any open ingress), `steps.json` as submitted, `cluster.json`,
  `steps-result.json`, `security-groups.json`, `input-hashes.txt`, `manifests/`, `logs/`.
- **Rollback:** nothing to roll back: outputs are new write-once run directories and the cluster
  terminates itself. On timeout (exit 6) the cluster ID is printed; terminating a stuck cluster is a
  human action.

## load-stores.sh

- **Purpose:** the cloud window 2 store loads, in order: S3, Neptune, DynamoDB, S3 Vectors.
- **Command:** `scripts/load-stores.sh --store-run run-<32 hex> --vectors-run run-<32 hex> [--root scratch/stores/s3] [--vectors-root <root>] [--window-summary-run run-6fbad498beb0d8f570df67665fb97544] [--window-summary-root scratch/runtime/wo8/s3] [--dynamo-concurrency 8] [--neptune-timeout-minutes 60] [--record-dir operations/pipeline-runs] [--profile p] [--region us-east-2] [--dry-run]`
- **Steps:** clean tree; account and Region; runtime outputs present; the laptop loaders get exactly the
  tasks' `SCHOLARCHAIN_*` variables from the `runtime_environment` output.
  1. `LoadS3Cli --manifest <m> --root <root> --live --profile <p> --region <r>` for the store-conversion
     run, the embedding run (vectors backup and rendered text), and the window-summary load run.
  2. Neptune through the in-VPC path: `aws ecs run-task` of `scholarchain-course-neptune-loader` with
     the command `-main scholarchain.storage.cli.LoadNeptuneCli --live --stage-from-bucket --dir
     /tmp/stores/s3/graph/neptune/snapshot=2026-10-03/run=<store-run> --out /tmp/stores` (task role, no
     profile; the loader role ARN comes from `SCHOLARCHAIN_NEPTUNE_LOADER_ROLE_ARN`), the load ID from the
     task log, then a `--live --status <loadId>` task every 2 minutes until `LOAD_COMPLETED` (bounded).
  3. `LoadDynamoCli --dir <root>/curated/dynamodb/snapshot=2026-10-03/run=<store-run> --live --profile <p> --region <r> --concurrency <n>` (about 876k conditional puts).
  4. `LoadVectorsCli --input <vectors-root>/vectors/backup/snapshot=2026-10-03/run=<vectors-run> --live --profile <p> --region <r>`.
- **Environment:** `AWS_PROFILE` (LiveAws reads the named profile). **Runs it:** the human (the storage
  live loaders are human-run, storage rulings 1 and 26). **Approval points:** the applied window 2 plan.
- **Evidence:** `operations/pipeline-runs/store-load-<stamp>/`: one log per loader as `<step>.txt`
  (each prints its counts), the Neptune task logs as `neptune-<start|status>-<task>.txt`, `run.json` (runs, Neptune load ID, per-step status and seconds).
- **Rollback:** none automatic: every load is write-once and a rerun skips loaded data. Removing loaded
  data is a human-gated destructive action; ending window 2 destroys the stores.

## run-experiment.sh

- **Purpose:** run one approved, committed experiment manifest (spec Phase 13 item 7). No manifest is
  written in Phase 12.
- **Command:** `scripts/run-experiment.sh --manifest experiments/<path>.json [--results-root experiments/results] [--profile p] [--region us-east-2] [--dry-run]`
- **Manifest (`experiment-manifest-v1`, JSON):** `experimentId`, `description`, `command` (first element
  a tracked executable under `experiments/` or `load-tests/`), `limits` (`maxMinutes` 1 to 240 is
  enforced; every limit is recorded), `metrics` (CloudWatch namespace, metric, dimensions).
- **Gates:** the manifest is tracked and identical to HEAD; clean tree; the command is tracked; account
  and Region printed; the command is stopped at `maxMinutes` (TERM, then KILL after 30 s).
- **Environment:** `AWS_PROFILE`; the command receives `SCHOLARCHAIN_EXPERIMENT_ID`,
  `SCHOLARCHAIN_EXPERIMENT_DIR`, `AWS_PROFILE`, `AWS_REGION`.
- **Runs it:** experiment-engineer (`/experiment`). **Approval points:** the human approves and commits
  the manifest before the run.
- **Evidence:** `experiments/results/<experimentId>/<stamp>/`: `raw/`, `stdout.log`, `stderr.log`,
  `run-record.json` (commit, manifest and its sha256, limits, start and end, exit code, account,
  Region, metric locations), made read-only.
- **Rollback:** none; the experiment's command must stay inside the approved bounds.

## archive-evidence.sh

- **Purpose:** one versioned evidence directory (spec Phase 13 item 8).
- **Command:** `scripts/archive-evidence.sh --out-dir <new dir> [--plans-dir scratch/plans] [--metrics-from <ISO UTC> --metrics-to <ISO UTC>] [--s3-prefix reports/evidence/<name>/] [--profile p] [--region us-east-2] [--dry-run]`
- **Gathers:** sbt JUnit reports, plan checksums and summaries (never the plan file or JSON), the
  `operations/` records (image digests, releases, smoke, rollbacks, pipeline and store-load runs with
  their manifests), `experiments/results/`, optional CloudWatch GetMetricData exports (ECS CPU and
  memory per service, ALB requests, target 5xx and p90 latency, queue and DLQ depth, Neptune CPU),
  `index.json`, and `SHA256SUMS`. The directory must not exist; an S3 prefix must be empty.
- **Environment:** `AWS_PROFILE` only for metrics or S3. **Runs it:** the human or deploy-engineer.
  **Approval points:** none (it writes only the new directory and the empty prefix).
- **Evidence:** the archive itself. **Rollback:** delete the new directory.

## cleanup.sh (human only)

- **Purpose:** the documented cleanup path (spec "Cleanup requirements", ruling 6). No agent runs it;
  `guard-dangerous.sh` blocks direct agent invocations (not every indirect form; see the work order 3
  report).
- **Command, first invocation:** `TF_VAR_alarm_email=... scripts/cleanup.sh --stage runtime|infra|final [--evidence-archive <location>] [--var-file ...] [--out-dir scratch/plans] [--record-dir operations/cleanup] [--profile p] [--region us-east-2] [--dry-run]`.
  It writes the inventory, a plan printed in full, every resource it destroys, every resource it keeps
  with the reason, and the plan sha256. Nothing changes.
- **Command, second invocation:** `scripts/cleanup.sh --plan-file scratch/plans/cleanup-<stamp>-<stage>/plan.tfplan --confirm <sha256>`.
  It checks the checksum, HEAD, and account, applies the saved plan, takes the second inventory, and
  writes `record.json` listing every retained resource and why.
- **Stages and what is kept:**

| Stage | Destroys | Keeps |
|---|---|---|
| `runtime` (end of window 2) | Neptune, DynamoDB tables, S3 Vectors bucket and indexes, SQS, the API key secret (7-day recovery window), ECS services and task definitions, ALB, runtime security groups and IAM roles, alarms, dashboard | network, data bucket, ECR, EMR roles and groups, CodeBuild, log groups, Terraform state |
| `infra` (evidence archived) | network, EMR roles and groups, CodeBuild and its role, any runtime module left | data bucket (grading evidence), ECR repository and images, every log group, Terraform state |
| `final` (needs `--evidence-archive`, the instructor-approved location) | everything in state | Terraform state; the data bucket and ECR repository survive unless already empty (`force_destroy` and `force_delete` are false; the script never empties them) |

  Ephemeral EMR clusters are listed in the inventory, never terminated by the script. KMS: no
  customer-managed key exists (ruling 1).
- **Environment:** `AWS_PROFILE`, `TF_VAR_alarm_email`. **Approval points:** the `--confirm` checksum
  is the documented human confirmation. **Evidence:** `operations/cleanup/cleanup-<stamp>-<stage>/`
  (inventories before and after, `apply.txt`, retained list, record).
- **Rollback:** a destroyed runtime is recreated by `plan.sh --runtime-enabled true` and `deploy.sh`,
  then `load-stores.sh` from the write-once S3 files.

## Dependencies (Phase 12 work orders 3 to 8, all complete)

- **dataset-engineer (work order 7):** `datasets/scripts/make_fraction.sh` writes the 25 and 50 percent
  subsets under `scratch/datasets/fraction=<p>/` (rule fraction-v1, `datasets/schemas/fractions.md`);
  `run-bounded-pipeline.sh` reads that layout.
- **storage-engineer (work order 5):** `LoadNeptuneCli --live` without `--profile`, with
  `--stage-from-bucket`; `load-stores.sh` step 2 uses that form.
- **service-engineer and domain-engineer (work orders 6 and 4):** `--profile` is optional in the
  services launcher, and the core loader honours `SCHOLARCHAIN_LLM_REQUESTS_PER_MINUTE`; the ECS task
  definitions use both.

===== UNTRACKED: scripts/archive-evidence.sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Gathers the evidence of a run into one versioned directory (spec Phase 13 item 8).
set -euo pipefail
# shellcheck source=lib/common.sh
. "$(dirname "$0")/lib/common.sh"

usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage: scripts/archive-evidence.sh --out-dir <new directory> [--plans-dir scratch/plans]
           [--metrics-from <ISO-8601 UTC> --metrics-to <ISO-8601 UTC>] [--s3-prefix reports/evidence/<name>/]
           [--profile <name>] [--region us-east-2] [--dry-run]

Copies into <out-dir> (it must not exist: one directory per archive, never overwritten):
  test-reports/<project>/   sbt JUnit XML from modules/*/target/test-reports and tests/target/test-reports
  plans/<plan>/             plan.sha256, plan-meta.json, summary.md of every plan under --plans-dir
                            (never plan.tfplan, plan.json, or plan.txt: they hold planned values)
  operations/               package, release, smoke and rollback records (image digests), pipeline
                            run and store-load records with their run manifests, plan summaries
  experiments-results/      experiments/results/ (experiment outputs)
  metrics/cloudwatch.json   with --metrics-from/--metrics-to: CloudWatch GetMetricData (60 s) for
                            ECS CPU and memory per service, ALB requests, target 5xx and p90 latency,
                            job queue and DLQ depth, Neptune CPU (runtime outputs only)
  index.json, SHA256SUMS    commit, creation time, sources; a checksum of every file
With --s3-prefix (must start with reports/evidence/ and be empty): uploads the directory to the data
bucket under that prefix.

Without --metrics-* and --s3-prefix no AWS call is made.
--dry-run validates flags, lists the sources found, makes no AWS/Terraform/git call.
Environment: AWS_PROFILE (or --profile) only for metrics or S3.
Run by: the human or deploy-engineer (read-only on the sources; writes only <out-dir> and the prefix).
Rollback: none; delete the new directory to discard an archive.
Exit: 0 archived; 2 usage; 3 the out-dir or S3 prefix already exists.
EOF
}

OUT=""
PLANS="$SC_ROOT/scratch/plans"
FROM=""
TO=""
PREFIX=""
while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do
  case "$1" in
    --out-dir) OUT="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --plans-dir) PLANS="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --metrics-from) FROM="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --metrics-to) TO="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --s3-prefix) PREFIX="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --profile) PROFILE="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --region) REGION="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --dry-run) DRY_RUN=1; shift ;;
    --help | -h) usage; exit 0 ;;
    *) usage >&2; die "unknown argument: $1" 2 ;;
  esac
done
[ -n "$OUT" ] || die "--out-dir is required" 2
[ ! -e "$OUT" ] || die "$OUT exists; an archive is never overwritten" 3
ISO='^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}Z$'
if [ -n "$FROM$TO" ]; then
  printf '%s' "$FROM" | grep -Eq "$ISO" && printf '%s' "$TO" | grep -Eq "$ISO" || die "--metrics-from and --metrics-to must both be YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ" 2
fi
if [ -n "$PREFIX" ]; then
  case "$PREFIX" in reports/evidence/?*/) ;; *) die "--s3-prefix must look like reports/evidence/<name>/" 2 ;; esac
fi
need jq

REPORTS=""
for d in "$SC_ROOT"/modules/*/target/test-reports "$SC_ROOT"/tests/target/test-reports; do
  [ -d "$d" ] && REPORTS="$REPORTS $d"
done

dry_run_exit \
  "test reports:${REPORTS:- none}" \
  "plans: $PLANS/*/{plan.sha256,plan-meta.json,summary.md}" \
  "operations/ and experiments/results/ trees" \
  "$([ -n "$FROM" ] && echo "CloudWatch GetMetricData $FROM to $TO" || echo "no metrics (no --metrics-from/--metrics-to)")" \
  "$([ -n "$PREFIX" ] && echo "upload to s3://<data bucket>/$PREFIX (must be empty)" || echo "no S3 upload")" \
  "index.json and SHA256SUMS in $OUT"

mkdir -p "$OUT"
step "test-reports"
for d in $REPORTS; do
  p="${d#"$SC_ROOT"/}"
  p="${p%%/target/*}"
  mkdir -p "$OUT/test-reports/${p//\//-}"
  cp -R "$d/." "$OUT/test-reports/${p//\//-}/"
done

step "plans"
for d in "$PLANS"/*/; do
  [ -f "$d/plan.sha256" ] || continue
  n="$(basename "$d")"
  mkdir -p "$OUT/plans/$n"
  for f in plan.sha256 plan-meta.json summary.md; do [ ! -f "$d/$f" ] || cp "$d/$f" "$OUT/plans/$n/"; done
done

step "records"
[ ! -d "$SC_ROOT/operations" ] || cp -R "$SC_ROOT/operations" "$OUT/operations"
[ ! -d "$SC_ROOT/experiments/results" ] || cp -R "$SC_ROOT/experiments/results" "$OUT/experiments-results"

if [ -n "$FROM$PREFIX" ]; then
  require_profile
  aws_target
  tf_outputs
fi

if [ -n "$FROM" ]; then
  step "metrics"
  [ "$(tfo '.runtime_enabled.value')" = true ] || die "runtime metrics need runtime_enabled outputs" 3
  QUERIES="$(jq -c '
    def q($id; $ns; $m; $dims; $stat): {Id: $id, MetricStat: {Metric: {Namespace: $ns, MetricName: $m,
      Dimensions: ($dims | to_entries | map({Name: .key, Value: .value}))}, Period: 60, Stat: $stat}};
    .ecs_cluster_name.value as $c | .alb_arn_suffix.value as $alb
    | (.job_queue_url.value | split("/") | last) as $q | (.job_dlq_url.value | split("/") | last) as $dlq
    | [ (.ecs_service_names.value | to_entries[] | .value as $s | ($s | gsub("-"; "_")) as $i
         | q("cpu_" + $i; "AWS/ECS"; "CPUUtilization"; {ClusterName: $c, ServiceName: $s}; "Average"),
           q("mem_" + $i; "AWS/ECS"; "MemoryUtilization"; {ClusterName: $c, ServiceName: $s}; "Average")),
        q("alb_requests"; "AWS/ApplicationELB"; "RequestCount"; {LoadBalancer: $alb}; "Sum"),
        q("alb_target_5xx"; "AWS/ApplicationELB"; "HTTPCode_Target_5XX_Count"; {LoadBalancer: $alb}; "Sum"),
        q("alb_latency_p90"; "AWS/ApplicationELB"; "TargetResponseTime"; {LoadBalancer: $alb}; "p90"),
        q("queue_depth"; "AWS/SQS"; "ApproximateNumberOfMessagesVisible"; {QueueName: $q}; "Maximum"),
        q("dlq_depth"; "AWS/SQS"; "ApproximateNumberOfMessagesVisible"; {QueueName: $dlq}; "Maximum"),
        q("neptune_cpu"; "AWS/Neptune"; "CPUUtilization"; {DBClusterIdentifier: .neptune_cluster_id.value}; "Average") ]' "$TF_OUT")"
  mkdir -p "$OUT/metrics"
  printf '%s\n' "$QUERIES" >"$OUT/metrics/queries.json"
  aws_retry cloudwatch get-metric-data --start-time "$FROM" --end-time "$TO" \
    --metric-data-queries "file://$OUT/metrics/queries.json" --output json >"$OUT/metrics/cloudwatch.json"
  ok "metrics: $(jq '.MetricDataResults | length' "$OUT/metrics/cloudwatch.json") series"
fi

step "index"
COMMIT="$(git_head 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)"
jq -n --arg commit "$COMMIT" --arg at "$(utc_now)" --arg from "$FROM" --arg to "$TO" --arg prefix "$PREFIX" \
  '{schemaVersion: "evidence-archive-v1", commit: $commit, createdAt: $at,
    metricsWindow: (if $from == "" then null else {from: $from, to: $to} end),
    s3Prefix: (if $prefix == "" then null else $prefix end),
    sources: ["test-reports", "plans", "operations", "experiments-results", "metrics"]}' >"$OUT/index.json"
(cd "$OUT" && find . -type f ! -name SHA256SUMS | LC_ALL=C sort | while IFS= read -r f; do
  printf '%s  %s\n' "$(sha256_hex "$f")" "${f#./}"
done >SHA256SUMS)
ok "$(wc -l <"$OUT/SHA256SUMS" | tr -d ' ') files in $OUT"

if [ -n "$PREFIX" ]; then
  step "upload"
  BUCKET="$(tfo '.data_bucket_name.value')"
  [ "$(aws_retry s3api list-objects-v2 --bucket "$BUCKET" --prefix "$PREFIX" --max-items 1 --query 'length(Contents || `[]`)' --output text)" = 0 ] ||
    die "s3://$BUCKET/$PREFIX is not empty; archives are never overwritten" 3
  aws_retry s3 cp --only-show-errors --recursive "$OUT" "s3://$BUCKET/$PREFIX"
  ok "uploaded to s3://$BUCKET/$PREFIX"
fi
printf '==> ARCHIVE dir=%s\n' "$OUT"

===== UNTRACKED: scripts/check.sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Non-cloud validation of a checkout (spec Phase 13 item 1). No AWS call. Runs every gate, then
# exits 1 if any failed.
set -euo pipefail
# shellcheck source=lib/common.sh
. "$(dirname "$0")/lib/common.sh"

usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage: scripts/check.sh [--help]

Gates, in order (all run; the exit code is 1 if any failed):
  json               jq parses .claude/settings.json and .mcp.json
  constitution       .claude/hooks/validate-agent-files.sh (agent files and hook self-tests)
  bash-syntax        bash -n on .claude/hooks/*.sh, scripts/**/*.sh and datasets/scripts/*.sh
  shellcheck         shellcheck -S error on scripts/**/*.sh (SKIPPED when not installed)
  dataset-validation bash datasets/scripts/validate.sh (manifest, samples, checksums, self-tests;
                     no network)
  terraform-fmt      terraform fmt -check -recursive infra/terraform
  terraform-validate terraform init -backend=false -lockfile=readonly, then validate
  sbt-check          SPARK_LOCAL_IP=127.0.0.1 sbt -J-Xmx6g -batch check
                     (scalafmt check, Test/compile, every test, markTestRun)

Needs: jq, bash, terraform 1.16.6, sbt, a JDK. Network: only the first terraform init (provider
download) and sbt dependency resolution. No AWS credentials are read.
EOF
}

case "${1-}" in
  --help | -h) usage; exit 0 ;;
  "") ;;
  *) usage >&2; exit 2 ;;
esac

need jq terraform sbt
FAILED=""

gate() {
  local name="$1"
  shift
  step "$name"
  if "$@"; then ok "$name"; else
    printf '==> FAIL %s\n' "$name" >&2
    FAILED="$FAILED $name"
  fi
}

json_ok() { jq -e . "$SC_ROOT/.claude/settings.json" "$SC_ROOT/.mcp.json" >/dev/null; }

constitution_ok() { "$SC_ROOT/.claude/hooks/validate-agent-files.sh"; }

bash_syntax_ok() {
  local f rc=0
  for f in "$SC_ROOT"/.claude/hooks/*.sh "$SC_ROOT"/scripts/*.sh "$SC_ROOT"/scripts/*/*.sh "$SC_ROOT"/datasets/scripts/*.sh; do
    if bash -n "$f"; then info "ok   ${f#"$SC_ROOT"/}"; else info "FAIL ${f#"$SC_ROOT"/}"; rc=1; fi
  done
  return "$rc"
}

shellcheck_ok() {
  if ! command -v shellcheck >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    info "SKIPPED: shellcheck not installed"
    return 0
  fi
  shellcheck -x -S error "$SC_ROOT"/scripts/*.sh "$SC_ROOT"/scripts/*/*.sh
}

# Through bash, as the manifest's validation_commands do: the datasets/scripts files are not executable.
datasets_ok() { bash "$SC_ROOT/datasets/scripts/validate.sh"; }

tf_fmt_ok() { terraform fmt -check -recursive "$SC_ROOT/infra/terraform"; }

tf_validate_ok() {
  tf_require_version
  tf init -backend=false -input=false -lockfile=readonly >/dev/null && tf validate
}

sbt_check_ok() { sbt_run check; }

gate json json_ok
gate constitution constitution_ok
gate bash-syntax bash_syntax_ok
gate shellcheck shellcheck_ok
gate dataset-validation datasets_ok
gate terraform-fmt tf_fmt_ok
gate terraform-validate tf_validate_ok
gate sbt-check sbt_check_ok

if [ -n "$FAILED" ]; then
  die "check failed:$FAILED" 1
fi
ok "check: every gate passed"

===== UNTRACKED: scripts/cleanup.sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Course-environment cleanup (spec "Cleanup requirements", ruling 6). HUMAN ONLY: no agent runs
# this script (docs/agents.md section 8). Two invocations: the first writes and prints a plan and
# the inventory; the second destroys only with --confirm <sha256 of that plan>.
set -euo pipefail
# shellcheck source=lib/common.sh
. "$(dirname "$0")/lib/common.sh"

usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage: scripts/cleanup.sh --stage runtime|infra|final [--evidence-archive <location>]
                          [--var-file <path>] [--profile <name>] [--region us-east-2]
                          [--out-dir scratch/plans] [--record-dir operations/cleanup] [--dry-run]
       scripts/cleanup.sh --plan-file <dir>/plan.tfplan --confirm <64 hex> [--profile <name>]

Stages (each keeps the Terraform state, local and backed up by the human outside the repository):
  runtime  end of cloud window 2 (D-13): a plan with runtime_enabled = false. Destroys Neptune, the
           DynamoDB tables, the S3 Vectors bucket and indexes, SQS, the API key secret (it enters its
           7-day recovery window), ECS services, task definitions, the ALB, runtime security groups,
           runtime IAM roles, alarms and dashboard. Keeps the network, data bucket, ECR, EMR roles,
           CodeBuild, and log groups.
  infra    after the evidence is archived: plan -destroy of the network, EMR roles and security
           groups, CodeBuild and its role (and any runtime module left). Keeps the data bucket (grading
           evidence), the ECR repository and its images, and every log group.
  final    only after the instructor approved the evidence archive location, given as
           --evidence-archive: plan -destroy of everything. The data bucket (force_destroy = false)
           and the ECR repository (force_delete = false) are deleted only when already empty; the
           script lists what they still hold and never empties them.
Ephemeral EMR clusters are not Terraform resources: active ones are listed in the inventory, never
terminated here.

First invocation: clean tree, account and Region, inventory-before (Terraform state addresses,
resources tagged Project=ScholarChain, active EMR clusters, ECR images, top-level bucket prefixes),
the plan printed in full, every resource it destroys, every resource it keeps with the reason,
and the plan sha256. Nothing is changed.
Second invocation (--plan-file and --confirm): the sha256 must match, HEAD and the account must be
the plan's; then terraform apply <plan file> (saved plan, no -auto-approve), inventory-after, and
<record-dir>/cleanup-<stamp>-<stage>/record.json listing what remains and why.

Environment: AWS_PROFILE (or --profile); TF_VAR_alarm_email (the configuration requires it; may be empty).
Run by: the human architect only. Approval: the --confirm checksum is the documented human confirmation.
Exit: 0 planned or destroyed; 2 usage; 3 precondition; 4 checksum mismatch; 1 terraform failure.
EOF
}

STAGE=""
ARCHIVE=""
VAR_FILE="$SC_TF_DIR/course.tfvars"
OUT_DIR="$SC_ROOT/scratch/plans"
RECORD_DIR="$SC_ROOT/operations/cleanup"
PLAN_FILE=""
CONFIRM=""
while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do
  case "$1" in
    --stage) STAGE="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --evidence-archive) ARCHIVE="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --var-file) VAR_FILE="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --profile) PROFILE="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --region) REGION="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --out-dir) OUT_DIR="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --record-dir) RECORD_DIR="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --plan-file) PLAN_FILE="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --confirm) CONFIRM="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --dry-run) DRY_RUN=1; shift ;;
    --help | -h) usage; exit 0 ;;
    *) usage >&2; die "unknown argument: $1" 2 ;;
  esac
done
need terraform jq git aws

# Destroyed by the infra stage; everything else (data bucket, ECR, log groups) is kept.
INFRA_TARGETS="module.network module.emr module.build.aws_codebuild_project.services_image
module.build.aws_iam_role_policy.codebuild module.build.aws_iam_role.codebuild module.ecs module.iam
module.neptune module.dynamodb module.vectors module.messaging module.apikey"

inventory() { # <file prefix>
  step "inventory $(basename "$1")"
  tf show -json | jq '[.. | objects | select(has("address") and .mode? == "managed") | .address] | sort' >"$1.terraform.json"
  aws_retry resourcegroupstaggingapi get-resources --tag-filters Key=Project,Values=ScholarChain \
    --query 'ResourceTagMappingList[].ResourceARN' --output json >"$1.tagged.json"
  aws_retry emr list-clusters --active --query 'Clusters[].[Id, Name, Status.State]' --output json >"$1.emr-active.json"
  local bucket repo
  bucket="$(jq -r '.data_bucket_name.value // empty' "$TF_OUT")"
  repo="$(jq -r '.ecr_repository_name.value // empty' "$TF_OUT")"
  if [ -n "$repo" ]; then
    aws_retry ecr list-images --repository-name "$repo" --query 'imageIds[].imageTag' --output json >"$1.ecr-images.json" || echo '[]' >"$1.ecr-images.json"
  fi
  if [ -n "$bucket" ]; then
    aws_retry s3api list-objects-v2 --bucket "$bucket" --delimiter / --query 'CommonPrefixes[].Prefix' --output json >"$1.bucket-prefixes.json" || echo '[]' >"$1.bucket-prefixes.json"
  fi
  info "terraform: $(jq length "$1.terraform.json") resources; tagged: $(jq length "$1.tagged.json"); active EMR: $(jq length "$1.emr-active.json")"
  [ "$(jq length "$1.emr-active.json")" = 0 ] || warn "active EMR clusters (not Terraform; terminate them by hand if they are stuck): $(jq -c . "$1.emr-active.json")"
}

retained_reason() {
  case "$1" in
    module.data.*) echo "data bucket: grading evidence until the instructor approves the archive (force_destroy=false)" ;;
    module.build.aws_ecr_*) echo "ECR: image history for reproduction (force_delete=false)" ;;
    *aws_cloudwatch_log_group*) echo "log groups: logs kept as evidence (14-day retention)" ;;
    module.foundation.*) echo "data source only" ;;
    *) echo "kept by this stage" ;;
  esac
}

# ---- second invocation: destroy with the confirmed plan -----------------------------------
if [ -n "$CONFIRM$PLAN_FILE" ]; then
  [ -f "$PLAN_FILE" ] || die "--plan-file not found" 2
  is_hex64 "$CONFIRM" || die "--confirm must be the 64-hex sha256 printed by the first invocation" 2
  META="$(dirname "$PLAN_FILE")/cleanup-meta.json"
  [ -f "$META" ] || die "cleanup-meta.json missing beside the plan (write cleanup plans only with this script)" 3
  ACTUAL="$(sha256_hex "$PLAN_FILE")"
  [ "$ACTUAL" = "$CONFIRM" ] && [ "$(jq -r .planSha256 "$META")" = "$ACTUAL" ] ||
    die "plan sha256 $ACTUAL does not equal --confirm $CONFIRM; nothing destroyed" 4
  STAGE="$(jq -r .stage "$META")"
  dry_run_exit "verify HEAD, account and Region against $META" "terraform apply $PLAN_FILE (stage $STAGE)" "inventory-after; record in $RECORD_DIR"
  tf_require_version
  require_clean_tree
  [ "$(git_head)" = "$(jq -r .commit "$META")" ] || die "HEAD is not the plan's commit" 3
  require_profile
  aws_target
  [ "$ACCOUNT" = "$(jq -r .account "$META")" ] || die "account $ACCOUNT is not the plan's" 3
  tf_outputs
  REC="$RECORD_DIR/cleanup-$(utc_stamp)-$STAGE"
  mkdir -p "$REC"
  inventory "$REC/before"
  step "destroy"
  info "the state file stays; back it up outside the repository after this step"
  tf apply -input=false -lock-timeout=60s "$PLAN_FILE" 2>&1 | tee "$REC/apply.txt"
  tf output -json >"$TF_OUT" || echo '{}' >"$TF_OUT"
  inventory "$REC/after"
  jq -r '.[]' "$REC/after.terraform.json" | while IFS= read -r a; do printf '%s\t%s\n' "$a" "$(retained_reason "$a")"; done >"$REC/retained.tsv"
  jq -n --arg stage "$STAGE" --arg plan "$ACTUAL" --arg at "$(utc_now)" --arg account "$ACCOUNT" --arg region "$REGION" \
    --arg commit "$(git_head)" --arg archive "$(jq -r '.evidenceArchive // ""' "$META")" --rawfile retained "$REC/retained.tsv" \
    '{schemaVersion: "cleanup-record-v1", stage: $stage, planSha256: $plan, finishedAt: $at, account: $account,
      region: $region, commit: $commit, evidenceArchive: (if $archive == "" then null else $archive end),
      retained: ($retained | split("\n") | map(select(. != "") | split("\t") | {address: .[0], reason: .[1]})),
      inventories: ["before.*.json", "after.*.json"], stateKept: true}' >"$REC/record.json"
  cat "$REC/retained.tsv"
  printf '==> CLEANUP stage=%s record=%s\n' "$STAGE" "$REC/record.json"
  ok "cleanup $STAGE applied"
  exit 0
fi

# ---- first invocation: inventory and plan, nothing changed --------------------------------
case "$STAGE" in
  runtime | infra) ;;
  final) [ -n "$ARCHIVE" ] || die "--stage final needs --evidence-archive <instructor-approved location>" 2 ;;
  *) die "--stage runtime|infra|final is required" 2 ;;
esac
[ -f "$VAR_FILE" ] || die "var file not found: $VAR_FILE" 2
VAR_FILE="$(cd "$(dirname "$VAR_FILE")" && pwd)/$(basename "$VAR_FILE")"
[ "${TF_VAR_alarm_email+set}" = set ] || die "set TF_VAR_alarm_email (an empty value is fine)" 2
case "$STAGE" in
  runtime) PLAN_ARGS="-var runtime_enabled=false" ;;
  infra) PLAN_ARGS="-destroy$(for t in $INFRA_TARGETS; do printf ' -target=%s' "$t"; done)" ;;
  final) PLAN_ARGS="-destroy" ;;
esac

dry_run_exit \
  "refuse a dirty tree; account and Region; terraform outputs; inventory-before" \
  "terraform plan -input=false -var-file=$VAR_FILE -var region=$REGION $PLAN_ARGS -out=<dir>/plan.tfplan (printed in full)" \
  "list every resource destroyed and every resource kept with its reason; print the sha256" \
  "nothing is destroyed until: scripts/cleanup.sh --plan-file <dir>/plan.tfplan --confirm <sha256>"

tf_require_version
require_clean_tree
require_profile
aws_target
COMMIT="$(git_head)"
tf init -input=false -lockfile=readonly
tf_outputs
DIR="$OUT_DIR/cleanup-$(utc_stamp)-$STAGE"
mkdir -p "$DIR"
chmod 700 "$DIR"
inventory "$DIR/inventory-before"

step "plan $STAGE"
# shellcheck disable=SC2086
tf plan -input=false -lock-timeout=60s -var-file="$VAR_FILE" -var "region=$REGION" $PLAN_ARGS \
  -out="$DIR/plan.tfplan" 2>&1 | tee "$DIR/plan.txt"
(umask 077 && tf show -json "$DIR/plan.tfplan" >"$DIR/plan.json")
SHA="$(sha256_hex "$DIR/plan.tfplan")"

step "resources destroyed"
jq -r '.resource_changes[] | select(.mode == "managed") | select(.change.actions | index("delete"))
  | "- \(.change.actions | join("+")) \(.address)"' "$DIR/plan.json" | tee "$DIR/destroyed.txt"
step "resources kept"
jq -r '[.resource_changes[] | select(.mode == "managed") | select(.change.actions | index("delete")) | .address] as $d
  | .prior_state.values // {} | [.. | objects | select(has("address") and .mode? == "managed") | .address]
  | map(select(. as $a | $d | index($a) | not)) | .[]' "$DIR/plan.json" |
  while IFS= read -r a; do printf -- '- %s: %s\n' "$a" "$(retained_reason "$a")"; done | tee "$DIR/kept.txt"
if [ "$STAGE" = final ]; then
  warn "the data bucket and the ECR repository are deleted only if empty: prefixes $(jq -c . "$DIR/inventory-before.bucket-prefixes.json" 2>/dev/null), images $(jq length "$DIR/inventory-before.ecr-images.json" 2>/dev/null)"
fi

jq -n --arg stage "$STAGE" --arg commit "$COMMIT" --arg account "$ACCOUNT" --arg region "$REGION" \
  --arg sha "$SHA" --arg archive "$ARCHIVE" --arg at "$(utc_now)" \
  '{schemaVersion: "cleanup-meta-v1", stage: $stage, commit: $commit, account: $account, region: $region,
    planSha256: $sha, createdAt: $at, evidenceArchive: (if $archive == "" then null else $archive end)}' >"$DIR/cleanup-meta.json"
printf '==> CLEANUP-PLAN stage=%s file=%s sha256=%s destroys=%s\n' "$STAGE" "$DIR/plan.tfplan" "$SHA" "$(grep -c . "$DIR/destroyed.txt" || true)"
ok "nothing destroyed. To proceed, the human runs: scripts/cleanup.sh --plan-file $DIR/plan.tfplan --confirm $SHA"

===== UNTRACKED: scripts/deploy.sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Applies one approved saved plan and verifies the deployed revision (spec Phase 13 item 4,
# ruling 6). The approval is the plan's sha256, given explicitly; nothing else is applied.
set -euo pipefail
# shellcheck source=lib/common.sh
. "$(dirname "$0")/lib/common.sh"

usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage: scripts/deploy.sh --plan-file <dir>/plan.tfplan --approved-plan-sha256 <64 hex>
                         [--package-record <operations/releases/package-<sha>.json>]
                         [--profile <name>] [--region us-east-2] [--release-dir <dir>]
                         [--stable-timeout-minutes 20] [--resume] [--dry-run]

Gates, in order:
  1. clean tree (outside operations/ and experiments/results/), HEAD = the plan's commit
  2. sha256(plan file) = --approved-plan-sha256, else exit 4 and nothing is applied
  3. the profile's account and Region = the plan's (plan-meta.json beside the plan file)
  4. terraform apply <plan file> (a saved plan: no prompt, no -auto-approve, no other input);
     skipped with --resume
  5. runtime_enabled only:
     - the API key secret must have a value (DescribeSecret, the value is never read); if not, exit 5:
       the human sets it (infra/terraform/README.md), then the same command runs again with --resume
     - --resume forces a new deployment of query-api, graph-evidence-api and worker (together)
     - waits until every service is stable (one COMPLETED deployment, running = desired), bounded
     - every running task's image digest = the package record's digest, and its task definition image
       = <repository>:<package record tag>
     - query-api and the worker print the same runtime run ID (SERVICES.md section 5)
  6. writes the release record <release-dir>/release-<UTC stamp>-<commit 12>.json and the apply log
     (.apply.txt, not .log, so that Git does not ignore it)

Flags:
  --plan-file              required; written by scripts/plan.sh
  --approved-plan-sha256   required; the checksum the human signed at the plan gate
  --package-record         required when the plan has runtime_enabled = true (scripts/package.sh)
  --release-dir            default operations/releases
  --stable-timeout-minutes default 20
  --resume                 no apply: the plan was applied already (for example before the secret
                           value existed); still requires the same plan file and checksum
  --dry-run                validate flags and local files, print the steps, no AWS/Terraform/git call

Environment: AWS_PROFILE (or --profile).
Run by: deploy-engineer through the gated command, after the human approved the plan checksum
(docs/agents.md section 8 and escalation 4/5).
Rollback: a failed ECS deployment is rolled back by the ECS circuit breaker; otherwise
scripts/rollback.sh --to-release <earlier release record>. Terraform changes are not rolled back
automatically.
Exit: 0 deployed and verified; 1 verification failed; 3 precondition; 4 checksum mismatch;
5 waiting for the secret value; 6 timeout.
EOF
}

PLAN_FILE=""
APPROVED=""
PACKAGE_RECORD=""
RELEASE_DIR="$SC_ROOT/operations/releases"
STABLE_MIN=20
RESUME=0
while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do
  case "$1" in
    --plan-file) PLAN_FILE="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --approved-plan-sha256) APPROVED="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --package-record) PACKAGE_RECORD="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --profile) PROFILE="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --region) REGION="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --release-dir) RELEASE_DIR="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --stable-timeout-minutes) STABLE_MIN="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --resume) RESUME=1; shift ;;
    --dry-run) DRY_RUN=1; shift ;;
    --help | -h) usage; exit 0 ;;
    *) usage >&2; die "unknown argument: $1" 2 ;;
  esac
done

[ -f "$PLAN_FILE" ] || die "--plan-file not found: ${PLAN_FILE:-<none>}" 2
is_hex64 "$APPROVED" || die "--approved-plan-sha256 must be 64 lower-case hex" 2
printf '%s' "$STABLE_MIN" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' && [ "$STABLE_MIN" -ge 1 ] && [ "$STABLE_MIN" -le 60 ] ||
  die "--stable-timeout-minutes must be 1 to 60" 2
META="$(dirname "$PLAN_FILE")/plan-meta.json"
[ -f "$META" ] || die "plan-meta.json missing beside the plan file (write plans only with scripts/plan.sh)" 3
need terraform jq git aws

step "approval"
ACTUAL="$(sha256_hex "$PLAN_FILE")"
[ "$ACTUAL" = "$APPROVED" ] || die "plan sha256 $ACTUAL does not equal the approved $APPROVED; nothing applied" 4
[ "$(jq -r .planSha256 "$META")" = "$ACTUAL" ] || die "plan-meta.json names another plan" 4
ok "plan checksum approved: $ACTUAL"

RUNTIME="$(jq -r .runtimeEnabled "$META")"
if [ "$RUNTIME" = true ]; then
  [ -f "$PACKAGE_RECORD" ] || die "--package-record is required for a runtime_enabled plan" 2
  IMAGE_TAG="$(jq -er .image.tag "$PACKAGE_RECORD")" || die "package record has no image.tag" 3
  IMAGE_DIGEST="$(jq -er .image.digest "$PACKAGE_RECORD")" || die "package record has no image.digest" 3
  is_sha40 "$IMAGE_TAG" || die "package image tag is not a Git SHA: $IMAGE_TAG" 3
fi

dry_run_exit \
  "refuse a dirty tree; HEAD must equal plan commit $(jq -r .commit "$META")" \
  "aws sts get-caller-identity; account must be $(jq -r .account "$META"), Region $(jq -r .region "$META")" \
  "$([ "$RESUME" = 1 ] && echo 'skip apply (--resume)' || echo "terraform apply $PLAN_FILE (saved plan, output shown in full)")" \
  "terraform output -json into the release directory" \
  "runtime_enabled=$RUNTIME: secret value check, $([ "$RESUME" = 1 ] && echo 'force a new deployment, ')wait stable (${STABLE_MIN} min), verify digests and runtime run IDs" \
  "write $RELEASE_DIR/release-<stamp>-<sha12>.json"

tf_require_version
require_clean_tree
COMMIT="$(git_head)"
[ "$COMMIT" = "$(jq -r .commit "$META")" ] || die "HEAD $COMMIT is not the plan's commit $(jq -r .commit "$META")" 3
require_profile
[ "$REGION" = "$(jq -r .region "$META")" ] || die "Region $REGION is not the plan's $(jq -r .region "$META")" 3
aws_target
[ "$ACCOUNT" = "$(jq -r .account "$META")" ] || die "account $ACCOUNT is not the plan's $(jq -r .account "$META")" 3

STAMP="$(utc_stamp)"
REL="$RELEASE_DIR/release-$STAMP-${COMMIT:0:12}"
mkdir -p "$RELEASE_DIR"

if [ "$RESUME" = 0 ]; then
  step "apply"
  info "state stays local (ruling 1): back up terraform state outside the repository after this step"
  tf apply -input=false -lock-timeout=60s "$PLAN_FILE" 2>&1 | tee "$REL.apply.txt"
  ok "apply"
else
  step "apply"
  info "SKIPPED (--resume)"
fi

tf_outputs
cp "$TF_OUT" "$REL.outputs.json"
[ "$(tfo '.runtime_enabled.value')" = "$RUNTIME" ] || die "state runtime_enabled differs from the plan's" 3

SERVICES_JSON='[]'
RUN_IDS='{}'
if [ "$RUNTIME" = true ]; then
  CLUSTER="$(tfo '.ecs_cluster_name.value')"
  REPO="$(tfo '.ecr_repository_url.value')"
  SECRET="$(tfo '.api_key_secret_arn.value')"
  step "secret-value"
  CURRENT="$(aws_retry secretsmanager describe-secret --secret-id "$SECRET" \
    --query 'length(VersionIdsToStages || `{}`)' --output text)"
  if [ "$CURRENT" = 0 ]; then
    die "the API key secret has no value yet: the human sets it (infra/terraform/README.md, Human actions 5), then rerun this command with --resume" 5
  fi
  ok "secret has a version (value not read)"

  if [ "$RESUME" = 1 ]; then
    step "force-new-deployment"
    for s in $SC_SERVICES; do
      # Not idempotent (each call starts another deployment): one attempt (code review 1 F3).
      aws_once ecs update-service --cluster "$CLUSTER" --service "$s" --force-new-deployment \
        --query 'service.serviceName' --output text
    done
  fi

  ecs_wait_stable "$CLUSTER" $((STABLE_MIN * 60))
  ecs_verify_image "$CLUSTER" "$REPO:$IMAGE_TAG" "$IMAGE_DIGEST"
  SERVICES_JSON="$ECS_SERVICES_JSON"
  ecs_verify_run_ids "$(jq -c '.log_group_names.value' "$TF_OUT")"
fi

step "record"
jq -n --arg commit "$COMMIT" --arg plan "$ACTUAL" --arg account "$ACCOUNT" --arg region "$REGION" \
  --arg at "$(utc_now)" --argjson runtime "$RUNTIME" --argjson resume "$([ "$RESUME" = 1 ] && echo true || echo false)" \
  --arg tag "${IMAGE_TAG:-}" --arg digest "${IMAGE_DIGEST:-}" --arg pkg "${PACKAGE_RECORD#"$SC_ROOT"/}" \
  --argjson services "$SERVICES_JSON" --argjson runIds "$RUN_IDS" --arg outputs "$(basename "$REL").outputs.json" \
  '{schemaVersion: "release-record-v1", commit: $commit, planSha256: $plan, account: $account, region: $region,
    environment: "course", deployedAt: $at, runtimeEnabled: $runtime, resumed: $resume,
    image: (if $tag == "" then null else {tag: $tag, digest: $digest, packageRecord: $pkg} end),
    services: $services, runtimeRunIds: $runIds, outputsFile: $outputs, verified: true}' >"$REL.json"
printf '==> RELEASE record=%s commit=%s plan=%s\n' "$REL.json" "$COMMIT" "$ACTUAL"
ok "deploy complete; next: scripts/smoke-test.sh"

===== UNTRACKED: scripts/emr/step.sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Runs on the EMR primary node, one call per cluster step (scripts/run-bounded-pipeline.sh uploads
# it beside the job jars and starts it through command-runner.jar). Never run on a laptop.
#
#   step.sh classpath
#       prints the Java, Hadoop, and Spark versions and the classpath entries that matter for the
#       Phase 11 check (Typesafe Config, AWS SDK v2, Scala libraries); never fails the cluster
#   step.sh hadoop <timeout s> <s3 jar> <Pipeline args...>
#       hadoop jar (Main-Class scholarchain.hadoop.Pipeline, J01 to J10 in their documented order)
#   step.sh spark <timeout s> <s3 jar> <bucket> <snapshot> <comma list of earlier Hadoop run dirs> <PipelineJob args...>
#       finds the one Hadoop run directory this cluster created (not in the earlier list, with
#       _manifest.json), then spark-submit PipelineJob (Spark jobs 1 to 11) with --input set to it
set -euo pipefail

mode="${1:?mode}"
shift

# Every AWS CLI call has explicit timeouts (scripts/README.md conventions; code review 1 F7b).
aws_t() { aws --cli-connect-timeout 10 --cli-read-timeout 120 "$@"; }
fetch() { aws_t s3 cp --only-show-errors "$1" "$2"; }

case "$mode" in
  classpath)
    set +e
    java -version 2>&1
    hadoop version 2>&1 | head -3
    spark-submit --version 2>&1 | head -12
    echo "== hadoop classpath (config, awssdk, scala)"
    hadoop classpath --glob | tr ':' '\n' | grep -iE 'typesafe|/config-[0-9]|awssdk|bundle-2|scala' | sort -u
    echo "== /usr/lib/spark/jars (config, awssdk, scala)"
    ls /usr/lib/spark/jars | grep -iE 'typesafe|^config-|awssdk|bundle|scala' | sort
    exit 0
    ;;
  hadoop)
    timeout_s="$1" jar="$2"
    shift 2
    fetch "$jar" /tmp/scholarchain-hadoop.jar
    exec timeout "$timeout_s" hadoop jar /tmp/scholarchain-hadoop.jar "$@"
    ;;
  spark)
    timeout_s="$1" jar="$2" bucket="$3" snapshot="$4" earlier="$5"
    shift 5
    base="s3://$bucket/curated/hadoop/snapshot=$snapshot/"
    run="" n=0
    for d in $(aws_t s3 ls "$base" | awk '$1 == "PRE" {sub("/$", "", $2); print $2}'); do
      case ",$earlier," in *",$d,"*) continue ;; esac
      run="$d" n=$((n + 1))
    done
    [ "$n" = 1 ] || { echo "expected exactly one new Hadoop run under $base, found $n" >&2; exit 1; }
    aws_t s3 ls "$base$run/_manifest.json" >/dev/null || { echo "no _manifest.json in $base$run" >&2; exit 1; }
    echo "==> HADOOP-RUN ${base}${run}"
    fetch "$jar" /tmp/scholarchain-spark.jar
    exec timeout "$timeout_s" spark-submit --master yarn --deploy-mode client \
      --class scholarchain.spark.PipelineJob /tmp/scholarchain-spark.jar \
      --input "s3a://$bucket/curated/hadoop/snapshot=$snapshot/$run" "$@"
    ;;
  *)
    echo "unknown mode: $mode" >&2
    exit 2
    ;;
esac

===== UNTRACKED: scripts/lib/common.sh
# shellcheck shell=bash
# Shared helpers for scripts/*.sh. Sourced, never executed. Bash 3.2 compatible (macOS): no
# associative arrays, no mapfile, no ${x,,}.
#
# Exit codes used by every script:
#   0 success
#   1 a gate or verification failed (evidence is printed)
#   2 usage error or missing tool
#   3 precondition failed (dirty tree, wrong account or Region, missing input or record)
#   4 approval mismatch (plan checksum or confirmation does not match)
#   5 waiting for a documented human action (for example the API key secret value)
#   6 timeout of a cloud operation

set -euo pipefail

SC_ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/../.." && pwd)"
SC_TF_DIR="$SC_ROOT/infra/terraform/envs/course"
SC_TF_VERSION="1.16.6"
SC_DEFAULT_REGION="us-east-2"
SC_SNAPSHOT="2026-10-03"
DRY_RUN=0
PROFILE="${AWS_PROFILE:-}"
REGION="$SC_DEFAULT_REGION"

step() { printf '==> STEP %s\n' "$*"; }
info() { printf '    %s\n' "$*"; }
ok() { printf '==> OK %s\n' "$*"; }
warn() { printf '==> WARN %s\n' "$*" >&2; }
die() {
  printf '==> FAIL %s (exit %s)\n' "$1" "${2:-1}" >&2
  exit "${2:-1}"
}

# Flag value or a usage error: VAR="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2
flag_val() {
  [ -n "${2-}" ] && [ "${2#--}" = "$2" ] || die "$1 needs a value" 2
  printf '%s' "$2"
}

need() {
  local t
  for t in "$@"; do command -v "$t" >/dev/null 2>&1 || die "missing tool: $t" 2; done
}

utc_now() { date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ; }
utc_stamp() { date -u +%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ; }

sha256_hex() {
  if command -v sha256sum >/dev/null 2>&1; then sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'; else shasum -a 256 "$1" | awk '{print $1}'; fi
}

is_hex64() { printf '%s' "$1" | grep -Eq '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'; }
is_sha40() { printf '%s' "$1" | grep -Eq '^[0-9a-f]{40}$'; }
is_run_id() { printf '%s' "$1" | grep -Eq '^run-[0-9a-f]{32}$'; }

# Dry run: validate flags and local inputs, print the steps, make no AWS, Terraform, or git call.
dry_run_exit() {
  [ "$DRY_RUN" = 1 ] || return 0
  step "dry-run"
  info "no AWS, Terraform, or git call was made; steps that would run:"
  local s
  for s in "$@"; do info "- $s"; done
  ok "dry-run"
  exit 0
}

git_head() { git -C "$SC_ROOT" rev-parse HEAD; }

# Refuses any change outside the generated record trees (operations/, experiments/results/),
# including untracked files: an untracked .tf or .scala file would change what is built or applied.
require_clean_tree() {
  step "clean-tree"
  local dirty
  dirty="$(git -C "$SC_ROOT" status --porcelain --untracked-files=all -- . \
    ':(exclude)operations' ':(exclude)experiments/results')" || die "git status failed" 3
  if [ -n "$dirty" ]; then
    printf '%s\n' "$dirty" | head -50 >&2
    die "dirty working tree: commit or remove the changes above" 3
  fi
  ok "clean-tree at $(git_head)"
}

require_profile() {
  [ -n "$PROFILE" ] || die "set --profile or AWS_PROFILE" 2
  [ "$REGION" = "$SC_DEFAULT_REGION" ] || die "Region must be $SC_DEFAULT_REGION (D-04); got $REGION" 2
  export AWS_PROFILE="$PROFILE" AWS_REGION="$REGION" AWS_PAGER=""
}

# Read calls and idempotent writes (same key and bytes, same desired state): SDK standard retries.
aws_retry() {
  AWS_RETRY_MODE=standard AWS_MAX_ATTEMPTS=5 aws --profile "$PROFILE" --region "$REGION" \
    --cli-connect-timeout 10 --cli-read-timeout 120 "$@"
}

# Non-idempotent calls (create a cluster, start a build, run a task, register a revision): one attempt.
aws_once() {
  AWS_RETRY_MODE=standard AWS_MAX_ATTEMPTS=1 aws --profile "$PROFILE" --region "$REGION" \
    --cli-connect-timeout 10 --cli-read-timeout 120 "$@"
}

# Prints the target before any action, and sets ACCOUNT.
aws_target() {
  step "target"
  ACCOUNT="$(aws_retry sts get-caller-identity --query Account --output text)" || die "cannot resolve the account (credentials?)" 3
  printf '==> TARGET account=%s region=%s profile=%s\n' "$ACCOUNT" "$REGION" "$PROFILE"
}

tf() { terraform -chdir="$SC_TF_DIR" "$@"; }

tf_require_version() {
  local v
  v="$(terraform version -json | jq -r .terraform_version)"
  [ "$v" = "$SC_TF_VERSION" ] || die "terraform $SC_TF_VERSION required (ruling 4); found $v" 2
}

# Reads the root outputs from local state (no cloud call) into TF_OUT and checks the account.
tf_outputs() {
  step "terraform-outputs"
  TF_OUT="$(mktemp "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/sc-tfout.XXXXXX")"
  tf output -json >"$TF_OUT" || die "terraform output failed (is the environment applied?)" 3
  local acct
  acct="$(tfo '.account_id.value')"
  [ "$acct" = "$ACCOUNT" ] || die "state account $acct differs from the profile account $ACCOUNT" 3
  ok "outputs for account $acct"
}

# One output value by jq path; fails when absent or null (false is a value, so no jq -e).
tfo() {
  local v
  v="$(jq -r "$1" "$TF_OUT")" && [ "$v" != null ] || die "terraform output missing: $1" 3
  printf '%s\n' "$v"
}

# Polls a predicate until it succeeds. The predicate may call die for a terminal failure.
wait_until() {
  local desc="$1" timeout_s="$2" interval_s="$3"
  shift 3
  local deadline=$((SECONDS + timeout_s))
  while ! "$@"; do
    [ "$SECONDS" -lt "$deadline" ] || die "timeout after ${timeout_s}s waiting for $desc" 6
    sleep "$interval_s"
  done
}

# Write-once upload: an object already at the key must carry the same sha256 metadata.
s3_put_if_absent() {
  local f="$1" bucket="$2" key="$3" sha remote err
  sha="$(sha256_hex "$f")"
  err="$(mktemp "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/sc-head.XXXXXX")"
  if remote="$(aws_retry s3api head-object --bucket "$bucket" --key "$key" \
    --query 'Metadata.sha256' --output text 2>"$err")"; then
    rm -f "$err"
    [ "$remote" = "$sha" ] || die "s3://$bucket/$key exists with sha256 '$remote', local $sha (write-once, not overwritten)" 1
    info "present  s3://$bucket/$key"
  else
    if ! grep -Eq '\(404\)|Not Found' "$err"; then
      cat "$err" >&2
      rm -f "$err"
      die "head-object failed for s3://$bucket/$key" 1
    fi
    rm -f "$err"
    aws_retry s3 cp --only-show-errors --metadata "sha256=$sha" "$f" "s3://$bucket/$key"
    info "uploaded s3://$bucket/$key"
  fi
}

# The three runtime services; query-api and the worker always move together (SERVICES.md section 5).
SC_SERVICES="query-api graph-evidence-api worker"

# Waits until every service has one COMPLETED deployment with running = desired >= 1.
ecs_wait_stable() {
  local cluster="$1" timeout_s="$2"
  _ecs_stable() {
    local s line
    for s in $SC_SERVICES; do
      line="$(aws_retry ecs describe-services --cluster "$cluster" --services "$s" --query \
        'services[0].[length(deployments), deployments[0].rolloutState, runningCount, desiredCount]' --output text)"
      info "$s deployments/rollout/running/desired: $line"
      case "$line" in *FAILED*) die "$s deployment FAILED (the circuit breaker rolls it back); see the ECS service events" 1 ;; esac
      printf '%s' "$line" | awk '{exit !($1 == 1 && $2 == "COMPLETED" && $3 == $4 && $3 >= 1)}' || return 1
    done
  }
  step "wait-stable"
  wait_until "ECS services stable" "$timeout_s" 20 _ecs_stable
  ok "services stable"
}

# Every running task of every service runs <image> with <digest>. Sets ECS_SERVICES_JSON.
ecs_verify_image() {
  local cluster="$1" image="$2" digest="$3" s td td_image tasks digests
  step "verify-digests"
  ECS_SERVICES_JSON='[]'
  for s in $SC_SERVICES; do
    td="$(aws_retry ecs describe-services --cluster "$cluster" --services "$s" --query 'services[0].taskDefinition' --output text)"
    td_image="$(aws_retry ecs describe-task-definition --task-definition "$td" --query 'taskDefinition.containerDefinitions[0].image' --output text)"
    [ "$td_image" = "$image" ] || die "$s task definition image $td_image is not $image" 1
    tasks="$(aws_retry ecs list-tasks --cluster "$cluster" --service-name "$s" --desired-status RUNNING --query 'taskArns' --output text)"
    [ -n "$tasks" ] && [ "$tasks" != None ] || die "$s has no running task" 1
    # shellcheck disable=SC2086
    digests="$(aws_retry ecs describe-tasks --cluster "$cluster" --tasks $tasks \
      --query 'tasks[].containers[0].imageDigest' --output text | tr '\t' '\n' | sort -u)"
    [ "$digests" = "$digest" ] || die "$s runs digest(s) '$digests', expected $digest" 1
    info "$s $td $digests"
    ECS_SERVICES_JSON="$(jq -c --arg s "$s" --arg td "$td" --arg d "$digests" '. + [{service: $s, taskDefinitionArn: $td, imageDigest: $d}]' <<<"$ECS_SERVICES_JSON")"
  done
  ok "running digests equal $digest"
}

# query-api and the worker must log the same runtime run ID (SERVICES.md section 5). Sets RUN_IDS.
ecs_verify_run_ids() {
  local logs="$1" s group rid
  step "runtime-run-ids"
  RUN_IDS='{}'
  for s in query-api worker; do
    group="$(jq -er --arg s "$s" '.[$s]' <<<"$logs")"
    rid="$(aws_retry logs filter-log-events --log-group-name "$group" --start-time $((($(date +%s) - 3600) * 1000)) \
      --filter-pattern '"live mode: runtime run"' --query 'events[-1].message' --output text |
      sed -n 's/.*runtime run \(run-[0-9a-f]*\).*/\1/p')"
    [ -n "$rid" ] || die "no 'live mode: runtime run' line from $s in the last hour" 1
    info "$s runtime run $rid"
    RUN_IDS="$(jq -c --arg s "$s" --arg r "$rid" '. + {($s): $r}' <<<"$RUN_IDS")"
  done
  [ "$(jq -r '."query-api"' <<<"$RUN_IDS")" = "$(jq -r .worker <<<"$RUN_IDS")" ] ||
    die "query-api and worker runtime run IDs differ: $RUN_IDS (SERVICES.md section 5)" 1
  ok "query-api and worker share one runtime run ID"
}

# Runs sbt under the Phase 12 ruling 8 limits (SPARK_LOCAL_IP, heap at most 6g, one process).
sbt_run() {
  if pgrep -f 'sbt-launch|xsbt.boot' >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    die "another sbt process is running; never run two at once" 3
  fi
  (cd "$SC_ROOT" && SPARK_LOCAL_IP=127.0.0.1 sbt -J-Xmx6g -batch "$@")
}

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Cloud window 2 store loads (D-13), in the order the storage contracts need: S3 objects first
# (the Neptune bulk loader and the runtime read them), then Neptune, DynamoDB, S3 Vectors.
set -euo pipefail

usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage: scripts/load-stores.sh --store-run <run-..> --vectors-run <run-..>
           [--root scratch/stores/s3] [--vectors-root <root>] [--window-summary-run <run-..>]
           [--window-summary-root scratch/runtime/wo8/s3] [--dynamo-concurrency 8]
           [--neptune-timeout-minutes 60] [--profile <name>] [--region us-east-2]
           [--record-dir <dir>] [--dry-run]

Order (each step stops the script on failure; every loader is write-once and idempotent, so a
rerun skips what is already loaded):
  1. s3       LoadS3Cli --live --profile, once per run manifest, from the local roots:
              runs/store-conversion/snapshot=2026-10-03/run=<store-run>/manifest.json (graph/neptune,
              curated/dynamodb, curated/coauthor-evidence, semantic-context/candidates),
              runs/embed/snapshot=2026-10-03/run=<vectors-run>/manifest.json (vectors/backup,
              semantic-context/text), and
              runs/load-window-summary/snapshot=2026-10-03/run=<window-summary-run>/manifest.json
              (curated/window-summary, from scratch/runtime/wo8/s3)
  2. neptune  the in-VPC path: one-off ECS task scholarchain-course-neptune-loader (no --profile; task
              role) running LoadNeptuneCli --live --stage-from-bucket --dir
              /tmp/stores/s3/graph/neptune/snapshot=2026-10-03/run=<store-run> --out /tmp/stores, then
              one --status task every 2 minutes until LOAD_COMPLETED (bounded)
  3. dynamodb LoadDynamoCli --live --profile --dir <root>/curated/dynamodb/snapshot=.../run=<store-run>
              (about 876k conditional puts) --concurrency <n>
  4. vectors  LoadVectorsCli --live --profile --input <vectors-root>/vectors/backup/snapshot=.../run=<vectors-run>
The laptop loaders get exactly the tasks' SCHOLARCHAIN_* variables (terraform output
runtime_environment), so SCHOLARCHAIN_ENVIRONMENT is non-local and the names are the deployed ones.

Defaults: --root scratch/stores/s3, --vectors-root = --root, --window-summary-run
run-6fbad498beb0d8f570df67665fb97544, --record-dir operations/pipeline-runs (writes
store-load-<stamp>/ with every loader log as <step>.txt, the Neptune task logs as
neptune-<start|status>-<task>.txt, and run.json; .txt, not .log, so that Git does not ignore them).
--dry-run validates flags and local manifests, prints the steps, no AWS/Terraform/git/sbt call.

Environment: AWS_PROFILE (or --profile), used by the laptop loaders through LiveAws.
Run by: the human (the storage live loaders are human-run, storage rulings 1 and 26), after deploy.sh
applied cloud window 2. sbt runs with SPARK_LOCAL_IP=127.0.0.1 and -J-Xmx6g, one process at a time.
Step 2 uses LoadNeptuneCli --live without --profile and --stage-from-bucket (Phase 12 work order 5).
Rollback: none automatic; loads are write-once. Removing loaded data is a human-gated destructive
action (docs/agents.md escalation 2); ending window 2 destroys the stores.
Exit: 0 all loads done; 1 a loader failed; 3 precondition; 6 timeout.
EOF
}

printf '%s' "$CONCURRENCY" | grep -Eq '^([1-9]|[12][0-9]|3[0-2])$' || die "--dynamo-concurrency must be 1 to 32" 2

loader() { # name sbt-command-line
  local name="$1" t0=$SECONDS
  step "$name"
  if sbt_run "$2" 2>&1 | tee "$REC/$name.txt"; then
    note "$name" ok "$((SECONDS - t0))" "$name.txt"
    ok "$name"
  else
    note "$name" failed "$((SECONDS - t0))" "$name.txt"
    die "$name failed; log $REC/$name.txt" 1
  fi
}

# run_loader_task <label> <LoadNeptuneCli args...>: one Fargate task (no retry), waits, saves its log.
run_loader_task() {
  local label="$1" overrides task id code
  shift
  overrides="$(printf '%s\n' "$@" | jq -cRn '[inputs] as $a | {containerOverrides: [{name: "neptune-loader",
    command: (["-main", "scholarchain.storage.cli.LoadNeptuneCli"] + $a)}]}')"
  task="$(aws_once ecs run-task --cluster "$CLUSTER" --task-definition "$FAMILY" --launch-type FARGATE --count 1 \
    --started-by scholarchain-load-stores --overrides "$overrides" \
    --network-configuration "awsvpcConfiguration={subnets=[$SUBNETS],securityGroups=[$SG],assignPublicIp=ENABLED}" \
    --query 'tasks[0].taskArn' --output text)"
  [ -n "$task" ] && [ "$task" != None ] || die "run-task started no task ($label)" 1
  id="${task##*/}"
  info "task $id ($label)"
  _stopped() { [ "$(aws_retry ecs describe-tasks --cluster "$CLUSTER" --tasks "$task" --query 'tasks[0].lastStatus' --output text)" = STOPPED ]; }
  wait_until "loader task $id" 1800 15 _stopped
  code="$(aws_retry ecs describe-tasks --cluster "$CLUSTER" --tasks "$task" --query 'tasks[0].containers[0].exitCode' --output text)"
  aws_retry logs get-log-events --log-group-name "$LOG_GROUP" --log-stream-name "neptune-loader/neptune-loader/$id" \
    --start-from-head --query 'events[].message' --output text | tr '\t' '\n' >"$REC/neptune-$label-$id.txt" || true
  cat "$REC/neptune-$label-$id.txt"
  [ "$code" = 0 ] || die "loader task $id exited $code ($(aws_retry ecs describe-tasks --cluster "$CLUSTER" --tasks "$task" --query 'tasks[0].stoppedReason' --output text)); log $REC/neptune-$label-$id.txt" 1
  LAST_LOG="$REC/neptune-$label-$id.txt"
}

step "neptune"
T0=$SECONDS
run_loader_task start --live --stage-from-bucket --dir "/tmp/stores/s3/graph/neptune/snapshot=$D/run=$STORE_RUN" --out /tmp/stores
LOAD_ID="$(sed -n 's/.*started load \([^ ;]*\).*/\1/p' "$LAST_LOG" | head -1)"
[ -n "$LOAD_ID" ] || die "no 'started load <id>' line in $LAST_LOG" 1
printf '==> NEPTUNE load=%s\n' "$LOAD_ID"
NEPTUNE_STATUS=""
_load_done() {
  run_loader_task status --live --status "$LOAD_ID"
  NEPTUNE_STATUS="$(sed -n 's/^load [^ ]* status \([A-Z_]*\).*/\1/p' "$LAST_LOG" | head -1)"
  info "load $LOAD_ID $NEPTUNE_STATUS"
  case "$NEPTUNE_STATUS" in
    LOAD_COMPLETED) return 0 ;;
    LOAD_NOT_STARTED | LOAD_IN_PROGRESS | LOAD_IN_QUEUE | "") sleep 120; return 1 ;;
    *) die "Neptune load $LOAD_ID ended $NEPTUNE_STATUS; log $LAST_LOG" 1 ;;
  esac
}
wait_until "Neptune load $LOAD_ID" $((NEPTUNE_MIN * 60)) 1 _load_done
note neptune "$NEPTUNE_STATUS" "$((SECONDS - T0))" "$(basename "$LAST_LOG")"
ok "neptune load $LOAD_ID completed"

loader dynamodb "storage/runMain scholarchain.storage.cli.LoadDynamoCli --dir $DYNAMO_DIR $LIVE --concurrency $CONCURRENCY"
loader vectors "storage/runMain scholarchain.storage.cli.LoadVectorsCli --input $VECTORS_DIR $LIVE"

step "record"
jq -n --arg at "$(utc_now)" --arg account "$ACCOUNT" --arg region "$REGION" --arg commit "$(git_head)" \
  --arg storeRun "$STORE_RUN" --arg vectorsRun "$VECTORS_RUN" --arg wsRun "$WS_RUN" --arg loadId "$LOAD_ID" \
  --argjson steps "$STEPS" \
  '{schemaVersion: "store-load-record-v1", finishedAt: $at, account: $account, region: $region, commit: $commit,
    snapshot: "2026-10-03", storeRun: $storeRun, vectorsRun: $vectorsRun, windowSummaryRun: $wsRun,
    neptuneLoadId: $loadId, steps: $steps,
    counts: "printed by each loader; see the per-step logs in this directory"}' >"$REC/run.json"
printf '==> STORES record=%s\n' "$REC/run.json"
ok "store loads complete"

===== UNTRACKED: scripts/package.sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Builds the job jars and the services image for one commit (spec Phase 13 item 2, ruling 3).
# No Docker on the laptop: sbt stages the Docker context, CodeBuild builds and pushes it.
set -euo pipefail
# shellcheck source=lib/common.sh
. "$(dirname "$0")/lib/common.sh"

usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage: scripts/package.sh [--profile <name>] [--region us-east-2] [--record-dir <dir>]
                          [--build-timeout-minutes 30] [--dry-run]

Steps:
  1. clean tree; commit = git rev-parse HEAD (the image tag and the code version)
  2. sbt packageJobs; sha256 of both assembly jars; the Hadoop jar's Main-Class must be
     scholarchain.hadoop.Pipeline (the EMR step relies on it)
  3. SCHOLARCHAIN_IMAGE_TAG=<commit> sbt services/Docker/stage (no Docker), then zip the staged
     context (Dockerfile at the zip root) and take its sha256
  4. upload, write-once by sha256: the jars to s3://<bucket>/artifacts/jobs/<commit>/ and the
     context to s3://<bucket>/artifacts/codebuild/source/<commit>.zip
  5. if ECR has no image tagged <commit>: start one CodeBuild build of that zip with IMAGE_TAG=<commit>
     (no retry), wait for it (bounded); ECR tags are immutable, so an existing tag is reused
  6. read the image digest and the scan-on-push status from ECR
  7. write <record-dir>/package-<commit>.json: commit, jar sha256 values and S3 keys, context
     sha256, image repository, tag, digest, build ID, scan status

Flags:
  --record-dir             default operations/releases
  --build-timeout-minutes  default 30 (the CodeBuild project's own timeout is also 30)
  --dry-run                validate flags, print the steps, no AWS/Terraform/git/sbt call

Environment: AWS_PROFILE (or --profile). sbt runs with SPARK_LOCAL_IP=127.0.0.1 and -J-Xmx6g.
Run by: the human or deploy-engineer, before deploy.sh (cloud window 1 must be applied: ECR,
CodeBuild, and the bucket come from Terraform). Rollback: none needed; artifacts are additive and
keyed by commit.
Exit: 0 recorded; 1 build or verification failed; 3 precondition; 6 timeout.
EOF
}

RECORD_DIR="$SC_ROOT/operations/releases"
BUILD_MIN=30
while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do
  case "$1" in
    --profile) PROFILE="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --region) REGION="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --record-dir) RECORD_DIR="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --build-timeout-minutes) BUILD_MIN="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --dry-run) DRY_RUN=1; shift ;;
    --help | -h) usage; exit 0 ;;
    *) usage >&2; die "unknown argument: $1" 2 ;;
  esac
done
printf '%s' "$BUILD_MIN" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' && [ "$BUILD_MIN" -ge 5 ] && [ "$BUILD_MIN" -le 60 ] ||
  die "--build-timeout-minutes must be 5 to 60" 2
need sbt zip unzip jq git aws terraform

HADOOP_JAR="$SC_ROOT/modules/hadoop/target/scala-3.3.7/hadoop-assembly-0.1.0.jar"
SPARK_JAR="$SC_ROOT/modules/spark/target/scala-3.3.7/sparkAnalytics-assembly-0.1.0.jar"
STAGE="$SC_ROOT/modules/services/target/docker/stage"

dry_run_exit \
  "refuse a dirty tree; commit = HEAD" \
  "sbt -batch packageJobs; sha256 ${HADOOP_JAR#"$SC_ROOT"/} and ${SPARK_JAR#"$SC_ROOT"/}" \
  "SCHOLARCHAIN_IMAGE_TAG=<commit> sbt -batch services/Docker/stage; zip ${STAGE#"$SC_ROOT"/}" \
  "aws sts get-caller-identity; terraform output (bucket, CodeBuild project, ECR)" \
  "upload jars and context write-once under artifacts/" \
  "CodeBuild start-build (once) unless ECR has the tag; wait at most ${BUILD_MIN} min" \
  "ECR describe-images: digest and scan status; write $RECORD_DIR/package-<commit>.json"

require_profile

step "package-jobs"
sbt_run packageJobs
[ -f "$HADOOP_JAR" ] && [ -f "$SPARK_JAR" ] || die "assembly jars not found after packageJobs" 1
MAIN="$(unzip -p "$HADOOP_JAR" META-INF/MANIFEST.MF | tr -d '\r' | sed -n 's/^Main-Class: //p')"
[ "$MAIN" = scholarchain.hadoop.Pipeline ] || die "Hadoop jar Main-Class is '$MAIN', not scholarchain.hadoop.Pipeline" 1
HADOOP_SHA="$(sha256_hex "$HADOOP_JAR")"
SPARK_SHA="$(sha256_hex "$SPARK_JAR")"
info "hadoop $HADOOP_SHA"
info "spark  $SPARK_SHA"
