SCHOLARCHAIN_IMAGE_TAG="$COMMIT" sbt_run services/Docker/stage
for f in Dockerfile 4/opt/docker/bin/query-api 4/opt/docker/bin/graph-evidence-api 4/opt/docker/bin/worker opt/docker/prompts; do
  [ -e "$STAGE/$f" ] || die "staged context lacks $f" 1
done
CONTEXT="$SC_ROOT/modules/services/target/docker/context-$COMMIT.zip"
rm -f "$CONTEXT"
(cd "$STAGE" && zip -qrX "$CONTEXT" .)
CONTEXT_SHA="$(sha256_hex "$CONTEXT")"
info "context $CONTEXT_SHA"

aws_target
tf_outputs
BUCKET="$(tfo '.data_bucket_name.value')"
PROJECT="$(tfo '.codebuild_project_name.value')"
SOURCE_PREFIX="$(tfo '.codebuild_source_prefix.value')"
REPO_NAME="$(tfo '.ecr_repository_name.value')"
REPO_URL="$(tfo '.ecr_repository_url.value')"
JOBS_PREFIX="artifacts/jobs/$COMMIT"

step "upload"
s3_put_if_absent "$HADOOP_JAR" "$BUCKET" "$JOBS_PREFIX/hadoop-assembly.jar"
s3_put_if_absent "$SPARK_JAR" "$BUCKET" "$JOBS_PREFIX/sparkAnalytics-assembly.jar"
s3_put_if_absent "$SC_ROOT/scripts/emr/step.sh" "$BUCKET" "$JOBS_PREFIX/step.sh"
SOURCE_KEY="${SOURCE_PREFIX}$COMMIT.zip"
s3_put_if_absent "$CONTEXT" "$BUCKET" "$SOURCE_KEY"

image_digest() {
  aws_retry ecr describe-images --repository-name "$REPO_NAME" --image-ids "imageTag=$COMMIT" \
    --query 'imageDetails[0].imageDigest' --output text 2>/dev/null
}

step "image-build"
BUILD_ID=""
if DIGEST="$(image_digest)"; then
  info "ECR already holds $REPO_NAME:$COMMIT (immutable tag); no build"
else
  BUILD_ID="$(aws_once codebuild start-build --project-name "$PROJECT" \
    --source-type-override S3 --source-location-override "$BUCKET/$SOURCE_KEY" \
    --environment-variables-override "name=IMAGE_TAG,value=$COMMIT,type=PLAINTEXT" \
    --query 'build.id' --output text)"
  printf '==> BUILD id=%s\n' "$BUILD_ID"
  build_done() {
    local status
    status="$(aws_retry codebuild batch-get-builds --ids "$BUILD_ID" --query 'builds[0].buildStatus' --output text)"
    info "build $status"
    case "$status" in
      SUCCEEDED) return 0 ;;
      IN_PROGRESS) return 1 ;;
      *) die "build $BUILD_ID ended $status; logs: log group $(tfo '.log_group_names.value.codebuild')" 1 ;;
    esac
  }
  wait_until "CodeBuild $BUILD_ID" $((BUILD_MIN * 60)) 20 build_done
  DIGEST="$(image_digest)" || die "build succeeded but ECR has no $REPO_NAME:$COMMIT" 1
fi
printf '%s' "$DIGEST" | grep -Eq '^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$' || die "unexpected image digest: $DIGEST" 1
SCAN="$(aws_retry ecr describe-images --repository-name "$REPO_NAME" --image-ids "imageTag=$COMMIT" \
  --query 'imageDetails[0].[imageScanStatus.status, to_string(imageScanFindingsSummary.findingSeverityCounts)]' --output text || echo "unknown")"
printf '==> IMAGE %s:%s digest=%s\n' "$REPO_URL" "$COMMIT" "$DIGEST"
info "scan (status, severity counts): $SCAN"

step "record"
mkdir -p "$RECORD_DIR"
REC="$RECORD_DIR/package-$COMMIT.json"
jq -n --arg commit "$COMMIT" --arg at "$(utc_now)" --arg account "$ACCOUNT" --arg region "$REGION" \
  --arg bucket "$BUCKET" --arg jp "$JOBS_PREFIX" --arg hs "$HADOOP_SHA" --arg ss "$SPARK_SHA" \
  --arg stepSha "$(sha256_hex "$SC_ROOT/scripts/emr/step.sh")" --arg ctx "$SOURCE_KEY" --arg cs "$CONTEXT_SHA" \
  --arg repo "$REPO_URL" --arg digest "$DIGEST" --arg build "$BUILD_ID" --arg scan "$SCAN" \
  '{schemaVersion: "package-record-v1", commit: $commit, createdAt: $at, account: $account, region: $region,
    jobs: [{name: "hadoop", key: ($jp + "/hadoop-assembly.jar"), sha256: $hs, mainClass: "scholarchain.hadoop.Pipeline"},
           {name: "spark", key: ($jp + "/sparkAnalytics-assembly.jar"), sha256: $ss, mainClass: "scholarchain.spark.PipelineJob"},
           {name: "emr-step", key: ($jp + "/step.sh"), sha256: $stepSha}],
    bucket: $bucket, dockerContext: {key: $ctx, sha256: $cs},
    image: {repository: $repo, tag: $commit, digest: $digest, buildId: (if $build == "" then null else $build end), scan: $scan}}' >"$REC"
printf '==> PACKAGE record=%s\n' "$REC"
ok "package complete"

===== UNTRACKED: scripts/plan.sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Terraform plan for the course environment (spec Phase 13 item 3). Never applies.
set -euo pipefail
# shellcheck source=lib/common.sh
. "$(dirname "$0")/lib/common.sh"

usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage: scripts/plan.sh --runtime-enabled true|false [--var-file <path>] [--profile <name>]
                       [--region us-east-2] [--out-dir <dir>] [--evidence-dir <dir>] [--dry-run]

Runs terraform init (local backend) and terraform plan -out for infra/terraform/envs/course, prints
the complete plan, and writes into <out-dir>/<UTC stamp>-<commit 12>/ (gitignored, under scratch/):
  plan.tfplan        the saved plan (deploy.sh applies only this file, by checksum)
  plan.sha256        its sha256
  plan.txt           the full human-readable plan, exactly as printed
  plan.json          terraform show -json (holds planned values; stays local, mode 600)
  plan-meta.json     commit, account, Region, runtime_enabled, var file and its sha256, counts
  summary.md         secret-free summary: counts, stateful destroys or replacements (first), IAM
                     changes, high-cost resources
and copies summary.md to <evidence-dir>/<UTC stamp>-<commit 12>.md for the phase evidence.

Flags:
  --runtime-enabled  required: false = cloud window 1, true = cloud window 2 (D-13)
  --var-file         default infra/terraform/envs/course/course.tfvars
  --profile          default $AWS_PROFILE (the human's scholarchain-sdk profile)
  --region           default us-east-2 (the only Region the configuration accepts)
  --out-dir          default scratch/plans
  --evidence-dir     default operations/plans
  --dry-run          validate flags and local files, print the steps, no AWS/Terraform/git call

Environment: AWS_PROFILE (or --profile); TF_VAR_alarm_email (required by the configuration; may be
empty for no alarm subscription; never printed by this script).
Run by: the human architect at the plan gate. Never applies; never uses -auto-approve.
Exit: 0 plan written; 2 usage; 3 dirty tree, wrong account or Region; 1 terraform failure.
EOF
}

RUNTIME=""
VAR_FILE="$SC_TF_DIR/course.tfvars"
OUT_DIR="$SC_ROOT/scratch/plans"
EVIDENCE_DIR="$SC_ROOT/operations/plans"
while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do
  case "$1" in
    --runtime-enabled) RUNTIME="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --var-file) VAR_FILE="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --profile) PROFILE="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --region) REGION="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --out-dir) OUT_DIR="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --evidence-dir) EVIDENCE_DIR="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --dry-run) DRY_RUN=1; shift ;;
    --help | -h) usage; exit 0 ;;
    *) usage >&2; die "unknown argument: $1" 2 ;;
  esac
done

case "$RUNTIME" in true | false) ;; *) die "--runtime-enabled true|false is required" 2 ;; esac
[ -f "$VAR_FILE" ] || die "var file not found: $VAR_FILE" 2
VAR_FILE="$(cd "$(dirname "$VAR_FILE")" && pwd)/$(basename "$VAR_FILE")"
[ "${TF_VAR_alarm_email+set}" = set ] || die "set TF_VAR_alarm_email (an empty value means no alarm subscription)" 2
need terraform jq git aws

dry_run_exit \
  "refuse a dirty tree; check terraform $SC_TF_VERSION" \
  "aws sts get-caller-identity; print account and Region $REGION" \
  "terraform init -input=false -lockfile=readonly (local backend)" \
  "terraform plan -input=false -var-file=$VAR_FILE -var runtime_enabled=$RUNTIME -var region=$REGION -out=<dir>/plan.tfplan (printed in full)" \
  "terraform show -json; sha256; summary into $OUT_DIR/<stamp>-<sha12>/ and $EVIDENCE_DIR/"

tf_require_version
require_clean_tree
require_profile
aws_target

COMMIT="$(git_head)"
STAMP="$(utc_stamp)"
DIR="$OUT_DIR/$STAMP-${COMMIT:0:12}"
mkdir -p "$DIR" "$EVIDENCE_DIR"
chmod 700 "$DIR"

step "init"
tf init -input=false -lockfile=readonly

step "plan"
# Full output on the terminal and in plan.txt; nothing is filtered.
tf plan -input=false -lock-timeout=60s -var-file="$VAR_FILE" -var "runtime_enabled=$RUNTIME" \
  -var "region=$REGION" -out="$DIR/plan.tfplan" 2>&1 | tee "$DIR/plan.txt"

step "show"
(umask 077 && tf show -json "$DIR/plan.tfplan" >"$DIR/plan.json")
PLAN_SHA="$(sha256_hex "$DIR/plan.tfplan")"
printf '%s  plan.tfplan\n' "$PLAN_SHA" >"$DIR/plan.sha256"

PLAN_ACCOUNT="$(jq -r '.planned_values.outputs.account_id.value // empty' "$DIR/plan.json")"
if [ -z "$PLAN_ACCOUNT" ]; then
  warn "the plan does not show account_id as a known value; the profile account is $ACCOUNT"
else
  [ "$PLAN_ACCOUNT" = "$ACCOUNT" ] || die "plan account $PLAN_ACCOUNT differs from profile account $ACCOUNT" 3
fi

step "summarize"
# Terraform's own count rule: a replacement is one add and one destroy.
COUNTS="$(jq -c '[.resource_changes[] | select(.mode == "managed") | .change.actions] |
  {add: map(select(index("create"))) | length,
   change: map(select(. == ["update"])) | length,
   destroy: map(select(index("delete"))) | length}' "$DIR/plan.json")"
STATEFUL='["aws_s3_bucket","aws_dynamodb_table","aws_neptune_cluster","aws_neptune_cluster_instance",
  "aws_s3vectors_vector_bucket","aws_s3vectors_index","aws_ecr_repository","aws_cloudwatch_log_group",
  "aws_secretsmanager_secret","aws_sqs_queue"]'
STATEFUL_LINES="$(jq -r --argjson s "$STATEFUL" '.resource_changes[] | select(.mode == "managed")
  | select(.type as $t | $s | index($t)) | select(.change.actions | index("delete"))
  | "- \(.change.actions | join("+")) \(.address)"' "$DIR/plan.json")"
IAM_LINES="$(jq -r '.resource_changes[] | select(.mode == "managed") | select(.type | startswith("aws_iam_"))
  | select(.change.actions != ["no-op"]) | "- \(.change.actions | join("+")) \(.address)"' "$DIR/plan.json")"
COST_LINES="$(jq -r '.resource_changes[] | select(.mode == "managed")
  | select(.type | IN("aws_neptune_cluster_instance","aws_ecs_service","aws_lb","aws_codebuild_project",
    "aws_dynamodb_table","aws_s3vectors_index","aws_appautoscaling_target"))
  | select(.change.actions != ["no-op"]) | "- \(.change.actions | join("+")) \(.address)"' "$DIR/plan.json")"

jq -n --arg commit "$COMMIT" --arg account "$ACCOUNT" --arg region "$REGION" --arg runtime "$RUNTIME" \
  --arg varFile "${VAR_FILE#"$SC_ROOT"/}" --arg varSha "$(sha256_hex "$VAR_FILE")" \
  --arg lockSha "$(sha256_hex "$SC_TF_DIR/.terraform.lock.hcl")" --arg planSha "$PLAN_SHA" \
  --arg createdAt "$(utc_now)" --argjson counts "$COUNTS" \
  '{schemaVersion: "plan-meta-v1", commit: $commit, account: $account, region: $region,
    runtimeEnabled: ($runtime == "true"), varFile: $varFile, varFileSha256: $varSha,
    lockFileSha256: $lockSha, planSha256: $planSha, createdAt: $createdAt, counts: $counts}' \
  >"$DIR/plan-meta.json"

{
  printf '# Terraform plan summary\n\n'
  printf -- '- Plan SHA-256: `%s`\n- Commit: `%s`\n- Account: `%s`\n- Region: `%s`\n' "$PLAN_SHA" "$COMMIT" "$ACCOUNT" "$REGION"
  printf -- '- runtime_enabled: `%s`\n- Var file: `%s`\n- Created: %s\n' "$RUNTIME" "${VAR_FILE#"$SC_ROOT"/}" "$(utc_now)"
  printf -- '- Counts: %s\n\n' "$COUNTS"
  printf '## Stateful destroy or replace (needs explicit human sign-off)\n\n%s\n\n' "${STATEFUL_LINES:-none}"
  printf '## IAM changes\n\n%s\n\n' "${IAM_LINES:-none}"
  printf '## High-cost resources\n\n%s\n' "${COST_LINES:-none}"
} >"$DIR/summary.md"
cp "$DIR/summary.md" "$EVIDENCE_DIR/$STAMP-${COMMIT:0:12}.md"

cat "$DIR/summary.md"
printf '==> PLAN file=%s sha256=%s counts=%s\n' "$DIR/plan.tfplan" "$PLAN_SHA" "$COUNTS"
[ -z "$STATEFUL_LINES" ] || warn "the plan destroys or replaces stateful resources (listed above)"
ok "plan written; nothing applied. Approve by checksum, then: scripts/deploy.sh --plan-file $DIR/plan.tfplan --approved-plan-sha256 $PLAN_SHA"

===== UNTRACKED: scripts/rollback.sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Moves the three ECS services back to the image of a known-good release record, without Terraform
# (spec Phase 14 rollback gate; docs/agents.md section 8).
set -euo pipefail
# shellcheck source=lib/common.sh
. "$(dirname "$0")/lib/common.sh"

usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage: scripts/rollback.sh --to-release <operations/releases/release-<stamp>-<sha>.json>
                           [--profile <name>] [--region us-east-2] [--reason "<text>"]
                           [--stable-timeout-minutes 20] [--record-dir <dir>] [--dry-run]
       scripts/rollback.sh --list [--record-dir <dir>]

--list prints the local release and smoke records (no AWS call), so the operator can pick the last
known-good release: one whose smoke record after it says PASS.

Steps:
  1. the record must be a runtime release of this account and Region with an image tag and digest
  2. ECR must still hold <repository>:<tag> with exactly that digest (tags are immutable)
  3. for query-api, graph-evidence-api and worker together (SERVICES.md section 5): copy the
     service's current task definition, change only the container image to <repository>:<tag>,
     register it as a new revision (one attempt each), and point the service at it
  4. wait until the services are stable (bounded), then verify every running task's digest and that
     query-api and the worker log one runtime run ID
  5. write <record-dir>/rollback-<stamp>.json (previous and new task definitions, image, reason)

No terraform apply. Terraform state still names the newer image, so the next plan shows the task
definitions changing back; to keep the rollback, set image_tag in course.tfvars to the release's tag
and go through plan.sh and deploy.sh.

Flags: --record-dir default operations/releases; --dry-run validates the record, prints the steps,
no AWS/Terraform call.
Environment: AWS_PROFILE (or --profile).
Run by: deploy-engineer for its own failing in-flight deployment, the incident-responder for a live
revision (/rollback); each AWS mutation passes the human's permission prompt.
Exit: 0 rolled back and verified; 1 verification failed; 3 precondition; 6 timeout.
EOF
}

TO=""
REASON=""
STABLE_MIN=20
RECORD_DIR="$SC_ROOT/operations/releases"
LIST=0
while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do
  case "$1" in
    --to-release) TO="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --profile) PROFILE="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --region) REGION="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --reason) REASON="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --stable-timeout-minutes) STABLE_MIN="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --record-dir) RECORD_DIR="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --list) LIST=1; shift ;;
    --dry-run) DRY_RUN=1; shift ;;
    --help | -h) usage; exit 0 ;;
    *) usage >&2; die "unknown argument: $1" 2 ;;
  esac
done
need jq

if [ "$LIST" = 1 ]; then
  step "list"
  for f in "$RECORD_DIR"/release-*.json "$RECORD_DIR"/smoke-*.json "$RECORD_DIR"/rollback-*.json; do
    [ -f "$f" ] || continue
    jq -r --arg f "${f#"$SC_ROOT"/}" '[$f, (.deployedAt // .finishedAt // .rolledBackAt),
      (.image.tag // .result // "-"), (.image.digest // .baseUrl // "-")] | join("  ")' "$f"
  done | sort -k2
  exit 0
fi

[ -f "$TO" ] || die "--to-release not found: ${TO:-<none>}" 2
printf '%s' "$STABLE_MIN" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' && [ "$STABLE_MIN" -ge 1 ] && [ "$STABLE_MIN" -le 60 ] ||
  die "--stable-timeout-minutes must be 1 to 60" 2
[ "$(jq -r .schemaVersion "$TO")" = release-record-v1 ] && [ "$(jq -r .runtimeEnabled "$TO")" = true ] ||
  die "$TO is not a runtime release record" 3
TAG="$(jq -er .image.tag "$TO")" || die "release record has no image tag" 3
DIGEST="$(jq -er .image.digest "$TO")" || die "release record has no image digest" 3
is_sha40 "$TAG" || die "release image tag is not a Git SHA: $TAG" 3
need aws terraform

dry_run_exit \
  "aws sts get-caller-identity; account must be $(jq -r .account "$TO"); terraform output (cluster, repository)" \
  "ECR describe-images $TAG must have digest $DIGEST" \
  "register one task definition revision per service with image <repository>:$TAG; update-service query-api, graph-evidence-api, worker" \
  "wait stable (${STABLE_MIN} min); verify digests and runtime run IDs; write $RECORD_DIR/rollback-<stamp>.json"

require_profile
aws_target
[ "$ACCOUNT" = "$(jq -r .account "$TO")" ] && [ "$REGION" = "$(jq -r .region "$TO")" ] ||
  die "the release record is for $(jq -r .account "$TO")/$(jq -r .region "$TO")" 3
tf_outputs
CLUSTER="$(tfo '.ecs_cluster_name.value')"
REPO="$(tfo '.ecr_repository_url.value')"
REPO_NAME="$(tfo '.ecr_repository_name.value')"

step "image"
HAVE="$(aws_retry ecr describe-images --repository-name "$REPO_NAME" --image-ids "imageTag=$TAG" \
  --query 'imageDetails[0].imageDigest' --output text)" || die "ECR has no $REPO_NAME:$TAG" 3
[ "$HAVE" = "$DIGEST" ] || die "ECR $REPO_NAME:$TAG has digest $HAVE, the record says $DIGEST" 3
ok "$REPO:$TAG $DIGEST"

step "register-and-update"
CHANGES='[]'
for s in $SC_SERVICES; do
  OLD="$(aws_retry ecs describe-services --cluster "$CLUSTER" --services "$s" --query 'services[0].taskDefinition' --output text)"
  INPUT="$(mktemp "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/sc-td.XXXXXX")"
  aws_retry ecs describe-task-definition --task-definition "$OLD" --include TAGS --output json |
    jq --arg img "$REPO:$TAG" '.tags as $t | .taskDefinition
      | {family, taskRoleArn, executionRoleArn, networkMode, containerDefinitions, volumes,
         placementConstraints, requiresCompatibilities, cpu, memory, runtimePlatform, ephemeralStorage}
      | with_entries(select(.value != null))
      | .containerDefinitions[0].image = $img
      | if ($t | length) > 0 then .tags = $t else . end' >"$INPUT"
  NEW="$(aws_once ecs register-task-definition --cli-input-json "file://$INPUT" \
    --query 'taskDefinition.taskDefinitionArn' --output text)"
  rm -f "$INPUT"
  aws_retry ecs update-service --cluster "$CLUSTER" --service "$s" --task-definition "$NEW" \
    --query 'service.serviceName' --output text
  info "$s $OLD -> $NEW"
  CHANGES="$(jq -c --arg s "$s" --arg o "$OLD" --arg n "$NEW" '. + [{service: $s, from: $o, to: $n}]' <<<"$CHANGES")"
done

ecs_wait_stable "$CLUSTER" $((STABLE_MIN * 60))
ecs_verify_image "$CLUSTER" "$REPO:$TAG" "$DIGEST"
ecs_verify_run_ids "$(jq -c '.log_group_names.value' "$TF_OUT")"

step "record"
mkdir -p "$RECORD_DIR"
REC="$RECORD_DIR/rollback-$(utc_stamp).json"
jq -n --arg at "$(utc_now)" --arg account "$ACCOUNT" --arg region "$REGION" --arg to "${TO#"$SC_ROOT"/}" \
  --arg tag "$TAG" --arg digest "$DIGEST" --arg reason "$REASON" --argjson changes "$CHANGES" \
  --argjson services "$ECS_SERVICES_JSON" --argjson runIds "$RUN_IDS" \
  '{schemaVersion: "rollback-record-v1", rolledBackAt: $at, account: $account, region: $region,
    toRelease: $to, image: {tag: $tag, digest: $digest}, reason: $reason, changes: $changes,
    services: $services, runtimeRunIds: $runIds, terraformDrift: "task definitions differ from state until image_tag is planned and applied"}' >"$REC"
printf '==> ROLLBACK record=%s image=%s\n' "$REC" "$TAG"
ok "rollback complete; next: scripts/smoke-test.sh"

===== UNTRACKED: scripts/run-bounded-pipeline.sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# One bounded Hadoop + Spark pipeline run on an ephemeral EMR cluster (spec Phase 13 item 6,
# ruling 2, D-13). The cluster is never a Terraform resource; it terminates itself after its steps.
set -euo pipefail
# shellcheck source=lib/common.sh
. "$(dirname "$0")/lib/common.sh"

usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage: scripts/run-bounded-pipeline.sh --fraction 25|50|100 --package-record <package-<sha>.json>
           [--profile <name>] [--region us-east-2] [--reducers 2] [--shuffle-partitions 16]
           [--hadoop-timeout-minutes 90] [--spark-timeout-minutes 150] [--record-dir <dir>] [--dry-run]

Inputs (DBLP snapshot 2026-10-03; OpenAlex, Crossref, ORCID snapshot 2026-11-03):
  --fraction 100  scratch/datasets/dblp/snapshot=2026-10-03/full/works.xml,
                  scratch/datasets/{openalex,crossref}/full/works.jsonl,
                  scratch/datasets/orcid/full/records.jsonl.gz
  --fraction 25|50 scratch/datasets/fraction=<p>/{dblp/works.xml, openalex/works.jsonl,
                  crossref/works.jsonl, orcid/records.jsonl.gz}, written by
                  datasets/scripts/make_fraction.sh (rule fraction-v1; exit 3 if one is missing)
  DTD             scratch/datasets/dblp/snapshot=2026-10-03/raw/dblp.dtd
  Every resolved input path is printed with its size and sha256 (also in --dry-run).

Steps:
  1. clean tree; HEAD = the package record's commit; local jars and scripts/emr/step.sh hash to it
  2. account and Region; terraform outputs (bucket, the emr object); ruling 2 bounds re-checked:
     release emr-spark-8.0.0, 1 primary, 1 to 2 core, Graviton type, on-demand
  3. write-once uploads (skipped when present with the same sha256): inputs to
     raw/<dataset>/snapshot=<d>/run=<input run>/ (input run = hash of the fraction and file hashes),
     jars and step.sh to artifacts/jobs/<commit>/
  4. one create-cluster call (no retry), auto-terminate, three steps:
     classpath-probe (CONTINUE), hadoop-pipeline J01..J10 (TERMINATE_CLUSTER),
     spark-pipeline jobs 1..11 (TERMINATE_CLUSTER); each job step has a timeout on the node
  5. wait for TERMINATED (bounded); collect step timings, instance hours, the new Hadoop and Spark
     run directories and their _manifest.json, and the step logs (classpath evidence)
  6. read-only describe-security-groups of both EMR-managed groups: any 0.0.0.0/0 or ::/0 ingress
     (which Terraform cannot see, it ignores their rule drift) is warned about and recorded
  7. write <record-dir>/<stamp>-fraction<p>/run.json plus the submitted steps, cluster description,
     security groups, manifests, and logs

Flags: --record-dir default operations/pipeline-runs; --dry-run validates flags and local inputs,
prints the steps, makes no AWS/Terraform/git call.
Environment: AWS_PROFILE (or --profile).
Run by: deploy-engineer (/run-pipeline) or the human, after cloud window 1 is applied and
scripts/package.sh recorded the jars. Rollback: nothing to roll back; outputs are new write-once
run directories, and the cluster terminates itself (a hung step is killed by its timeout).
Exit: 0 all steps completed and manifests found; 1 a step or check failed (cluster ID recorded);
3 precondition; 6 timeout (the cluster ID is printed; the human may terminate it).
EOF
}

FRACTION=""
PACKAGE_RECORD=""
REDUCERS=2
SHUFFLE=16
HADOOP_MIN=90
SPARK_MIN=150
RECORD_DIR="$SC_ROOT/operations/pipeline-runs"
ENRICH_SNAPSHOT="2026-11-03"
while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do
  case "$1" in
    --fraction) FRACTION="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --package-record) PACKAGE_RECORD="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --profile) PROFILE="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --region) REGION="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --reducers) REDUCERS="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --shuffle-partitions) SHUFFLE="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --hadoop-timeout-minutes) HADOOP_MIN="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --spark-timeout-minutes) SPARK_MIN="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --record-dir) RECORD_DIR="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --dry-run) DRY_RUN=1; shift ;;
    --help | -h) usage; exit 0 ;;
    *) usage >&2; die "unknown argument: $1" 2 ;;
  esac
done

case "$FRACTION" in 25 | 50 | 100) ;; *) die "--fraction 25|50|100 is required" 2 ;; esac
[ -f "$PACKAGE_RECORD" ] || die "--package-record not found: ${PACKAGE_RECORD:-<none>}" 2
in_range() { printf '%s' "$2" | grep -Eq '^[0-9]+$' && [ "$2" -ge "$3" ] && [ "$2" -le "$4" ] || die "$1 must be $3 to $4" 2; }
in_range --reducers "$REDUCERS" 1 16
in_range --shuffle-partitions "$SHUFFLE" 2 200
in_range --hadoop-timeout-minutes "$HADOOP_MIN" 10 240
in_range --spark-timeout-minutes "$SPARK_MIN" 10 240
need jq git aws terraform

DS="$SC_ROOT/scratch/datasets"
DTD="$DS/dblp/snapshot=$SC_SNAPSHOT/raw/dblp.dtd"
if [ "$FRACTION" = 100 ]; then
  DBLP="$DS/dblp/snapshot=$SC_SNAPSHOT/full/works.xml"
  OPENALEX="$DS/openalex/full/works.jsonl"
  CROSSREF="$DS/crossref/full/works.jsonl"
  ORCID="$DS/orcid/full/records.jsonl.gz"
else
  F="$DS/fraction=$FRACTION"
  DBLP="$F/dblp/works.xml"
  OPENALEX="$F/openalex/works.jsonl"
  CROSSREF="$F/crossref/works.jsonl"
  ORCID="$F/orcid/records.jsonl.gz"
fi
for f in "$DBLP" "$DTD" "$OPENALEX" "$CROSSREF" "$ORCID"; do
  [ -f "$f" ] || die "input missing: ${f#"$SC_ROOT"/}$([ "$FRACTION" != 100 ] && echo ' (write it with: bash datasets/scripts/make_fraction.sh)')" 3
done

# "<path> <sha256> <bytes>" per input, after a fraction line: printed here (dry-run too) and hashed
# into the input run ID in step upload-inputs.
step "inputs"
INPUT_LINES="$(
  printf 'fraction=%s\n' "$FRACTION"
  for f in "$DBLP" "$DTD" "$OPENALEX" "$CROSSREF" "$ORCID"; do
    printf '%s %s %s\n' "${f#"$SC_ROOT"/}" "$(sha256_hex "$f")" "$(wc -c <"$f" | tr -d ' ')"
  done
)"
printf '%s\n' "$INPUT_LINES" | sed 1d | while read -r p s b; do info "input $p bytes=$b sha256=$s"; done

COMMIT="$(jq -er .commit "$PACKAGE_RECORD")" || die "package record has no commit" 3
is_sha40 "$COMMIT" || die "package record commit is not a Git SHA" 3
HADOOP_JAR="$SC_ROOT/modules/hadoop/target/scala-3.3.7/hadoop-assembly-0.1.0.jar"
SPARK_JAR="$SC_ROOT/modules/spark/target/scala-3.3.7/sparkAnalytics-assembly-0.1.0.jar"
STEP_SH="$SC_ROOT/scripts/emr/step.sh"

dry_run_exit \
  "refuse a dirty tree; HEAD must be $COMMIT; jar and step.sh sha256 must equal the package record" \
  "aws sts get-caller-identity; terraform output (bucket, emr); bounds check" \
  "write-once upload of 5 inputs (fraction $FRACTION) under raw/, jars under artifacts/jobs/$COMMIT/" \
  "aws emr create-cluster (once): emr-spark-8.0.0, 1 primary + core per the emr output, auto-terminate; steps classpath-probe, hadoop-pipeline (${HADOOP_MIN} min), spark-pipeline (${SPARK_MIN} min, shuffle $SHUFFLE, reducers $REDUCERS)" \
  "poll describe-cluster until TERMINATED; collect steps, instance hours, run manifests, step logs" \
  "describe-security-groups (read-only) of both EMR groups; warn about and record any 0.0.0.0/0 or ::/0 ingress" \
  "write $RECORD_DIR/<stamp>-fraction$FRACTION/run.json"

require_clean_tree
[ "$(git_head)" = "$COMMIT" ] || die "HEAD $(git_head) is not the package record's commit $COMMIT" 3
check_sha() {
  [ "$(sha256_hex "$2")" = "$(jq -r --arg n "$1" '.jobs[] | select(.name == $n) | .sha256' "$PACKAGE_RECORD")" ] ||
    die "$2 does not hash to the package record's $1 sha256 (rerun scripts/package.sh)" 3
}
check_sha hadoop "$HADOOP_JAR"
check_sha spark "$SPARK_JAR"
check_sha emr-step "$STEP_SH"

require_profile
aws_target
tf_outputs
BUCKET="$(tfo '.data_bucket_name.value')"
EMR="$(jq -c '.emr.value' "$TF_OUT")"
emr() { jq -er "$1" <<<"$EMR"; }
RELEASE="$(emr .release_label)"
ITYPE="$(emr .instance_type)"
PRIMARY="$(emr .primary_instance_count)"
CORE="$(emr .core_instance_count)"
LOG_URI="$(emr .log_uri)"

step "bounds"
[ "$RELEASE" = emr-spark-8.0.0 ] || die "release $RELEASE is not emr-spark-8.0.0 (D-01)" 3
[ "$PRIMARY" = 1 ] && [ "$CORE" -ge 1 ] && [ "$CORE" -le 2 ] || die "ruling 2: 1 primary and 1 to 2 core; got $PRIMARY and $CORE" 3
printf '%s' "$ITYPE" | grep -Eq '^[a-z][0-9]+g[a-z]*\.[a-z0-9]+$' || die "$ITYPE is not a Graviton type" 3
ok "bounds: $RELEASE, $PRIMARY primary + $CORE core $ITYPE, on-demand"

STAMP="$(utc_stamp)"
REC="$RECORD_DIR/$STAMP-fraction$FRACTION"
mkdir -p "$REC/manifests" "$REC/logs"

step "upload-inputs"
LINES="$REC/input-hashes.txt"
printf '%s\n' "$INPUT_LINES" >"$LINES"
INPUT_RUN="run-$(sha256_hex "$LINES" | cut -c1-32)"
info "input run $INPUT_RUN"
k_dblp="raw/dblp/snapshot=$SC_SNAPSHOT/run=$INPUT_RUN/works.xml"
k_dtd="raw/dblp/snapshot=$SC_SNAPSHOT/run=$INPUT_RUN/dblp.dtd"
k_oa="raw/openalex/snapshot=$ENRICH_SNAPSHOT/run=$INPUT_RUN/works.jsonl"
k_cr="raw/crossref/snapshot=$ENRICH_SNAPSHOT/run=$INPUT_RUN/works.jsonl"
k_or="raw/orcid/snapshot=$ENRICH_SNAPSHOT/run=$INPUT_RUN/records.jsonl.gz"
s3_put_if_absent "$DBLP" "$BUCKET" "$k_dblp"
s3_put_if_absent "$DTD" "$BUCKET" "$k_dtd"
s3_put_if_absent "$OPENALEX" "$BUCKET" "$k_oa"
s3_put_if_absent "$CROSSREF" "$BUCKET" "$k_cr"
s3_put_if_absent "$ORCID" "$BUCKET" "$k_or"

step "upload-jobs"
JP="artifacts/jobs/$COMMIT"
s3_put_if_absent "$HADOOP_JAR" "$BUCKET" "$JP/hadoop-assembly.jar"
s3_put_if_absent "$SPARK_JAR" "$BUCKET" "$JP/sparkAnalytics-assembly.jar"
s3_put_if_absent "$STEP_SH" "$BUCKET" "$JP/step.sh"

list_runs() {
  { aws_retry s3 ls "s3://$BUCKET/$1" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '$1 == "PRE" {sub("/$", "", $2); print $2}'
}
HADOOP_ROOT="curated/hadoop/snapshot=$SC_SNAPSHOT/"
SPARK_ROOT="curated/spark/snapshot=$SC_SNAPSHOT/"
list_runs "$HADOOP_ROOT" >"$REC/hadoop-runs-before.txt"
list_runs "$SPARK_ROOT" >"$REC/spark-runs-before.txt"
EARLIER="$(paste -sd, "$REC/hadoop-runs-before.txt")"

step "submit"
S3A="s3a://$BUCKET"
BOOT="set -euo pipefail; aws --cli-connect-timeout 10 --cli-read-timeout 120 s3 cp --only-show-errors s3://$BUCKET/$JP/step.sh /tmp/scholarchain-step.sh; exec bash /tmp/scholarchain-step.sh \"\$@\""
jq -n --arg boot "$BOOT" --arg hjar "s3://$BUCKET/$JP/hadoop-assembly.jar" --arg sjar "s3://$BUCKET/$JP/sparkAnalytics-assembly.jar" \
  --arg ht "$((HADOOP_MIN * 60))" --arg st "$((SPARK_MIN * 60))" --arg bucket "$BUCKET" --arg snap "$SC_SNAPSHOT" \
  --arg earlier "$EARLIER" --arg commit "$COMMIT" --arg reducers "$REDUCERS" --arg shuffle "$SHUFFLE" \
  --arg dblp "$S3A/$k_dblp" --arg dtd "$S3A/$k_dtd" --arg oa "$S3A/$k_oa" --arg cr "$S3A/$k_cr" --arg or "$S3A/$k_or" \
  --arg es "$ENRICH_SNAPSHOT" --arg hout "$S3A/curated/hadoop" --arg sout "$S3A/curated/spark" \
  '[{Type: "CUSTOM_JAR", Name: "classpath-probe", ActionOnFailure: "CONTINUE", Jar: "command-runner.jar",
     Args: ["bash", "-c", $boot, "step", "classpath"]},
    {Type: "CUSTOM_JAR", Name: "hadoop-pipeline", ActionOnFailure: "TERMINATE_CLUSTER", Jar: "command-runner.jar",
     Args: ["bash", "-c", $boot, "step", "hadoop", $ht, $hjar, "--out", $hout, "--code-version", $commit,
            "--dblp", $dblp, "--dblp-dtd", $dtd, "--dblp-snapshot", $snap, "--openalex", $oa, "--openalex-snapshot", $es,
            "--crossref", $cr, "--crossref-snapshot", $es, "--orcid", $or, "--orcid-snapshot", $es, "--reducers", $reducers]},
    {Type: "CUSTOM_JAR", Name: "spark-pipeline", ActionOnFailure: "TERMINATE_CLUSTER", Jar: "command-runner.jar",
     Args: ["bash", "-c", $boot, "step", "spark", $st, $sjar, $bucket, $snap, $earlier, "--out", $sout,
            "--checkpoint-dir", "hdfs:///scholarchain/spark/checkpoints", "--code-version", $commit,
            "--shuffle-partitions", $shuffle]}]' >"$REC/steps.json"
jq -n --arg t "$ITYPE" --argjson c "$CORE" \
  '[{InstanceGroupType: "MASTER", InstanceCount: 1, InstanceType: $t},
    {InstanceGroupType: "CORE", InstanceCount: $c, InstanceType: $t}]' >"$REC/instance-groups.json"

CLUSTER_ID="$(aws_once emr create-cluster --name "scholarchain-course-pipeline-f$FRACTION-${COMMIT:0:12}" \
  --release-label "$RELEASE" --applications Name=Hadoop Name=Spark \
  --service-role "$(emr .service_role)" \
  --ec2-attributes "InstanceProfile=$(emr .instance_profile),SubnetId=$(emr .subnet_id),EmrManagedMasterSecurityGroup=$(emr .primary_security_group_id),EmrManagedSlaveSecurityGroup=$(emr .core_security_group_id)" \
  --instance-groups "file://$REC/instance-groups.json" --steps "file://$REC/steps.json" \
  --log-uri "$LOG_URI" --auto-terminate \
  --tags for-use-with-amazon-emr-managed-policies=true Project=ScholarChain Environment=course Course=CS441 \
  Owner=scholarchain-human-architect Expiration=2026-12-15 ManagedBy=scripts/run-bounded-pipeline.sh \
  --query ClusterId --output text)"
printf '==> CLUSTER id=%s\n' "$CLUSTER_ID"
printf '%s\n' "$CLUSTER_ID" >"$REC/cluster-id.txt"

terminated() {
  local s
  s="$(aws_retry emr describe-cluster --cluster-id "$CLUSTER_ID" --query 'Cluster.Status.State' --output text)"
  info "$(utc_now) cluster $s"
  case "$s" in TERMINATED | TERMINATED_WITH_ERRORS) return 0 ;; *) return 1 ;; esac
}
step "wait"
wait_until "cluster $CLUSTER_ID to terminate (terminate it by hand only if it is stuck)" \
  $(((30 + HADOOP_MIN + SPARK_MIN + 15) * 60)) 60 terminated

step "collect"
aws_retry emr describe-cluster --cluster-id "$CLUSTER_ID" >"$REC/cluster.json"
aws_retry emr list-steps --cluster-id "$CLUSTER_ID" >"$REC/steps-result.json"
list_runs "$HADOOP_ROOT" >"$REC/hadoop-runs-after.txt"
list_runs "$SPARK_ROOT" >"$REC/spark-runs-after.txt"
NEW_HADOOP="$(comm -13 <(sort "$REC/hadoop-runs-before.txt") <(sort "$REC/hadoop-runs-after.txt"))"
NEW_SPARK="$(comm -13 <(sort "$REC/spark-runs-before.txt") <(sort "$REC/spark-runs-after.txt"))"
for r in $NEW_HADOOP; do aws_retry s3 cp --only-show-errors "s3://$BUCKET/$HADOOP_ROOT$r/_manifest.json" "$REC/manifests/hadoop-$r.json" || warn "no manifest in $HADOOP_ROOT$r"; done
for r in $NEW_SPARK; do aws_retry s3 cp --only-show-errors "s3://$BUCKET/$SPARK_ROOT$r/_manifest.json" "$REC/manifests/spark-$r.json" || warn "no manifest in $SPARK_ROOT$r"; done

# EMR pushes step logs to S3 a few minutes after termination; wait at most 10 minutes.
LOG_PREFIX="${LOG_URI%/}/$CLUSTER_ID/steps/"
logs_ready() { [ "$(aws_retry s3 ls --recursive "$LOG_PREFIX" 2>/dev/null | grep -c 'stdout.gz' || true)" -ge 3 ]; }
if (wait_until "step logs under $LOG_PREFIX" 600 30 logs_ready); then :; else warn "step logs incomplete; fetched what exists"; fi
aws_retry s3 cp --only-show-errors --recursive "$LOG_PREFIX" "$REC/logs/" || warn "no step logs fetched"

# Terraform ignores ingress drift on the EMR-managed groups (code review 1 F7c), so check read-only
# what EMR added. Warns and records; never changes a rule (the fix is a human action).
step "emr-security-groups"
OPEN_INGRESS_JQ='[.SecurityGroups[] | .GroupId as $g | .IpPermissions[]? | . as $p
  | ([.IpRanges[]? | select(.CidrIp == "0.0.0.0/0") | .CidrIp] + [.Ipv6Ranges[]? | select(.CidrIpv6 == "::/0") | .CidrIpv6])[]
  | {groupId: $g, protocol: $p.IpProtocol, fromPort: ($p.FromPort // "all"), toPort: ($p.ToPort // "all"), source: .}]'
SG_CHECKED=false
SG_OPEN='[]'
if aws_retry ec2 describe-security-groups --group-ids "$(emr .primary_security_group_id)" "$(emr .core_security_group_id)" \
  >"$REC/security-groups.json"; then
  SG_CHECKED=true
  SG_OPEN="$(jq -c "$OPEN_INGRESS_JQ" "$REC/security-groups.json")"
  if [ "$SG_OPEN" = '[]' ]; then
    ok "no 0.0.0.0/0 or ::/0 ingress on the EMR groups"
  else
    jq -r '.[] | "\(.groupId) \(.protocol) \(.fromPort)-\(.toPort) from \(.source)"' <<<"$SG_OPEN" |
      while IFS= read -r l; do warn "EMR GROUP OPEN TO THE INTERNET: $l (recorded; the human reviews and removes it)"; done
  fi
else
  warn "describe-security-groups failed: the EMR ingress check is UNVERIFIED for this run"
fi

STATE="$(jq -r .Cluster.Status.State "$REC/cluster.json")"
REASON="$(jq -r .Cluster.Status.StateChangeReason.Code "$REC/cluster.json")"
HOURS="$(jq -r .Cluster.NormalizedInstanceHours "$REC/cluster.json")"
jq -n --arg commit "$COMMIT" --arg fraction "$FRACTION" --arg snap "$SC_SNAPSHOT" --arg es "$ENRICH_SNAPSHOT" \
  --arg inputRun "$INPUT_RUN" --arg cluster "$CLUSTER_ID" --arg state "$STATE" --arg reason "$REASON" \
  --arg hours "$HOURS" --arg account "$ACCOUNT" --arg region "$REGION" --arg release "$RELEASE" --arg itype "$ITYPE" \
  --argjson core "$CORE" --arg reducers "$REDUCERS" --arg shuffle "$SHUFFLE" --arg bucket "$BUCKET" \
  --arg hadoop "$NEW_HADOOP" --arg spark "$NEW_SPARK" --rawfile inputs "$LINES" --slurpfile steps "$REC/steps-result.json" \
  --argjson sgChecked "$SG_CHECKED" --argjson sgOpen "$SG_OPEN" \
  '{schemaVersion: "pipeline-run-record-v1", commit: $commit, codeVersion: $commit, fraction: ($fraction | tonumber),
    snapshots: {dblp: $snap, openalex: $es, crossref: $es, orcid: $es}, inputRun: $inputRun,
    inputs: ($inputs | split("\n") | map(select(test(" ")) | split(" ") | {path: .[0], sha256: .[1], bytes: (.[2] | tonumber)})),
    account: $account, region: $region,
    configuration: {releaseLabel: $release, instanceType: $itype, primary: 1, core: $core, market: "ON_DEMAND",
                    reducers: ($reducers | tonumber), shufflePartitions: ($shuffle | tonumber)},
    clusterId: $cluster, finalState: $state, stateChangeReason: $reason, normalizedInstanceHours: ($hours | tonumber? // null),
    steps: [$steps[0].Steps[] | {name: .Name, state: .Status.State, start: .Status.Timeline.StartDateTime,
             end: .Status.Timeline.EndDateTime}],
    outputs: {hadoopRuns: ($hadoop | split("\n") | map(select(. != "")) | map("s3://" + $bucket + "/curated/hadoop/snapshot=" + $snap + "/" + .)),
              sparkRuns: ($spark | split("\n") | map(select(. != "")) | map("s3://" + $bucket + "/curated/spark/snapshot=" + $snap + "/" + .))},
    emrSecurityGroups: {checked: $sgChecked, openIngress: $sgOpen, file: "security-groups.json"},
    logs: "logs/", manifests: "manifests/"}' >"$REC/run.json"
printf '==> PIPELINE record=%s cluster=%s state=%s reason=%s instance-hours=%s\n' "$REC/run.json" "$CLUSTER_ID" "$STATE" "$REASON" "$HOURS"

[ "$REASON" = ALL_STEPS_COMPLETED ] || die "cluster ended $STATE/$REASON; see $REC/steps-result.json and $REC/logs/" 1
[ -n "$NEW_HADOOP" ] && [ -n "$NEW_SPARK" ] || die "no new Hadoop or Spark run directory found" 1
ok "pipeline run recorded"

===== UNTRACKED: scripts/run-experiment.sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Runs one committed experiment manifest (spec Phase 13 item 7). No manifest exists yet; the
# experiment-engineer writes them in Phase 15 in the format below.
set -euo pipefail
# shellcheck source=lib/common.sh
. "$(dirname "$0")/lib/common.sh"

usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage: scripts/run-experiment.sh --manifest experiments/<path>.json [--profile <name>]
                                 [--region us-east-2] [--results-root experiments/results] [--dry-run]

Manifest (JSON, committed under experiments/ before the run):
  {
    "schemaVersion": "experiment-manifest-v1",
    "experimentId": "<a-z 0-9 ->",                 used in the results path
    "description": "<text>",
    "command": ["<tracked executable under experiments/ or load-tests/>", "<arg>", ...],
    "limits": {"maxMinutes": <1..240>, ...},       maxMinutes is enforced; every limit is recorded
    "metrics": [{"namespace": "AWS/ECS", "metricName": "CPUUtilization",
                 "dimensions": {"ClusterName": "...", "ServiceName": "..."}}, ...]
  }

Gates: the manifest is tracked by Git and unchanged from HEAD; the tree is clean (outside
operations/ and experiments/results/); the command's first element is a tracked executable under
experiments/ or load-tests/ (no other command can run); account and Region are printed first.
The command runs from the repository root with SCHOLARCHAIN_EXPERIMENT_ID,
SCHOLARCHAIN_EXPERIMENT_DIR (its raw results directory), AWS_PROFILE and AWS_REGION, and is stopped
(TERM, then KILL after 30 s) at limits.maxMinutes.

Writes <results-root>/<experimentId>/<UTC stamp>/: raw/ (the command's own outputs), stdout.log,
stderr.log, and run-record.json (commit, manifest path and sha256, limits, start and end times,
exit code, account, Region, metric locations). The directory is made read-only afterwards (raw
results are immutable).

Environment: AWS_PROFILE (or --profile).
Run by: experiment-engineer (/experiment) with an approved, committed manifest.
Rollback: none; an experiment changes no deployed resource beyond what its manifest's command does
within the approved bounds.
Exit: 0 command exited 0; 1 command failed; 3 precondition (uncommitted manifest, dirty tree);
6 maxMinutes reached.
EOF
}

MANIFEST=""
RESULTS_ROOT="$SC_ROOT/experiments/results"
while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do
  case "$1" in
    --manifest) MANIFEST="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --profile) PROFILE="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --region) REGION="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --results-root) RESULTS_ROOT="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --dry-run) DRY_RUN=1; shift ;;
    --help | -h) usage; exit 0 ;;
    *) usage >&2; die "unknown argument: $1" 2 ;;
  esac
done
need jq
[ -f "$MANIFEST" ] || die "--manifest not found: ${MANIFEST:-<none>}" 2
REL="$(cd "$(dirname "$MANIFEST")" && pwd)/$(basename "$MANIFEST")"
REL="${REL#"$SC_ROOT"/}"
case "$REL" in experiments/results/*) die "a manifest cannot live under experiments/results/" 2 ;; experiments/*.json) ;; *) die "the manifest must be a .json file under experiments/" 2 ;; esac

jq -e '.schemaVersion == "experiment-manifest-v1"
  and (.experimentId | type == "string" and test("^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{2,63}$"))
  and (.command | type == "array" and length >= 1 and all(type == "string"))
  and (.limits.maxMinutes | type == "number" and . >= 1 and . <= 240)
  and ((.metrics // []) | type == "array")' "$SC_ROOT/$REL" >/dev/null ||
  die "manifest fails the experiment-manifest-v1 checks (see --help)" 2
EXP_ID="$(jq -r .experimentId "$SC_ROOT/$REL")"
MAX_MIN="$(jq -r '.limits.maxMinutes | floor' "$SC_ROOT/$REL")"
CMD0="$(jq -r '.command[0]' "$SC_ROOT/$REL")"
case "$CMD0" in experiments/* | load-tests/*) ;; *) die "command[0] must be under experiments/ or load-tests/" 2 ;; esac
case "$CMD0" in *..*) die "command[0] must not contain .." 2 ;; esac
[ -x "$SC_ROOT/$CMD0" ] || die "command[0] is not an executable file: $CMD0" 2

dry_run_exit \
  "manifest $REL tracked and unchanged from HEAD; clean tree; $CMD0 tracked" \
  "aws sts get-caller-identity; print account and Region" \
  "run $(jq -r '.command | join(" ")' "$SC_ROOT/$REL") for at most $MAX_MIN min" \
  "write $RESULTS_ROOT/$EXP_ID/<stamp>/run-record.json; make the directory read-only"

need git aws
git -C "$SC_ROOT" ls-files --error-unmatch -- "$REL" >/dev/null 2>&1 || die "manifest $REL is not committed" 3
git -C "$SC_ROOT" diff --quiet HEAD -- "$REL" || die "manifest $REL differs from HEAD (commit it first)" 3
git -C "$SC_ROOT" ls-files --error-unmatch -- "$CMD0" >/dev/null 2>&1 || die "$CMD0 is not committed" 3
require_clean_tree
COMMIT="$(git_head)"
require_profile
aws_target

DIR="$RESULTS_ROOT/$EXP_ID/$(utc_stamp)"
mkdir -p "$DIR/raw"
CMD=()
while IFS= read -r a; do CMD+=("$a"); done < <(jq -r '.command[]' "$SC_ROOT/$REL")

step "run $EXP_ID"
START="$(utc_now)"
(cd "$SC_ROOT" && SCHOLARCHAIN_EXPERIMENT_ID="$EXP_ID" SCHOLARCHAIN_EXPERIMENT_DIR="$DIR/raw" \
  exec "${CMD[@]}" >"$DIR/stdout.log" 2>"$DIR/stderr.log") &
PID=$!
DEADLINE=$((SECONDS + MAX_MIN * 60))
TIMED_OUT=0
while kill -0 "$PID" 2>/dev/null; do
  if [ "$SECONDS" -ge "$DEADLINE" ]; then
    TIMED_OUT=1
    warn "maxMinutes $MAX_MIN reached: stopping the command"
    kill -TERM "$PID" 2>/dev/null || true
    sleep 30
    kill -KILL "$PID" 2>/dev/null || true
    break
  fi
  sleep 5
done
RC=0
wait "$PID" || RC=$?
END="$(utc_now)"
tail -20 "$DIR/stdout.log" || true

jq -n --arg commit "$COMMIT" --arg manifest "$REL" --arg msha "$(sha256_hex "$SC_ROOT/$REL")" \
  --arg start "$START" --arg end "$END" --argjson rc "$RC" --argjson timedOut "$TIMED_OUT" \
  --arg account "$ACCOUNT" --arg region "$REGION" --slurpfile m "$SC_ROOT/$REL" \
  '{schemaVersion: "experiment-run-record-v1", experimentId: $m[0].experimentId, commit: $commit,
    manifest: $manifest, manifestSha256: $msha, limits: $m[0].limits, command: $m[0].command,
    startedAt: $start, endedAt: $end, exitCode: $rc, timedOut: ($timedOut == 1),
    account: $account, region: $region, rawResults: "raw/", stdout: "stdout.log", stderr: "stderr.log",
    metricLocations: [($m[0].metrics // [])[] | . + {region: $region, source: "CloudWatch"}]}' >"$DIR/run-record.json"
chmod -R a-w "$DIR"
printf '==> EXPERIMENT id=%s record=%s exit=%s\n' "$EXP_ID" "$DIR/run-record.json" "$RC"
[ "$TIMED_OUT" = 0 ] || die "experiment stopped at maxMinutes $MAX_MIN" 6
[ "$RC" = 0 ] || die "experiment command exited $RC" 1
ok "experiment complete"

===== UNTRACKED: scripts/smoke-test.sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Smoke test of the deployed runtime through the ALB (spec Phase 13 item 5).
set -euo pipefail
# shellcheck source=lib/common.sh
. "$(dirname "$0")/lib/common.sh"

DEFAULT_QUESTION="Have Jack J. Dongarra and Yves Robert coauthored a paper?"

usage() {
  cat <<EOF
Usage: scripts/smoke-test.sh [--profile <name>] [--region us-east-2] [--base-url http://<alb dns>]
                             [--question "<text>"] [--record-dir <dir>] [--dry-run]

Checks, in order (each prints ==> OK or ==> FAIL; the first failure stops the run):
  healthz      GET /healthz = 200 {"status":"ok"} (no key; retried, idempotent)
  readyz       GET /readyz = 200 {"status":"ready"} (no key; retried up to 3 minutes)
  query        POST /v1/query {"question": <question>, "execution": "sync"} with a fresh
               Idempotency-Key = 200, a qry- ID, and at least one claim with an evidence ID
               (not retried; max 60 s against the 40 s synchronous deadline)
  evidence     GET /v1/evidence/<first evidence ID of the first such claim> = 200, same evidenceId
  idempotent   the same POST with the same Idempotency-Key = 200, same queryId and answerHash
  auth-error   POST /v1/query without X-Api-Key = 401 {"error":"UNAUTHORIZED"}

The API key is read from Secrets Manager (the ARN from terraform output api_key_secret_arn) into a
shell variable and passed to curl on standard input (--config -), never in argv, a file, or output.

Flags:
  --base-url     default http://<terraform output alb_dns_name>
  --question     default "$DEFAULT_QUESTION" (benchmark B07: one bounded researcher query)
  --record-dir   default operations/releases (writes smoke-<stamp>.json and smoke-<stamp>/ bodies)
  --dry-run      validate flags, print the checks, no AWS/Terraform/HTTP call

Environment: AWS_PROFILE (or --profile).
Run by: deploy-engineer after deploy.sh (and the incident-responder after a rollback).
Rollback: none; on failure the deploy-engineer follows the rollback gate (scripts/rollback.sh).
Exit: 0 all checks passed; 1 a check failed; 3 precondition.
EOF
}

BASE=""
QUESTION="$DEFAULT_QUESTION"
RECORD_DIR="$SC_ROOT/operations/releases"
while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do
  case "$1" in
    --profile) PROFILE="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --region) REGION="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --base-url) BASE="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --question) QUESTION="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --record-dir) RECORD_DIR="$(flag_val "$1" "${2-}")"; shift 2 ;;
    --dry-run) DRY_RUN=1; shift ;;
    --help | -h) usage; exit 0 ;;
    *) usage >&2; die "unknown argument: $1" 2 ;;
  esac
done
[ -z "$BASE" ] || printf '%s' "$BASE" | grep -Eq '^https?://[A-Za-z0-9.-]+(:[0-9]+)?$' || die "--base-url must be http(s)://host[:port]" 2
need curl jq aws terraform

dry_run_exit \
  "aws sts get-caller-identity; terraform output alb_dns_name and api_key_secret_arn" \
  "GetSecretValue into a variable (never printed)" \
  "healthz, readyz, query (question: $QUESTION), evidence, idempotent repeat, 401 without a key" \
  "write $RECORD_DIR/smoke-<stamp>.json"

require_profile
aws_target
tf_outputs
[ "$(tfo '.runtime_enabled.value')" = true ] || die "runtime_enabled is false: nothing to smoke-test" 3
[ -n "$BASE" ] || BASE="http://$(tfo '.alb_dns_name.value')"
SECRET="$(tfo '.api_key_secret_arn.value')"

STAMP="$(utc_stamp)"
OUT="$RECORD_DIR/smoke-$STAMP"
mkdir -p "$OUT"
RESULTS='[]'

step "api-key"
API_KEY="$(aws_retry secretsmanager get-secret-value --secret-id "$SECRET" --query SecretString --output text)"
[ -n "$API_KEY" ] && [ "$API_KEY" != None ] || die "the API key secret has no value" 3
KEY_ESC="${API_KEY//\\/\\\\}"
KEY_ESC="${KEY_ESC//\"/\\\"}"
unset API_KEY
ok "API key read (not printed)"

# req auth method path out [body-file] [idempotency-key] -> prints the HTTP status
req() {
  local auth="$1" method="$2" path="$3" out="$4" body="${5-}" idem="${6-}" cfg=""
  local args=(-sS -o "$out" -w '%{http_code}' --connect-timeout 10 --max-time 60 -X "$method"
    -H "X-Request-Id: smoke-$STAMP")
  [ -z "$body" ] || args+=(-H 'Content-Type: application/json' --data-binary "@$body")
  [ -z "$idem" ] || args+=(-H "Idempotency-Key: $idem")
  [ "$auth" = no ] || cfg="header = \"X-Api-Key: $KEY_ESC\""
  # curl prints 000 itself when no response arrives.
  printf '%s\n' "$cfg" | curl --config - "${args[@]}" "$BASE$path" || true
}

record() {
  RESULTS="$(jq -c --arg n "$1" --arg s "$2" --arg c "$3" --arg d "$4" '. + [{check: $n, result: $s, http: $c, detail: $d}]' <<<"$RESULTS")"
}

write_record() {
  jq -n --arg base "$BASE" --arg at "$(utc_now)" --arg account "$ACCOUNT" --arg region "$REGION" \
    --arg question "$QUESTION" --arg result "$1" --argjson checks "$RESULTS" \
    '{schemaVersion: "smoke-record-v1", baseUrl: $base, finishedAt: $at, account: $account, region: $region,
      question: $question, result: $result, checks: $checks, bodies: "smoke-'"$STAMP"'/"}' >"$RECORD_DIR/smoke-$STAMP.json"
}

fail() {
  record "$1" FAIL "$2" "$3"
  write_record FAIL
  die "$1: $3 (http $2; bodies in $OUT)" 1
}

pass() {
  record "$1" PASS "$2" "$3"
  ok "$1 $3"
}

step "healthz"
for i in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  CODE="$(req no GET /healthz "$OUT/healthz.json")"
  [ "$CODE" = 200 ] && break
  sleep 5
done
[ "$CODE" = 200 ] && [ "$(jq -r .status "$OUT/healthz.json" 2>/dev/null)" = ok ] || fail healthz "$CODE" "expected 200 ok"
pass healthz "$CODE" "ok"

step "readyz"
for i in $(seq 1 12); do
  CODE="$(req no GET /readyz "$OUT/readyz.json")"
  [ "$CODE" = 200 ] && break
  sleep 15
done
[ "$CODE" = 200 ] && [ "$(jq -r .status "$OUT/readyz.json" 2>/dev/null)" = ready ] || fail readyz "$CODE" "expected 200 ready"
pass readyz "$CODE" "ready"

step "query"
IDEM="smoke-$STAMP"
jq -n --arg q "$QUESTION" '{question: $q, execution: "sync"}' >"$OUT/query-request.json"
CODE="$(req yes POST /v1/query "$OUT/query.json" "$OUT/query-request.json" "$IDEM")"
[ "$CODE" = 200 ] || fail query "$CODE" "expected 200"
QID="$(jq -r .queryId "$OUT/query.json")"
HASH="$(jq -r .answer.answerHash "$OUT/query.json")"
EVID="$(jq -r '[.claims[]? | .evidenceIds[]?][0] // empty' "$OUT/query.json")"
printf '%s' "$QID" | grep -Eq '^qry-[0-9a-f]{32}$' || fail query "$CODE" "no qry- ID"
[ -n "$EVID" ] || fail query "$CODE" "no claim carries an evidence ID (abstained: $(jq -r .answer.abstained "$OUT/query.json"))"
pass query "$CODE" "$QID claims=$(jq '.claims | length' "$OUT/query.json") evidence=$EVID"

step "evidence"
CODE="$(req yes GET "/v1/evidence/$EVID" "$OUT/evidence.json")"
[ "$CODE" = 200 ] && [ "$(jq -r .evidenceId "$OUT/evidence.json")" = "$EVID" ] || fail evidence "$CODE" "expected 200 for $EVID"
pass evidence "$CODE" "$EVID $(jq -r .evidenceType "$OUT/evidence.json")"

step "idempotent"
CODE="$(req yes POST /v1/query "$OUT/query-repeat.json" "$OUT/query-request.json" "$IDEM")"
[ "$CODE" = 200 ] || fail idempotent "$CODE" "expected 200 replay"
[ "$(jq -r .queryId "$OUT/query-repeat.json")" = "$QID" ] && [ "$(jq -r .answer.answerHash "$OUT/query-repeat.json")" = "$HASH" ] ||
  fail idempotent "$CODE" "replay differs from the first answer"
pass idempotent "$CODE" "same queryId and answerHash"

step "auth-error"
CODE="$(req no POST /v1/query "$OUT/unauthorized.json" "$OUT/query-request.json")"
[ "$CODE" = 401 ] && [ "$(jq -r .error "$OUT/unauthorized.json")" = UNAUTHORIZED ] || fail auth-error "$CODE" "expected 401 UNAUTHORIZED"
pass auth-error "$CODE" "UNAUTHORIZED"

write_record PASS
printf '==> SMOKE result=PASS record=%s query=%s\n' "$RECORD_DIR/smoke-$STAMP.json" "$QID"
